script:--[[
    LvyuFlo v3.7 — Fixed: Config infinite loop, toggle clicks, notification spam
]]

local TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
local Plrs = game:GetService("Players")
local Http = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Ltg = game:GetService("Lighting")
local LP = Plrs.LocalPlayer
local Cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
local Mse = LP:GetMouse()

local T = {
    BG = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 22), Side = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 30), Elem = Color3.fromRGB(23, 23, 38),
    ElemH = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 50), Inner = Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 52), Acc = Color3.fromRGB(128, 90, 200),
    AccH = Color3.fromRGB(150, 112, 225), AccD = Color3.fromRGB(98, 60, 170), Txt = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 244),
    Sub = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 165), Dim = Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 110), Div = Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 60),
    Good = Color3.fromRGB(67, 181, 129), Warn = Color3.fromRGB(250, 168, 26), Bad = Color3.fromRGB(237, 66, 69),
    Hp = Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 120), HpM = Color3.fromRGB(240, 180, 60), HpL = Color3.fromRGB(240, 80, 80),
    Arm = Color3.fromRGB(80, 160, 255), W = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Bk = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
}

local Ex = {N = "Unknown", D = false, FS = false, Cl = false}
pcall(function() if getexecutorname then Ex.N = getexecutorname() elseif identifyexecutor then Ex.N = identifyexecutor() end end)
pcall(function() if Drawing and Drawing.new then Ex.D = true end end)
pcall(function() if writefile and readfile and isfile then Ex.FS = true end end)
pcall(function() if setclipboard then Ex.Cl = true end end)

local function RN() return string.char(math.random(97, 122))..Http:GenerateGUID(false):sub(1, 6) end

local function I(c, p, k)
    local o = Instance.new(c)
    for a, b in pairs(p or {}) do if a ~= "Parent" then pcall(function() o[a] = b end) end end
    for _, ch in ipairs(k or {}) do ch.Parent = o end
    if p and p.Parent then o.Parent = p.Parent end
    return o
end

local function Tw(o, g, d, s, dr)
    if not o then return nil end
    local ok = pcall(function() return o.Parent end)
    if not ok then return nil end
    local t = TS:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(d or .22, s or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, dr or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), g)
    t:Play()
    return t
end

local function RC(p, r) return I("UICorner", {CornerRadius = r or UDim.new(0, 8), Parent = p}) end
local function ST2(p, c, th, tr) return I("UIStroke", {Color = c or T.Div, Thickness = th or 1, Transparency = tr or .5, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent = p}) end
local function PD(p, t, b, l, r) return I("UIPadding", {PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, t or 0), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, b or 0), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, l or 0), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, r or 0), Parent = p}) end
local function LL(p, pd) return I("UIListLayout", {SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, pd or 6), Parent = p}) end

local function Rip(p)
    if not p or not p.Parent then return end
    local c = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.W, BackgroundTransparency = .78, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5, .5), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5, .5), Parent = p})
    RC(c, UDim.new(1, 0))
    Tw(c, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(p.AbsoluteSize.X, p.AbsoluteSize.Y) * 2.5, math.max(p.AbsoluteSize.X, p.AbsoluteSize.Y) * 2.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1}, .5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    task.delay(.55, function() pcall(game.Destroy, c) end)
end

local function JO(f, ts, d)
    d = d or .5
    f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    local t1 = Tw(f, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(ts.X.Offset * 1.06, ts.Y.Offset * 1.06)}, d * .5, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
    if t1 then t1.Completed:Wait() end
    local t2 = Tw(f, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(ts.X.Offset * .97, ts.Y.Offset * .97)}, d * .2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    if t2 then t2.Completed:Wait() end
    Tw(f, {Size = ts}, d * .3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
end

local function JC(f, d)
    d = d or .35
    local cs = f.Size
    local t1 = Tw(f, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cs.X.Offset * 1.04, cs.Y.Offset * 1.04)}, d * .2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    if t1 then t1.Completed:Wait() end
    local t2 = Tw(f, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)}, d * .8, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
    if t2 then t2.Completed:Wait() end
end

local function FV(v, inc)
    if inc >= 1 then return tostring(math.floor(v + .5))
    else local dp = math.max(0, math.ceil(-math.log10(inc))); return string.format("%."..dp.."f", v) end
end

local SG = I("ScreenGui", {Name = RN(), ResetOnSpawn = false, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling, DisplayOrder = math.random(900000, 999999), IgnoreGuiInset = true})
pcall(function() SG.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
if not SG.Parent then SG.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end

local S = {
    Vis = true, Alive = true, Mini = false, Tabs = {}, Fl = {}, Cn = {}, AT = nil, TK = Enum.KeyCode.RightControl,
    SLoc = nil, ESP = {}, ArmD = nil, ArmF = false, ASave = true, CCfg = "Default",
    AllDrawing = {},
    Destroying = false,
    Loading = false, -- FIX: Prevent config loading recursion
    ActiveSlider = nil,
    NotifyCount = 0, -- FIX: Track notification count
    MaxNotify = 10   -- FIX: Limit notifications
}

local OV = {}
pcall(function() OV.A = Ltg.Ambient; OV.B = Ltg.Brightness; OV.FE = Ltg.FogEnd; OV.FS2 = Ltg.FogStart; OV.FOV = Cam.FieldOfView; OV.G = workspace.Gravity end)
local Inp = {LC = false, RC = false, MC = false, AT2 = false}

table.insert(S.Cn, UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i2)
    if i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then Inp.LC = true
    elseif i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then Inp.RC = true
    elseif i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 then Inp.MC = true end
end))

table.insert(S.Cn, UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i2)
    if i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then Inp.LC = false; S.ActiveSlider = nil
    elseif i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then Inp.RC = false
    elseif i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 then Inp.MC = false end
end))

-- Centralized slider input handler
table.insert(S.Cn, UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
    if S.ActiveSlider and (inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        local slider = S.ActiveSlider
        if slider and slider.Update then
            slider.Update(inp)
        end
    end
end))

-- Safe Drawing wrapper
local function NewDraw(typ)
    if not Ex.D then return nil end
    local ok, obj = pcall(function() return Drawing.new(typ) end)
    if ok and obj then
        table.insert(S.AllDrawing, obj)
        return obj
    end
    return nil
end

-- Config
local CF = "LvyuFlo"
local function EF() if not Ex.FS then return end; pcall(function() if not isfolder(CF) then makefolder(CF) end end) end
local function GC() local l = {}; if not Ex.FS then return l end; pcall(function() if isfolder(CF) then for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(CF)) do local n = f:match("([^/\\]+)%.json$"); if n then table.insert(l, n) end end end end); return l end

-- FIX: Save config - don't save dropdown selections that would cause loops
local function SC(n)
    if not Ex.FS then return end
    EF()
    pcall(function()
        local d = {}
        for f, o in pairs(S.Fl) do
            if o.Get and o.Type ~= "Dropdown" then -- FIX: Skip dropdowns to prevent load loops
                local v = o:Get()
                if type(v) == "boolean" or type(v) == "number" or type(v) == "string" then
                    d[f] = v
                elseif typeof(v) == "Color3" then
                    d[f] = {R = v.R, G = v.G, B = v.B, _ = "C"}
                elseif typeof(v) == "EnumItem" then
                    d[f] = {N = v.Name, _ = "K"}
                end
            end
        end
        writefile(CF.."/"..n..".json", Http:JSONEncode(d))
    end)
end

-- FIX: Load config with recursion prevention
local function LC(n)
    if not Ex.FS then return end
    if S.Loading then return end -- Prevent infinite loop
    S.Loading = true
    pcall(function()
        local p = CF.."/"..n..".json"
        if isfile(p) then
            local d = Http:JSONDecode(readfile(p))
            for f, v in pairs(d) do
                local o = S.Fl[f]
                if o and o.Set then
                    if type(v) == "table" then
                        if v._ == "C" then
                            o:Set(Color3.new(v.R, v.G, v.B), true) -- true = skip callback
                        elseif v._ == "K" then
                            o:Set(Enum.KeyCode[v.N], true)
                        end
                    else
                        o:Set(v, true)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    S.Loading = false
end

-- Armor
local function DetArm() for _, p in ipairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do local c = p.Character; if c then local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if h then for _, n in ipairs({"Armor", "Shield", "Vest", "Protection"}) do local v = h:GetAttribute(n); if v and type(v) == "number" then S.ArmD = {t = "a", n = n}; return end end end end end end
local function GArm(p) if not S.ArmD and not S.ArmF then return nil end; local c = p.Character; if not c then return nil end; local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if not h then return nil end; if S.ArmD and S.ArmD.t == "a" then return h:GetAttribute(S.ArmD.n) end; for _, n in ipairs({"Armor", "Shield", "Vest"}) do local v = h:GetAttribute(n); if v and type(v) == "number" then return v end end; return nil end
DetArm()

-- Notifications - FIX: With count limit
local NH = I("Frame", {Name = "NotificationHolder", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0, 320, 1, -40), Position = UDim2.new(1, -330, 0, 20), Parent = SG})
I("UIListLayout", {SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, Parent = NH})

local function Notify(o)
    if S.Destroying or S.Loading then return end -- FIX: Don't notify during loading
    if S.NotifyCount >= S.MaxNotify then return end -- FIX: Limit notifications
    
    o = o or {}
    local ti = o.Title or ""
    local co = o.Content or ""
    local du = o.Duration or 4
    local tp = o.Type or "Info"
    local cl = ({Info = T.Acc, Success = T.Good, Warning = T.Warn, Error = T.Bad})[tp] or T.Acc
    
    S.NotifyCount = S.NotifyCount + 1
    
    local nf = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.BG, BackgroundTransparency = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 70), ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = NH})
    RC(nf, UDim.new(0, 10))
    ST2(nf, cl, 1, .4)
    I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = cl, Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -8), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4), Parent = nf})
    I("TextLabel", {Text = ti, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 10), Parent = nf})
    I("TextLabel", {Text = co, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextWrapped = true, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 28), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 32), Parent = nf})
    local pg = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = cl, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -3), Parent = nf})
    
    nf.Position = UDim2.new(1, 60, 0, 0)
    Tw(nf, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)}, .4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
    Tw(pg, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 3)}, du, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
    
    task.delay(du, function()
        S.NotifyCount = math.max(0, S.NotifyCount - 1)
        if not nf or not nf.Parent then return end
        local tw = Tw(nf, {Position = UDim2.new(1, 60, 0, 0)}, .35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        if tw then tw.Completed:Wait() end
        pcall(game.Destroy, nf)
    end)
end

-- Confirm Dialog with proper background
local function Cfm(ti, msg, onY, onN)
    if S.Destroying then return end
    
    local overlay = I("Frame", {
        Name = "ConfirmOverlay",
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        ZIndex = 9000,
        Parent = SG
    })
    Tw(overlay, {BackgroundTransparency = .4}, .25)

    local dlg = I("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5, .5),
        BackgroundColor3 = T.Side,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5, .5),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        ZIndex = 9001,
        ClipsDescendants = false,
        Parent = overlay
    })
    RC(dlg, UDim.new(0, 12))
    ST2(dlg, T.Acc, 2, .2)

    local shadow = I("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5, .5),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = .6,
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5, .5),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 20, 1, 20),
        ZIndex = 9000,
        Parent = dlg
    })
    RC(shadow, UDim.new(0, 16))

    local ct = I("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 9002, Parent = dlg})
    PD(ct, 20, 20, 20, 20)
    LL(ct, 12)
    I("TextLabel", {Text = ti, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = T.Txt, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), ZIndex = 9003, Parent = ct})
    I("TextLabel", {Text = msg, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextWrapped = true, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 9003, Parent = ct})

    local bf = I("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), ZIndex = 9003, Parent = ct})
    local cb = I("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, BackgroundTransparency = 0, Size = UDim2.new(.48, 0, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, Text = "Cancel", TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextSize = 13, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 9004, Parent = bf})
    RC(cb, UDim.new(0, 6))
    local yb = I("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc, BackgroundTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.new(.52, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(.48, 0, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, Text = "Confirm", TextColor3 = T.W, TextSize = 13, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 9004, Parent = bf})
    RC(yb, UDim.new(0, 6))

    cb.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(cb, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Div}, .12) end)
    cb.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(cb, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner}, .12) end)
    yb.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(yb, {BackgroundColor3 = T.AccH}, .12) end)
    yb.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(yb, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc}, .12) end)

    task.spawn(function() JO(dlg, UDim2.fromOffset(380, 0), .4) end)

    local closed = false
    local function cls()
        if closed then return end
        closed = true
        task.spawn(function()
            Tw(overlay, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, .25)
            JC(dlg, .3)
            task.wait(.35)
            pcall(game.Destroy, overlay)
        end)
    end
    
    cb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Rip(cb); cls(); if onN then task.spawn(onN) end end)
    yb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Rip(yb); cls(); if onY then task.spawn(onY) end end)
    
    -- Click outside to close
    overlay.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            local mPos = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
            local dPos = dlg.AbsolutePosition
            local dSize = dlg.AbsoluteSize
            if mPos.X < dPos.X or mPos.X > dPos.X + dSize.X or mPos.Y < dPos.Y or mPos.Y > dPos.Y + dSize.Y then
                cls()
                if onN then task.spawn(onN) end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function MkB(txt, off, hc)
    local b = I("TextButton", {Text = txt, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Sub, BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutoButtonColor = false, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28), Position = UDim2.new(1, off, 0, 11), ZIndex = 4, Parent = TB})
    RC(b, UDim.new(0, 6))
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(b, {BackgroundTransparency = 0, TextColor3 = hc}, .15) end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(b, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = T.Sub}, .15) end)
    return b
end

    end
end)
table.insert(S.Cn, UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
    if dOn and (inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        local d = inp.Position - dSt
        dTgt = UDim2.new(dSp.X.Scale, dSp.X.Offset + d.X, dSp.Y.Scale, dSp.Y.Offset + d.Y)
    end
end))
table.insert(S.Cn, RS.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not S.Alive or S.Destroying then return end
    local cx, cy = Main.Position.X.Offset, Main.Position.Y.Offset
    Main.Position = UDim2.new(dTgt.X.Scale, cx + (dTgt.X.Offset - cx) * .12, dTgt.Y.Scale, cy + (dTgt.Y.Offset - cy) * .12)
end))

mBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    Rip(mBtn)
    S.Mini = not S.Mini
    if S.Mini then
        Tw(Main, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SIZE.X.Offset, 50)}, .35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        mBtn.Text = "+"
    else
        Main.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
        task.spawn(function() JO(Main, SIZE, .45) end)
        mBtn.Text = "—"
    end
end)
hBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Rip(hBtn); S.Vis = false; task.spawn(function() JC(Main, .35) end) end)

local function TogUI()
    S.Vis = not S.Vis
    task.spawn(function()
        if S.Vis then
            SG.Enabled = true
            Main.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
            JO(Main, S.Mini and UDim2.fromOffset(SIZE.X.Offset, 50) or SIZE, .45)
        else
            JC(Main, .35)
        end
    end)
end

-- Destroy function
local function DestroyAll()
    if S.Destroying then return end
    S.Destroying = true
    S.Alive = false
    
    if S.ASave then pcall(SC, S.CCfg) end
    
    task.wait()
    
    local batchSize = 20
    local count = 0
    for _, c in pairs(S.Cn) do
        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
        count = count + 1
        if count % batchSize == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    S.Cn = {}
    task.wait()
    
    count = 0
    for _, obj in ipairs(S.AllDrawing) do
        pcall(function() obj.Visible = false end)
        pcall(function() obj:Remove() end)
        count = count + 1
        if count % batchSize == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    S.AllDrawing = {}
    task.wait()
    
    count = 0
    for _, e in pairs(S.ESP) do
        pcall(function() if e.Folder then e.Folder:Destroy() end end)
        count = count + 1
        if count % batchSize == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    S.ESP = {}
    task.wait()
    
    pcall(function()
        Ltg.Ambient = OV.A
        Ltg.Brightness = OV.B
        Ltg.FogEnd = OV.FE
        Ltg.FogStart = OV.FS2 or 0
        Ltg.GlobalShadows = true
        Cam.FieldOfView = OV.FOV
        workspace.Gravity = OV.G or 196
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
            LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").WalkSpeed = 16
            LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").JumpPower = 50
        end
    end)
    
    task.wait()
    pcall(game.Destroy, SG)
end

table.insert(S.Cn, UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    if inp.KeyCode == S.TK then TogUI() end
    if inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Insert then DestroyAll() end
end))

srB:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
    local q = srB.Text:lower()
    for _, tab in ipairs(S.Tabs) do
        if tab._cf then
            for _, ch in ipairs(tab._cf:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("Frame") then
                    local tl = ch:FindFirstChild("Title")
                    ch.Visible = not tl or q == "" or tl.Text:lower():find(q, 1, true) ~= nil
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- ═══════ TAB SYSTEM ═══════
local function CT(name, icon)
    local Tab = {_e = {}, _o = 0}
    local tB = I("TextButton", {Text = "", BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc, BackgroundTransparency = 1, AutoButtonColor = false, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), LayoutOrder = #S.Tabs + 1, ZIndex = 4, Parent = tScr})
    RC(tB, UDim.new(0, 6))
    local ind = I("Frame", {Name = "Ind", BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, .5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, .5), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5, Parent = tB})
    RC(ind, UDim.new(0, 2))
    local iL = I("TextLabel", {Text = icon or "📁", Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 15, TextColor3 = T.Dim, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 34), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), ZIndex = 5, Parent = tB})
    local nL = I("TextLabel", {Text = name, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 0), ZIndex = 5, Parent = tB})
    local cf = I("ScrollingFrame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.Acc, ScrollBarImageTransparency = .3, CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 3, Parent = Content})
    LL(cf, 6)
    PD(cf, 10, 10, 10, 10)
    Tab._cf = cf
    Tab._btn = tB
    
    local function Act()
        for _, ot in ipairs(S.Tabs) do
            if ot ~= Tab then
                ot._cf.Visible = false
                Tw(ot._btn, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, .2)
                local oi = ot._btn:FindFirstChild("Ind")
                if oi then Tw(oi, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 0)}, .2) end
                for _, ch in ipairs(ot._btn:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then
                        Tw(ch, {TextColor3 = ch.TextSize >= 15 and T.Dim or T.Sub}, .2)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        cf.Visible = true
        cf.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
        Tw(tB, {BackgroundTransparency = .88}, .2)
        Tw(ind, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 18)}, .25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
        Tw(iL, {TextColor3 = T.Acc}, .2)
        Tw(nL, {TextColor3 = T.Txt}, .2)
        S.AT = Tab
    end
    
    tB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Act(); Rip(tB) end)
    tB.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if S.AT ~= Tab then Tw(tB, {BackgroundTransparency = .92}, .12) end end)
    tB.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if S.AT ~= Tab then Tw(tB, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, .12) end end)
    if #S.Tabs == 0 then task.defer(Act) end
    table.insert(S.Tabs, Tab)
    
    local function GP(c) return (c and c.Section and c.Section._fr) or cf end
    local function NO() Tab._o = Tab._o + 1; return Tab._o end

    function Tab:CreateSection(c)
        c = c or {}
        local s = {}
        local sf = I("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = cf})
        I("TextLabel", {Text = "◆ "..(c.Name or "SECTION"):upper(), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.Acc, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0), Parent = sf})
        I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.Div, BackgroundTransparency = .5, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 19), BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = sf})
        local sc = I("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Parent = sf})
        LL(sc, 6)
        s._fr = sc
        return s
    end
    
    function Tab:CreateDivider()
        I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.Div, BackgroundTransparency = .5, Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 1), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = cf})
    end
    
    function Tab:CreateLabel(c)
        c = c or {}
        local lf = I("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP(c)})
        local lb = I("TextLabel", {Name = "Title", Text = c.Text or "", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = c.Color or T.Sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0), Parent = lf})
        return {Frame = lf, Set = function(_, t, cl2) lb.Text = t; if cl2 then lb.TextColor3 = cl2 end end}
    end
    
    function Tab:CreateColorInfo(t, cl)
        local inf = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP({})})
        RC(inf, UDim.new(0, 8))
        ST2(inf, T.Div, 1, .6)
        I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = cl or T.Acc, Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -8), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4), Parent = inf})
        local lbl = I("TextLabel", {Text = t, Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 0), Parent = inf})
        return {Frame = inf, Set = function(_, t2) lbl.Text = t2 end}
    end
    
    function Tab:CreateButton(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cbFunc = c.Callback or function() end
        local ds = c.Description
        local h = ds and 52 or 38
        local bf = I("Frame", {Name = c.Name or "B", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h), LayoutOrder = NO(), ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = GP(c)})
        RC(bf, UDim.new(0, 8))
        ST2(bf, T.Div, 1, .6)
        I("TextLabel", {Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "Button", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, ds and 6 or 10), Parent = bf})
        if ds then I("TextLabel", {Text = ds, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 26), Parent = bf}) end
        I("TextLabel", {Text = "→", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 18, TextColor3 = T.Acc, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, h), Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0), Parent = bf})
        local btn = I("TextButton", {Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 2, Parent = bf})
        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(bf, {BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH}, .12) end)
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(bf, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem}, .12) end)
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Rip(bf); task.spawn(cbFunc) end)
        return {Frame = bf, Set = function(_, n) local t = bf:FindFirstChild("Title"); if t then t.Text = n end end}
    end
    
    -- FIX: Toggle with skipCallback parameter
    function Tab:CreateToggle(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cbFunc = c.Callback or function() end
        local fl = c.Flag or c.Name or "T"
        local on = c.Default or false
        local ds = c.Description
        local tip = c.Tooltip
        local h = ds and 52 or 40
        
        local tf = I("Frame", {Name = c.Name or "T", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP(c)})
        RC(tf, UDim.new(0, 8))
        ST2(tf, T.Div, 1, .6)
        I("TextLabel", {Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "Toggle", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, ds and 6 or 11), Parent = tf})
        if ds then I("TextLabel", {Text = ds, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 26), Parent = tf}) end
        
        if tip then
            local tb = I("TextButton", {BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), Position = UDim2.new(1, -72, .5, -9), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "?", TextColor3 = T.Dim, TextSize = 10, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 5, Parent = tf})
            RC(tb, UDim.new(1, 0))
            local hv = false
            tb.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                hv = true
                Tw(tb, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc, TextColor3 = T.W}, .15)
                task.spawn(function()
                    local st = tick()
                    while hv and S.Alive do
                        if tick() - st >= .4 then
                            TTL.Text = tip
                            TT.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(tb.AbsolutePosition.X + 15, tb.AbsolutePosition.Y + 15)
                            TT.Visible = true
                            break
                        end
                        task.wait(.05)
                    end
                end)
            end)
            tb.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                hv = false
                TT.Visible = false
                Tw(tb, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, TextColor3 = T.Dim}, .15)
            end)
        end
        
        local sw = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, .5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, .5), ZIndex = 2, Parent = tf})
        RC(sw, UDim.new(1, 0))
        local kn = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.Sub, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, .5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, .5), ZIndex = 3, Parent = sw})
        RC(kn, UDim.new(1, 0))
        
        local function U(v, skipCb)
            on = v
            if on then
                Tw(kn, {Position = UDim2.new(1, -19, .5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = T.W}, .2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                Tw(sw, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc}, .15)
            else
                Tw(kn, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, .5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = T.Sub}, .2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                Tw(sw, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner}, .15)
            end
            if not skipCb then task.spawn(function() pcall(cbFunc, on) end) end
        end
        
        -- FIX: Button for clicking
        local btn = I("TextButton", {Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 4, Parent = tf})
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() U(not on) end)
        tf.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(tf, {BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH}, .12) end)
        tf.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(tf, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem}, .12) end)
        
        if c.Default then U(true, true) end
        
        local obj = {
            Frame = tf,
            Type = "Toggle",
            Flag = fl,
            Set = function(_, v, skipCb) U(v, skipCb) end,
            Get = function() return on end
        }
        S.Fl[fl] = obj
        return obj
    end
    
    -- FIX: Slider with skipCallback and centralized input
    function Tab:CreateSlider(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cbFunc = c.Callback or function() end
        local fl = c.Flag or c.Name or "S"
        local mn, mx = c.Min or 0, c.Max or 100
        local inc = c.Increment or 1
        local suf = c.Suffix or ""
        local ds = c.Description
        local h = ds and 64 or 54
        
        local val = math.clamp(c.Default or mn, mn, mx)
        
        local sf = I("Frame", {Name = c.Name or "S", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP(c)})
        RC(sf, UDim.new(0, 8))
        ST2(sf, T.Div, 1, .6)
        I("TextLabel", {Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "Slider", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(.55, 0, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, ds and 6 or 8), Parent = sf})
        if ds then I("TextLabel", {Text = ds, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(.55, 0, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 24), Parent = sf}) end
        local vL = I("TextLabel", {Text = FV(val, inc)..suf, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.Acc, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(.4, -14, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(.55, 0, 0, ds and 6 or 8), Parent = sf})
        
        local ty = ds and 44 or 34
        local trk = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 8), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, ty), ZIndex = 2, Parent = sf})
        RC(trk, UDim.new(1, 0))
        
        local pct = (val - mn) / (mx - mn)
        local fill = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc, Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0), ZIndex = 3, Parent = trk})
        RC(fill, UDim.new(1, 0))
        I("UIGradient", {Color = ColorSequence.new(T.AccD, T.Acc), Parent = fill})
        local knb = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.W, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), Position = UDim2.new(pct, -8, .5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, .5), ZIndex = 4, Parent = trk})
        RC(knb, UDim.new(1, 0))
        
        local sliderData = {}
        
        sliderData.Update = function(inp)
            if not trk or not trk.Parent then return end
            local trkPos = trk.AbsolutePosition
            local trkSize = trk.AbsoluteSize
            if trkSize.X <= 0 then return end
            local rx = math.clamp((inp.Position.X - trkPos.X) / trkSize.X, 0, 1)
            val = math.floor((mn + (mx - mn) * rx) / inc + .5) * inc
            val = math.clamp(val, mn, mx)
            local p = (val - mn) / (mx - mn)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(p, 0, 1, 0)
            knb.Position = UDim2.new(p, -8, .5, 0)
            vL.Text = FV(val, inc)..suf
            task.spawn(function() pcall(cbFunc, val) end)
        end
        
        local ca = I("TextButton", {Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-8, -8), ZIndex = 5, Parent = trk})
        ca.InputBegan:Connect(function(i2)
            if i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                S.ActiveSlider = sliderData
                sliderData.Update(i2)
            end
        end)
        
        sf.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(sf, {BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH}, .12) end)
        sf.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(sf, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem}, .12) end)
        
        local obj = {
            Frame = sf,
            Type = "Slider",
            Flag = fl,
            Set = function(_, v, skipCb)
                val = math.clamp(v, mn, mx)
                local p = (val - mn) / (mx - mn)
                fill.Size = UDim2.new(p, 0, 1, 0)
                knb.Position = UDim2.new(p, -8, .5, 0)
                vL.Text = FV(val, inc)..suf
                if not skipCb then task.spawn(function() pcall(cbFunc, val) end) end
            end,
            Get = function() return val end
        }
        S.Fl[fl] = obj
        return obj
    end
    
    -- FIX: Dropdown with skipCallback
    function Tab:CreateDropdown(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cbFunc = c.Callback or function() end
        local fl = c.Flag or c.Name or "D"
        local opts = c.Options or {}
        local sel = c.Default or (opts[1] or "")
        local op2 = false
        
        local df = I("Frame", {Name = c.Name or "D", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), LayoutOrder = NO(), ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 3, Parent = GP(c)})
        RC(df, UDim.new(0, 8))
        ST2(df, T.Div, 1, .6)
        I("TextLabel", {Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "DD", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(.45, 0, 0, 40), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0), ZIndex = 4, Parent = df})
        local sL = I("TextLabel", {Text = tostring(sel), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(.45, -40, 0, 40), Position = UDim2.new(.45, 0, 0, 0), ZIndex = 4, Parent = df})
        local ar = I("TextLabel", {Text = "▼", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.Sub, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 40), Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 0), ZIndex = 4, Parent = df})
        local ow = I("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 46), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 5, Parent = df})
        local oL = LL(ow, 3)
        
        local function MO(n)
            local iS = sel == n
            local ob = I("TextButton", {Name = n, Text = "", BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, AutoButtonColor = false, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), ZIndex = 6, Parent = ow})
            RC(ob, UDim.new(0, 6))
            local ck = I("Frame", {Name = "Ck", BackgroundColor3 = iS and T.Acc or T.Inner, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, .5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, .5), ZIndex = 7, Parent = ob})
            RC(ck, UDim.new(1, 0))
            ST2(ck, T.Div, 1, .5)
            local cm = I("TextLabel", {Text = "✓", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.W, TextTransparency = iS and 0 or 1, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 8, Parent = ck})
            I("TextLabel", {Text = n, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 0), ZIndex = 7, Parent = ob})
            ob.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(ob, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Div}, .1) end)
            ob.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(ob, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner}, .1) end)
            ob.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                sel = n
                sL.Text = n
                for _, ch in ipairs(ow:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local k2 = ch:FindFirstChild("Ck")
                        if k2 then
                            local m2 = k2:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                            k2.BackgroundColor3 = ch.Name == n and T.Acc or T.Inner
                            if m2 then m2.TextTransparency = ch.Name == n and 0 or 1 end
                        end
                    end
                end
                task.spawn(function() pcall(cbFunc, sel) end)
            end)
        end
        
        for _, o in ipairs(opts) do MO(o) end
        
        local tBtn = I("TextButton", {Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), ZIndex = 5, Parent = df})
        tBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            op2 = not op2
            if op2 then
                df.ClipsDescendants = false
                Tw(df, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48 + oL.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10)}, .28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                Tw(ar, {Rotation = 180}, .2)
            else
                Tw(df, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)}, .22)
                Tw(ar, {Rotation = 0}, .2)
                task.delay(.25, function() if df then df.ClipsDescendants = true end end)
            end
        end)
        tBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(df, {BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH}, .12) end)
        tBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(df, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem}, .12) end)
        
        local obj = {
            Frame = df,
            Type = "Dropdown",
            Flag = fl,
            Set = function(_, v, skipCb)
                sel = v
                sL.Text = v
                for _, ch in ipairs(ow:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local k2 = ch:FindFirstChild("Ck")
                        if k2 then
                            local m2 = k2:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                            k2.BackgroundColor3 = ch.Name == v and T.Acc or T.Inner
                            if m2 then m2.TextTransparency = ch.Name == v and 0 or 1 end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if not skipCb then task.spawn(function() pcall(cbFunc, sel) end) end
            end,
            Get = function() return sel end,
            Refresh = function(_, nO)
                for _, ch in ipairs(ow:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end end
                opts = nO
                for _, o in ipairs(opts) do MO(o) end
            end
        }
        S.Fl[fl] = obj
        return obj
    end
    
    -- FIX: Input with skipCallback
    function Tab:CreateInput(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cbFunc = c.Callback or function() end
        local fl = c.Flag or c.Name or "I"
        
        local inf = I("Frame", {Name = c.Name or "I", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP(c)})
        RC(inf, UDim.new(0, 8))
        ST2(inf, T.Div, 1, .6)
        I("TextLabel", {Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "Input", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(.4, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0), Parent = inf})
        local bw = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.new(.52, -12, 0, 26), Position = UDim2.new(.45, 0, .5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, .5), ZIndex = 2, Parent = inf})
        RC(bw, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local bS = ST2(bw, T.Div, 1, .6)
        local box = I("TextBox", {PlaceholderText = c.Placeholder or "Type...", PlaceholderColor3 = T.Dim, Text = "", Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ClearTextOnFocus = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 0), ZIndex = 3, Parent = bw})
        box.Focused:Connect(function() Tw(bS, {Color = T.Acc, Transparency = .2}, .15) end)
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function() Tw(bS, {Color = T.Div, Transparency = .6}, .15); task.spawn(function() pcall(cbFunc, box.Text) end) end)
        inf.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(inf, {BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH}, .12) end)
        inf.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(inf, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem}, .12) end)
        
        local obj = {
            Frame = inf,
            Type = "Input",
            Flag = fl,
            Set = function(_, v, skipCb) box.Text = tostring(v); if not skipCb then task.spawn(function() pcall(cbFunc, v) end) end end,
            Get = function() return box.Text end
        }
        S.Fl[fl] = obj
        return obj
    end
    
    -- FIX: Keybind with skipCallback
    function Tab:CreateKeybind(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cbFunc = c.Callback or function() end
        local fl = c.Flag or c.Name or "K"
        local key = c.Default or Enum.KeyCode.E
        local lst = false
        local nc = c.NoCallback
        
        local kf = I("Frame", {Name = c.Name or "K", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP(c)})
        RC(kf, UDim.new(0, 8))
        ST2(kf, T.Div, 1, .6)
        I("TextLabel", {Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "Keybind", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(.6, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0), Parent = kf})
        local kB = I("TextButton", {Text = key == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "None" or key.Name, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.Acc, BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, AutoButtonColor = false, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 24), Position = UDim2.new(1, -82, .5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, .5), ZIndex = 2, Parent = kf})
        RC(kB, UDim.new(0, 6))
        ST2(kB, T.Div, 1, .5)
        kB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            lst = true
            kB.Text = "..."
            Tw(kB, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc, TextColor3 = T.W}, .12)
        end)
        table.insert(S.Cn, UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, gpe)
            if lst then
                if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                    if inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                        key = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
                        kB.Text = "None"
                    else
                        key = inp.KeyCode
                        kB.Text = key.Name
                    end
                    lst = false
                    Tw(kB, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, TextColor3 = T.Acc}, .12)
                    if c.OnChanged then task.spawn(function() pcall(c.OnChanged, key) end) end
                end
            elseif not nc and not gpe and inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                if inp.KeyCode == key and key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
                    task.spawn(cbFunc)
                end
            end
        end))
        kf.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(kf, {BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH}, .12) end)
        kf.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(kf, {BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem}, .12) end)
        
        local obj = {
            Frame = kf,
            Type = "Keybind",
            Flag = fl,
            Set = function(_, k, skipCb) key = k; kB.Text = k == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "None" or k.Name end,
            Get = function() return key end
        }
        S.Fl[fl] = obj
        return obj
    end
    
    return Tab
end

-- ═══════════ ESP ═══════════
local function CrESP(plr)
    if plr == LP then return end
    local e = {P = plr}
    local folder = I("Folder", {Name = RN(), Parent = SG})
    local bb = I("BillboardGui", {AlwaysOnTop = true, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 50), StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0), Parent = folder})
    local ct = I("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = bb})
    e.NL = I("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = plr.Name, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextSize = 13, TextStrokeColor3 = T.Bk, TextStrokeTransparency = .3, Parent = ct})
    e.DL = I("TextLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 15), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = "", TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextSize = 11, TextStrokeColor3 = T.Bk, TextStrokeTransparency = .3, Parent = ct})
    e.BB = bb
    e.Folder = folder
    if Ex.D then
        e.Box = {}
        for j = 1, 8 do e.Box[j] = NewDraw("Line"); if e.Box[j] then e.Box[j].Visible = false; e.Box[j].Thickness = 1 end end
        e.HB = NewDraw("Line"); if e.HB then e.HB.Visible = false; e.HB.Thickness = 3 end
        e.HBg = NewDraw("Line"); if e.HBg then e.HBg.Visible = false; e.HBg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40); e.HBg.Thickness = 3 end
        e.AB = NewDraw("Line"); if e.AB then e.AB.Visible = false; e.AB.Color = T.Arm; e.AB.Thickness = 2 end
        e.ABg = NewDraw("Line"); if e.ABg then e.ABg.Visible = false; e.ABg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40); e.ABg.Thickness = 2 end
    end
    S.ESP[plr] = e
end

local function RmESP(plr)
    local e = S.ESP[plr]
    if not e then return end
    if e.Box then for j = 1, 8 do if e.Box[j] then pcall(function() e.Box[j].Visible = false end) end end end
    if e.HB then pcall(function() e.HB.Visible = false end) end
    if e.HBg then pcall(function() e.HBg.Visible = false end) end
    if e.AB then pcall(function() e.AB.Visible = false end) end
    if e.ABg then pcall(function() e.ABg.Visible = false end) end
    pcall(function() e.Folder:Destroy() end)
    S.ESP[plr] = nil
end

for _, p in ipairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do CrESP(p) end
Plrs.PlayerAdded:Connect(CrESP)
Plrs.PlayerRemoving:Connect(RmESP)

-- Crosshair
local function BldX()
    local old = SG:FindFirstChild("XH")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    if not (S.Fl.XOn and S.Fl.XOn:Get()) then return end
    local st = S.Fl.XSt and S.Fl.XSt:Get() or "Cross"
    local sz = S.Fl.XSz and S.Fl.XSz:Get() or 10
    local ch = I("Frame", {Name = "XH", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5, .5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5, .5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sz * 2, sz * 2), ZIndex = 9999, Parent = SG})
    if st == "Cross" or st == "Cross+Dot" then
        I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.W, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5, .5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5, .5), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), ZIndex = 10000, Parent = ch})
        I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.W, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5, .5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5, .5), Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0), ZIndex = 10000, Parent = ch})
    end
    if st == "Dot" or st == "Cross+Dot" then
        local d = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.W, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5, .5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5, .5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4), ZIndex = 10001, Parent = ch})
        RC(d, UDim.new(1, 0))
    end
    if st == "Circle" then
        local c2 = I("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5, .5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5, .5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sz * 2, sz * 2), ZIndex = 10000, Parent = ch})
        RC(c2, UDim.new(1, 0))
        ST2(c2, T.W, 1.5, 0)
    end
    if st == "Diamond" then
        local d = I("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = T.W, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5, .5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5, .5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sz * .7, sz * .7), Rotation = 45, ZIndex = 10000, Parent = ch})
        RC(d, UDim.new(0, 1))
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    local lOn, lSt, lSz = false, "", 0
    while S.Alive and not S.Destroying do
        local cOn = S.Fl.XOn and S.Fl.XOn:Get() or false
        local cSt = S.Fl.XSt and S.Fl.XSt:Get() or "Cross"
        local cSz = S.Fl.XSz and S.Fl.XSz:Get() or 10
        if cOn ~= lOn or cSt ~= lSt or cSz ~= lSz then
            lOn, lSt, lSz = cOn, cSt, cSz
            BldX()
        end
        task.wait(.3)
    end
end)

-- Aimbot helpers
local function IsAimOn()
    if not (S.Fl.AO and S.Fl.AO:Get()) then return false end
    local act = S.Fl.AAct and S.Fl.AAct:Get() or "Hold"
    if act == "Always" then return true
    elseif act == "Toggle" then return Inp.AT2
    else
        local k = S.Fl.AK and S.Fl.AK:Get() or Enum.KeyCode.Q
        local mb = S.Fl.AMB and S.Fl.AMB:Get() or "Right Click"
        local kd = false
        pcall(function() kd = UIS:IsKeyDown(k) end)
        if kd then return true end
        if mb == "Left Click" and Inp.LC then return true end
        if mb == "Right Click" and Inp.RC then return true end
        if mb == "Middle Click" and Inp.MC then return true end
    end
    return false
end

table.insert(S.Cn, UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    if (S.Fl.AAct and S.Fl.AAct:Get()) == "Toggle" then
        local k = S.Fl.AK and S.Fl.AK:Get() or Enum.KeyCode.Q
        if inp.KeyCode == k then
            Inp.AT2 = not Inp.AT2
            Notify({Title = "Aimbot "..(Inp.AT2 and "ON" or "OFF"), Type = Inp.AT2 and "Success" or "Info", Duration = 1.5})
        end
    end
end))

local function ClosestBone(char)
    local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
    local ctr = Vector2.new(Cam.ViewportSize.X / 2, Cam.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
    for _, n in ipairs({"Head", "UpperTorso", "HumanoidRootPart", "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm", "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperLeg"}) do
        local b = char:FindFirstChild(n)
        if b and b:IsA("BasePart") then
            local sp, on = Cam:WorldToScreenPoint(b.Position)
            if on then
                local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - ctr).Magnitude
                if d < bestD then bestD = d; best = b end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

local function GetTgt()
    local fov = S.Fl.AFOV and S.Fl.AFOV:Get() or 80
    local tc = S.Fl.ATC and S.Fl.ATC:Get()
    local tp = S.Fl.APt and S.Fl.APt:Get() or "Head"
    local best, bestD = nil, fov
    local ctr = Vector2.new(Cam.ViewportSize.X / 2, Cam.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
    for _, plr in ipairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr == LP then continue end
        local char = plr.Character
        if not char then continue end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then continue end
        if tc and plr.Team and LP.Team and plr.Team == LP.Team then continue end
        local part
        if tp == "Closest Bone" then part = ClosestBone(char) else part = char:FindFirstChild(tp) or char:FindFirstChild("Head") end
        if not part then continue end
        local sp, on = Cam:WorldToScreenPoint(part.Position)
        if not on then continue end
        local d = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - ctr).Magnitude
        if d < bestD then bestD = d; best = {P = part, C = char} end
    end
    return best
end

local function PredPos(part, char)
    if not (S.Fl.APr and S.Fl.APr:Get()) then return part.Position end
    local amt = (S.Fl.APA and S.Fl.APA:Get() or 16) / 100
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return part.Position end
    local vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
    if vel.Magnitude < 3 then return part.Position end
    return part.Position + (vel * amt * math.clamp(vel.Magnitude / 50, .3, 1.5))
end

                        e.Box[2].From = Vector2.new(x1, y1); e.Box[2].To = Vector2.new(x1, y1 + cs); e.Box[2].Color = tCol; e.Box[2].Visible = true
                        e.Box[3].From = Vector2.new(x2, y1); e.Box[3].To = Vector2.new(x2 - cs, y1); e.Box[3].Color = tCol; e.Box[3].Visible = true
                        e.Box[4].From = Vector2.new(x2, y1); e.Box[4].To = Vector2.new(x2, y1 + cs); e.Box[4].Color = tCol; e.Box[4].Visible = true
                        e.Box[5].From = Vector2.new(x1, y2); e.Box[5].To = Vector2.new(x1 + cs, y2); e.Box[5].Color = tCol; e.Box[5].Visible = true
                        e.Box[6].From = Vector2.new(x1, y2); e.Box[6].To = Vector2.new(x1, y2 - cs); e.Box[6].Color = tCol; e.Box[6].Visible = true
                        e.Box[7].From = Vector2.new(x2, y2); e.Box[7].To = Vector2.new(x2 - cs, y2); e.Box[7].Color = tCol; e.Box[7].Visible = true
                        e.Box[8].From = Vector2.new(x2, y2); e.Box[8].To = Vector2.new(x2, y2 - cs); e.Box[8].Color = tCol; e.Box[8].Visible = true

                        e.Box[2].From = Vector2.new(x2, y1); e.Box[2].To = Vector2.new(x2, y2); e.Box[2].Color = tCol; e.Box[2].Visible = true
                        e.Box[3].From = Vector2.new(x2, y2); e.Box[3].To = Vector2.new(x1, y2); e.Box[3].Color = tCol; e.Box[3].Visible = true
                        e.Box[4].From = Vector2.new(x1, y2); e.Box[4].To = Vector2.new(x1, y1); e.Box[4].Color = tCol; e.Box[4].Visible = true

## 🚨 CRITICAL BUG FIXES

### Aimbot Issues (PRIORITY)
| Issue | Problem | Solution |
|-------|---------|----------|
| **FOV Circle Misalignment** | Circle position doesn't account for GUI inset (36px topbar) | Use `GuiInsetOffset` or `ViewportSize` properly with inset compensation |
| **Player Detection in FOV** | Using screen distance but FOV circle uses viewport center | Align detection math with visual circle position |
| **Junky Movement** | Camera lerp is frame-dependent and inconsistent | Use delta-time based smoothing with proper easing curves |
| **Smoothing Scale** | 1-2 = fast, 2-18 = slow (non-linear) | Implement exponential/logarithmic scaling from 1-180 |
| **Server-Side Mouse Games** | Camera moves but mouse position doesn't update | Add multiple aim methods: Camera, Mouse.Hit spoof, mousemoverel |
| **Target Switching** | Abrupt snapping when target changes | Add target persistence with grace period |
| **FOV Circle Jitter** | Circle position updates inconsistently | Sync with camera render cycle |
| **Target Loss Flicker** | ESP/Aimbot flickers when target briefly occluded | Add grace period before dropping target |

### Current Smoothing Problem Analysis:
```lua
-- CURRENT CODE (v3.7):
Cam.CFrame = Cam.CFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, math.clamp(1 / smoothing, .01, 1))

-- PROBLEMS:
-- 1. smoothing = 1  → 1/1 = 1.0 (instant snap)
-- 2. smoothing = 2  → 1/2 = 0.5 (very fast)
-- 3. smoothing = 5  → 1/5 = 0.2 (fast)
-- 4. smoothing = 10 → 1/10 = 0.1 (slow)
-- 5. smoothing = 18 → 1/18 = 0.055 (very slow)

-- The jump from 1→2 is MASSIVE (1.0 → 0.5)
-- The jump from 10→18 is tiny (0.1 → 0.055)
-- This is why it feels "junky" - non-linear response
```

### Proposed Smoothing Fix (Multiple Options):
```lua
-- OPTION 1: Exponential Scaling (Recommended)
-- Range: 1-180, where 1 = instant, 180 = very slow
-- Creates a gradual, natural feel
local function CalculateSmoothAlpha(smoothing, deltaTime)
    -- Normalize to 0-1 range (inverted because higher = slower)
    local normalized = 1 - ((smoothing - 1) / 179)
    
    -- Apply exponential curve for natural feel
    -- Using e^(-x) curve for smooth deceleration
    local speed = normalized * 25 -- Max speed multiplier
    local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-speed * deltaTime)
    
    return math.clamp(alpha, 0.001, 1)
end

-- OPTION 2: Logarithmic Scaling
local function CalculateSmoothAlphaLog(smoothing, deltaTime)
    -- Log scale provides more control at lower values
    local logSmooth = math.log(smoothing + 1) / math.log(181)
    local speed = (1 - logSmooth) * 30
    return 1 - math.exp(-speed * deltaTime)
end

-- OPTION 3: Custom Curve with Control Points
local function CalculateSmoothAlphaCurve(smoothing, deltaTime)
    -- Define control points for desired feel
    local curve = {
        {1, 1.0},      -- Instant
        {10, 0.7},     -- Very fast
        {30, 0.4},     -- Fast
        {60, 0.2},     -- Medium
        {90, 0.1},     -- Slow
        {120, 0.05},   -- Very slow
        {180, 0.02}    -- Snail
    }
    -- Interpolate between control points
    -- ... implementation
end
```

### Frame-Rate Independent Movement:
```lua
-- CURRENT (Bad - varies with FPS):
Cam.CFrame = Cam.CFrame:Lerp(target, alpha)

-- NEW (Good - consistent at any FPS):
local function FrameIndependentLerp(current, target, speed, dt)
    -- Using exponential decay for smooth, FPS-independent movement
    local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-speed * dt)
    return current:Lerp(target, alpha)
end

-- In RenderStepped:
RS.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
    local speed = GetSpeedFromSmoothing(smoothingValue)
    Cam.CFrame = FrameIndependentLerp(Cam.CFrame, targetCFrame, speed, deltaTime)
end)
```

### Aimbot Tab Structure (FULLY EXPANDED)

## 👁️ ESP SYSTEM ENHANCEMENTS

### Current ESP Issues Analysis
| Issue | Severity | Description | Solution |
|-------|----------|-------------|----------|
| BillboardGui Detection | MEDIUM | Some anti-cheats scan for orphaned BillboardGuis | Use Drawing API primarily, fallback to GUI |
| Performance | HIGH | Updating every frame for all players | Throttle updates, use LOD system |
| No Depth Info | LOW | Can't tell who's in front | Add depth indicator or sort order |
| Health Bar Always On | LOW | Should follow its own toggle | Separate toggle implemented |
| Limited Colors | LOW | Only enemy/team | Add per-player, health-based, distance-based |
| No Occlusion | MEDIUM | ESP through entire map | Add optional visibility check |

### Notification System Rework:
```lua
NotificationConfig = {
    Enabled = true,
    Position = "TopRight",      -- TopRight/TopLeft/BottomRight/BottomLeft/TopCenter/BottomCenter
    Style = "Toast",            -- Toast/Banner/Minimal/None
    MaxVisible = 5,             -- Maximum simultaneous
    DefaultDuration = 4,        -- Seconds
    
    -- Grouping
    GroupSimilar = true,        -- Stack similar notifications
    GroupDelay = 1,             -- Seconds to consider "similar"
    
    -- Sounds
    EnableSounds = false,
    SoundVolume = 0.5,
    SoundPerType = {            -- Different sound per type
        Info = "rbxasset://...",
        Success = "rbxasset://...",
        Warning = "rbxasset://...",
        Error = "rbxasset://...",
    },
    
    -- Behavior
    PauseOnHover = true,        -- Pause timer when hovered
    ClickToDismiss = true,      -- Click to close
    SwipeToDismiss = true,      -- Mobile swipe
    DoNotDisturb = false,       -- Only show critical
    
    -- History
    KeepHistory = true,
    MaxHistory = 50,
}

-- New Notification Types:
Notify({
    Title = "Title",
    Content = "Message",
    Type = "Success",           -- Info/Success/Warning/Error/Custom
    Duration = 4,
    Icon = "✓",                 -- Custom icon
    Color = Color3,             -- Custom color
    Actions = {                 -- Action buttons
        {Text = "Undo", Callback = function() end},
        {Text = "Details", Callback = function() end},
    },
    Progress = true,            -- Show countdown bar
    Persistent = false,         -- Don't auto-dismiss
    Sound = true,               -- Play sound
    Unique = "key",             -- Replace existing with same key
})
```

## 🏗️ CODE ARCHITECTURE IMPROVEMENTS

### Current Code Issues Analysis:
```lua
-- ISSUE 1: Magic Numbers Everywhere
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, ds and 6 or 11)  -- What do these mean?
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 22)                   -- Why these values?

-- ISSUE 2: Inconsistent Naming
local S = {}   -- State? Settings? Sauce?
local T = {}   -- Theme? Tables? Type?
local Ex = {}  -- Executor? Example? Extension?

-- ISSUE 3: God Functions (100+ lines)
function Tab:CreateToggle(c)
    -- 80 lines of mixed concerns
end

-- ISSUE 4: Scattered Global State
S.ESP = {}
S.Fl = {}
S.Cn = {}
-- All mixed together

-- ISSUE 5: Inconsistent Error Handling
pcall(function() ... end)  -- Sometimes
-- vs just calling directly other times

-- ISSUE 6: Memory Leaks
-- Connections not always tracked
-- Drawing objects not always cleaned

-- ISSUE 7: No Separation of Concerns
-- UI, logic, rendering all mixed
```

### Proposed Architecture:
```

    ├── RenderStepped (visuals)
    ├── Heartbeat (physics)
    ├── Stepped (pre-physics)
    └── Custom intervals
```

### Naming Convention Guide:
```lua
-- Constants: SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE
```

### Error Handling Strategy:
```lua
-- Centralized error handler
local ErrorHandler = {}

function ErrorHandler.wrap(func, context)
    return function(...)
        local success, result = pcall(func, ...)
        if not success then
            ErrorHandler.log(context, result)
            return nil
        end
        return result
    end
end

function ErrorHandler.log(context, error)
    -- Log to console
    warn(string.format("[LvyuFlo] Error in %s: %s", context, tostring(error)))
    
    -- Optional: notify user of critical errors
    if ErrorHandler.shouldNotify(context) then
        Notify({
            Title = "Error",
            Content = "An error occurred in " .. context,
            Type = "Error"
        })
    end
end

-- Usage:
local safeFunction = ErrorHandler.wrap(function()
    -- risky code here
end, "ESP Update")
```

## 🛡️ ANTI-DETECTION & UNDETECTABILITY

### Current Vulnerabilities Analysis:
| Vulnerability | Risk Level | Detection Method | Status |
|--------------|------------|------------------|--------|
| ScreenGui in CoreGui | LOW | Parent scan | ✅ Using random names |
| Drawing library usage | MEDIUM | Environment check | ⚠️ Some ACs detect |
| BillboardGui ESP | MEDIUM | Instance scan | ⚠️ Can be detected |
| Highlight instances | MEDIUM | Instance scan | ⚠️ Can be detected |
| Camera manipulation speed | LOW | Sudden movement | ⚠️ Needs smoothing |
| Mouse.Hit spoofing | HIGH | Hook detection | ❌ Very detectable |
| Raycast hooking | HIGH | Hook detection | ❌ Very detectable |
| Connection patterns | LOW | Connection count | ⚠️ Many connections |
| Memory patterns | LOW | Scanning | ✅ Standard patterns |
| GUI inset position | LOW | Position check | ✅ Handled |

### Anti-Detection Measures:

#### 1. Instance Protection
```lua
local InstanceProtection = {}

-- Randomize all instance names
function InstanceProtection.randomName()
    local chars = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ"
    local length = math.random(8, 16)
    local name = ""
    for i = 1, length do
        local idx = math.random(1, #chars)
        name = name .. chars:sub(idx, idx)
    end
    return name
end

-- Hide from common detection methods
function InstanceProtection.protect(instance)
    -- Random name
    instance.Name = InstanceProtection.randomName()
    
    -- Avoid common parent paths
    -- Don't use easily-scannable properties
    
    -- For ScreenGuis
    if instance:IsA("ScreenGui") then
        instance.ResetOnSpawn = false
        instance.DisplayOrder = math.random(1, 100)
    end
end

-- Anti-GetChildren scan
function InstanceProtection.hideFromScan(gui)
    -- Technique: Use unusual parenting
    -- Some scripts scan PlayerGui/CoreGui children
    -- Using nested containers can help
end
```

#### 2. Hook Protection (for Silent Aim)
```lua
local HookProtection = {}

-- Check if we're being scanned
function HookProtection.isBeingDetected()
    -- Check for common AC patterns
    local suspicious = false
    
    -- Check for AC remote events
    -- Check for integrity checks
    -- Check for timing anomalies
    
    return suspicious
end

-- Safer hooking methods
function HookProtection.safeHook(method, hook)
    -- Only hook if safe
    if HookProtection.isBeingDetected() then
        return false
    end
    
    -- Use metamethod hooks instead of direct replacement when possible
    -- Preserve original function signatures
    -- Match original call patterns
    
    return true
end
```

#### 3. Behavior Mimicry
```lua
local BehaviorMimicry = {}

-- Humanize aimbot movement
function BehaviorMimicry.humanizeMovement(currentAngle, targetAngle, dt)
    -- Never instant snap (even at smoothing = 1, add tiny delay)
    -- Add micro-jitter
    -- Simulate reaction time
    -- Occasional overshoot
    
    local humanizedAngle = targetAngle
    
    -- Add reaction delay
    if self.reactionDelay > 0 then
        return currentAngle
    end
    
    -- Add tiny random offset
    local jitter = Vector2.new(
        math.random(-100, 100) / 1000,
        math.random(-100, 100) / 1000
    )
    humanizedAngle = humanizedAngle + jitter
    
    -- Occasional slight overshoot
    if math.random() < 0.1 then
        local overshoot = (targetAngle - currentAngle) * 0.1
        humanizedAngle = humanizedAngle + overshoot
    end
    
    return humanizedAngle
end

-- Randomize action timing
function BehaviorMimicry.randomDelay(baseDelay)
    -- Add variance to prevent pattern detection
    return baseDelay * (0.8 + math.random() * 0.4)
end
```

## ⚡ PERFORMANCE OPTIMIZATIONS

### Current Performance Issues:
| Issue | Impact | Current Behavior | Solution |
|-------|--------|-----------------|----------|
| ESP every frame | HIGH | Updates all players every frame | Throttle to 30fps |
| Full player iteration | MEDIUM | Loops all players constantly | Cache, spatial hash |
| Billboard GUI updates | MEDIUM | Updates even when not visible | Visibility culling |
| Drawing object creation | LOW | Creates/destroys frequently | Object pooling |
| Multiple raycasts | HIGH | Wall check per player per frame | Batch, cache results |
| No distance culling | MEDIUM | Processes far-away players | Early distance check |
| Unoptimized loops | LOW | Inefficient iteration | Use native loops |
| Memory accumulation | MEDIUM | Objects not cleaned | Proper GC |

### Optimization Strategies:

#### 1. Update Throttling
```lua
local UpdateThrottler = {}

-- Different features update at different rates
local UPDATE_RATES = {
    ESP_BOXES = 1/30,       -- 30 FPS
    ESP_HEALTH = 1/15,      -- 15 FPS (health doesn't change fast)
    ESP_DISTANCE = 1/10,    -- 10 FPS (distance calc is expensive)
    ESP_NAMES = 1/5,        -- 5 FPS (names rarely change)
    AIMBOT = 1/60,          -- 60 FPS (needs to be responsive)
    WALL_CHECK = 1/20,      -- 20 FPS (expensive raycasts)
    UI_UPDATES = 1/30,      -- 30 FPS
}

local lastUpdate = {}

function UpdateThrottler.shouldUpdate(feature, currentTime)
    local rate = UPDATE_RATES[feature] or 1/30
    local last = lastUpdate[feature] or 0
    
    if currentTime - last >= rate then
        lastUpdate[feature] = currentTime
        return true
    end
    return false
end

-- Usage in main loop:
RS.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    local now = tick()
    
    if UpdateThrottler.shouldUpdate("ESP_BOXES", now) then
        updateESPBoxes()
    end
    
    if UpdateThrottler.shouldUpdate("AIMBOT", now) then
        updateAimbot()
    end
end)
```

#### 2. Distance-Based LOD (Level of Detail)
```lua
local LODSystem = {}

local LOD_LEVELS = {
    FULL = {distance = 100, features = {"box", "name", "health", "skeleton", "tracer"}},
    HIGH = {distance = 300, features = {"box", "name", "health"}},
    MEDIUM = {distance = 600, features = {"box", "name"}},
    LOW = {distance = 1000, features = {"box"}},
    MINIMAL = {distance = 2000, features = {"tracer"}},
    NONE = {distance = math.huge, features = {}},
}

function LODSystem.getLODForDistance(distance)
    for _, lod in ipairs(LOD_LEVELS) do
        if distance <= lod.distance then
            return lod
        end
    end
    return LOD_LEVELS.NONE
end

function LODSystem.shouldRenderFeature(distance, feature)
    local lod = LODSystem.getLODForDistance(distance)
    return table.find(lod.features, feature) ~= nil
end
```

#### 3. Spatial Partitioning
```lua
local SpatialHash = {}

local CELL_SIZE = 50 -- studs
local cells = {}

function SpatialHash.getCell(position)
    local cx = math.floor(position.X / CELL_SIZE)
    local cy = math.floor(position.Y / CELL_SIZE)
    local cz = math.floor(position.Z / CELL_SIZE)
    return string.format("%d,%d,%d", cx, cy, cz)
end

function SpatialHash.getNearbyPlayers(position, radius)
    -- Only check players in nearby cells
    local cellsToCheck = SpatialHash.getCellsInRadius(position, radius)
    local nearbyPlayers = {}
    
    for _, cellKey in ipairs(cellsToCheck) do
        local cellPlayers = cells[cellKey]
        if cellPlayers then
            for _, player in ipairs(cellPlayers) do
                table.insert(nearbyPlayers, player)
            end
        end
    end
    
    return nearbyPlayers
end

function SpatialHash.updatePlayer(player, position)
    -- Remove from old cell
    local oldCell = playerCells[player]
    if oldCell and cells[oldCell] then
        -- Remove from old cell
    end
    
    -- Add to new cell
    local newCell = SpatialHash.getCell(position)
    cells[newCell] = cells[newCell] or {}
    table.insert(cells[newCell], player)
    playerCells[player] = newCell
end
```

#### 4. Object Pooling
```lua
local ObjectPool = {}

local pools = {
    DrawingLine = {},
    DrawingCircle = {},
    DrawingText = {},
}

function ObjectPool.get(objectType)
    local pool = pools[objectType]
    if pool and #pool > 0 then
        return table.remove(pool)
    end
    
    -- Create new if pool empty
    if objectType == "DrawingLine" then
        return Drawing.new("Line")
    elseif objectType == "DrawingCircle" then
        return Drawing.new("Circle")
    end
    -- etc.
end

function ObjectPool.release(objectType, object)
    object.Visible = false
    local pool = pools[objectType]
    if pool and #pool < 100 then -- Max pool size
        table.insert(pool, object)
    else
        object:Remove()
    end
end

-- Clear pools periodically
function ObjectPool.trim()
    for poolName, pool in pairs(pools) do
        while #pool > 50 do
            local obj = table.remove(pool)
            pcall(function() obj:Remove() end)
        end
    end
end
```

#### 5. Visibility Culling
```lua
local VisibilityCuller = {}

function VisibilityCuller.isOnScreen(position)
    local screenPoint, onScreen = Camera:WorldToScreenPoint(position)
    return onScreen and screenPoint.Z > 0
end

function VisibilityCuller.isInFrustum(position, padding)
    padding = padding or 100
    local screenPoint, onScreen = Camera:WorldToScreenPoint(position)
    
    if not onScreen or screenPoint.Z <= 0 then
        return false
    end
    
    local viewportSize = Camera.ViewportSize
    return screenPoint.X > -padding 
        and screenPoint.X < viewportSize.X + padding
        and screenPoint.Y > -padding 
        and screenPoint.Y < viewportSize.Y + padding
end

-- Skip processing off-screen players
function processESPForPlayer(player)
    local character = player.Character
    if not character then return end
    
    local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not rootPart then return end
    
    -- Early culling
    if not VisibilityCuller.isInFrustum(rootPart.Position, 200) then
        hideESPForPlayer(player)
        return
    end
    
    -- Continue with ESP processing...
end
```
