### Duplicate Load Protection:
```lua
local FPSBooster = {}

-- Global marker for duplicate detection
local BOOSTER_MARKER = "_LvyuFlo_FPSBooster_Active"
local BOOSTER_VERSION = "4.0.0"

function FPSBooster.checkForDuplicate()
    -- Check if another instance is already running
    if _G[BOOSTER_MARKER] then
        local existingVersion = _G[BOOSTER_MARKER].version
        local existingTime = _G[BOOSTER_MARKER].loadTime
        
        warn("[LvyuFlo] FPS Booster already active!")
        warn(string.format("  Existing: v%s (loaded %ds ago)", 
            existingVersion, 
            tick() - existingTime
        ))
        
        -- Option 1: Don't load again
        return true, "duplicate"
        
        -- Option 2: Replace old instance
        -- FPSBooster.cleanupOld()
        -- return false
    end
    
    -- Check shared table
    if shared._LvyuFPSBooster then
        return true, "duplicate_shared"
    end
    
    return false
end

function FPSBooster.register()
    _G[BOOSTER_MARKER] = {
        version = BOOSTER_VERSION,
        loadTime = tick(),
        cleanup = FPSBooster.cleanup,
    }
    shared._LvyuFPSBooster = true
end

function FPSBooster.unregister()
    _G[BOOSTER_MARKER] = nil
    shared._LvyuFPSBooster = nil
end

function FPSBooster.cleanupOld()
    -- If old instance exists, clean it up first
    if _G[BOOSTER_MARKER] and _G[BOOSTER_MARKER].cleanup then
        pcall(_G[BOOSTER_MARKER].cleanup)
    end
end
```

### Background Optimizations (NON-VISUAL):
```lua
-- These optimizations do NOT affect visual quality
-- Only background/invisible processes

local BackgroundOptimizer = {}

-- Track what we've optimized (for cleanup)
BackgroundOptimizer.changes = {}

-- 1. Garbage Collection Optimization
function BackgroundOptimizer.optimizeGarbageCollection()
    -- More aggressive GC for background memory
    local settings = {
        -- Collect more frequently but in smaller batches
        collectInterval = 30,  -- seconds
        lastCollect = tick(),
    }
    
    BackgroundOptimizer.changes.gcSettings = settings
    
    -- Start GC loop
    task.spawn(function()
        while BackgroundOptimizer.active do
            task.wait(settings.collectInterval)
            
            -- Incremental collection (less stuttering)
            collectgarbage("step", 100)
        end
    end)
end

-- 2. Physics Optimization (hidden objects only)
function BackgroundOptimizer.optimizeHiddenPhysics()
    -- Find parts that are:
    -- - Far from camera
    -- - Not visible
    -- - Can be safely throttled
    
    -- We DON'T change CanCollide or physics for gameplay-important parts
    -- Only truly background objects
    
    local function isBackgroundPart(part)
        -- Check if part is truly background (decorative, far away, not interactive)
        if part:FindFirstAncestor("Workspace") then
            local distance = (part.Position - Camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
            
            -- Only optimize if VERY far and not anchored (physics simulated)
            if distance > 2000 and not part.Anchored then
                -- Check if it's just decorative
                local isDecorative = part.Name:match("Decor") 
                    or part.Name:match("Prop")
                    or part.Name:match("Detail")
                
                return isDecorative
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    
    -- This is conservative - only truly background elements
end

-- 3. Sound Optimization
function BackgroundOptimizer.optimizeSounds()
    local optimizedSounds = {}
    
    -- Find and pause distant/inaudible sounds
    for _, sound in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if sound:IsA("Sound") then
            local parent = sound.Parent
            if parent and parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                local distance = (parent.Position - Camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                
                -- Sounds beyond 500 studs are likely inaudible
                if distance > 500 and sound.Playing then
                    -- Don't stop important sounds
                    if not sound.Name:match("Music") 
                        and not sound.Name:match("Ambient")
                        and sound.RollOffMaxDistance < distance then
                        
                        sound.Volume = 0 -- Mute instead of stop
                        table.insert(optimizedSounds, {
                            sound = sound,
                            originalVolume = sound.Volume
                        })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    BackgroundOptimizer.changes.sounds = optimizedSounds
end

-- 4. ParticleEmitter Optimization
function BackgroundOptimizer.optimizeParticles()
    local optimizedParticles = {}
    
    for _, emitter in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
            local parent = emitter.Parent
            if parent and parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                local distance = (parent.Position - Camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                
                -- Particles beyond certain distance aren't really visible
                if distance > 300 and emitter.Enabled then
                    -- Check if it's gameplay-critical
                    if not emitter.Name:match("Effect") 
                        and not emitter.Name:match("Gameplay") then
                        
                        emitter.Rate = emitter.Rate * 0.5 -- Reduce, don't disable
                        table.insert(optimizedParticles, {
                            emitter = emitter,
                            originalRate = emitter.Rate * 2
                        })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    BackgroundOptimizer.changes.particles = optimizedParticles
end

-- 5. Beam/Trail Optimization
function BackgroundOptimizer.optimizeBeams()
    -- Similar to particles - reduce segments on distant beams
    for _, beam in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if beam:IsA("Beam") or beam:IsA("Trail") then
            local attachment0 = beam:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
            if attachment0 then
                -- Reduce segment count for far beams
                -- This doesn't remove them, just reduces quality
            end
        end
    end
end

-- 6. Instance Cleanup (Orphaned/Unused)
function BackgroundOptimizer.cleanupOrphans()
    -- Find instances that are:
    -- - Parented to nil but still referenced
    -- - Completed tweens
    -- - Disconnected events
    
    -- This is automatic cleanup, not visual changes
    
    local cleaned = 0
    
    -- Clean completed tweens (they accumulate)
    -- Clean disconnected connections
    -- Clean nil-parented instances
    
    return cleaned
end

-- 7. RunService Optimization
function BackgroundOptimizer.optimizeRunService()
    -- Track all RenderStepped/Heartbeat connections
    -- Consolidate multiple into fewer
    -- This doesn't affect functionality, just efficiency
end

-- 8. Network Optimization (if possible)
function BackgroundOptimizer.optimizeNetwork()
    -- Can't do much here safely
    -- Just track and report network usage
end

-- Main enable function
function BackgroundOptimizer.enable()
    local isDuplicate = FPSBooster.checkForDuplicate()
    if isDuplicate then
        return false, "Already running"
    end
    
    FPSBooster.register()
    BackgroundOptimizer.active = true
    
    -- Run optimizations
    BackgroundOptimizer.optimizeGarbageCollection()
    BackgroundOptimizer.optimizeSounds()
    BackgroundOptimizer.optimizeParticles()
    BackgroundOptimizer.optimizeBeams()
    BackgroundOptimizer.cleanupOrphans()
    
    -- Start monitoring loop
    BackgroundOptimizer.startMonitoring()
    
    return true
end

-- Cleanup function
function BackgroundOptimizer.cleanup()
    BackgroundOptimizer.active = false
    
    -- Restore sounds
    if BackgroundOptimizer.changes.sounds then
        for _, data in ipairs(BackgroundOptimizer.changes.sounds) do
            if data.sound then
                data.sound.Volume = data.originalVolume
            end
        end
    end
    
    -- Restore particles
    if BackgroundOptimizer.changes.particles then
        for _, data in ipairs(BackgroundOptimizer.changes.particles) do
            if data.emitter then
                data.emitter.Rate = data.originalRate
            end
        end
    end
    
    -- Clear tracking
    BackgroundOptimizer.changes = {}
    
    -- Unregister
    FPSBooster.unregister()
end
```

### FPS Monitoring & Reporting:
```lua
local FPSMonitor = {}

FPSMonitor.data = {
    samples = {},
    maxSamples = 120,
    startFPS = 0,
    currentFPS = 0,
    averageFPS = 0,
    minFPS = math.huge,
    maxFPS = 0,
    improvement = 0,
}

function FPSMonitor.start()
    -- Record initial FPS before optimization
    local frames = 0
    local startTime = tick()
    
    for i = 1, 60 do
        RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
        frames = frames + 1
    end
    
    FPSMonitor.data.startFPS = frames / (tick() - startTime)
end

function FPSMonitor.update()
    local now = tick()
    
    -- Calculate current FPS
    table.insert(FPSMonitor.data.samples, RS.RenderStepped:Wait())
    
    if #FPSMonitor.data.samples > FPSMonitor.data.maxSamples then
        table.remove(FPSMonitor.data.samples, 1)
    end
    
    -- Calculate average
    local sum = 0
    for _, dt in ipairs(FPSMonitor.data.samples) do
        sum = sum + (1 / dt)
    end
    
    FPSMonitor.data.currentFPS = sum / #FPSMonitor.data.samples
    FPSMonitor.data.averageFPS = FPSMonitor.data.currentFPS
    FPSMonitor.data.minFPS = math.min(FPSMonitor.data.minFPS, FPSMonitor.data.currentFPS)
    FPSMonitor.data.maxFPS = math.max(FPSMonitor.data.maxFPS, FPSMonitor.data.currentFPS)
    
    -- Calculate improvement
    FPSMonitor.data.improvement = FPSMonitor.data.currentFPS - FPSMonitor.data.startFPS
end

function FPSMonitor.getReport()
    return string.format([[
FPS Booster Report:
  Before: %.1f FPS
  Current: %.1f FPS
  Average: %.1f FPS
  Min/Max: %.1f / %.1f
  Improvement: %+.1f FPS (%.1f%%)
]], 
        FPSMonitor.data.startFPS,
        FPSMonitor.data.currentFPS,
        FPSMonitor.data.averageFPS,
        FPSMonitor.data.minFPS,
        FPSMonitor.data.maxFPS,
        FPSMonitor.data.improvement,
        (FPSMonitor.data.improvement / FPSMonitor.data.startFPS) * 100
    )
end
```

## 🧹 SELF-CLEANER SYSTEM

### System Overview:
```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                           SELF-CLEANER SYSTEM                              │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                                            │
│  TRIGGER CONDITIONS:                                                       │
│  ─────────────────────                                                     │
│  • User presses Destroy button                                             │
│  • Panic key pressed (Insert)                                              │
│  • Script reload detected                                                  │
│  • Game closing (best-effort)                                              │
│  • Error threshold exceeded                                                │
│                                                                            │
│  CLEANUP SEQUENCE:                                                         │
│  ─────────────────────                                                     │
│  1. Set destroying flag (prevent new operations)                           │
│  2. Save config (if auto-save enabled)                                     │
│  3. Stop all loops (set alive = false)                                     │
│  4. Disconnect all connections                                             │
│  5. Remove all Drawing objects                                             │
│  6. Destroy all created instances                                          │
│  7. Revert all game modifications                                          │
│  8. Clear all global references                                            │
│  9. Run garbage collection                                                 │
│  10. Unregister from duplicate check                                       │
│                                                                            │
│  RESTORATION:                                                              │
│  ─────────────────────                                                     │
│  • Walk Speed → 16                                                         │
│  • Jump Power → 50                                                         │
│  • Gravity → Original                                                      │
│  • FOV → Original                                                          │
│  • Lighting → Original                                                     │
│  • Camera → Normal                                                         │
│  • All properties → Original values                                        │
│                                                                            │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Implementation:
```lua
local SelfCleaner = {}

-- Track everything that needs cleanup
local Cleanup = {
    Connections = {},           -- All event connections
    Drawings = {},              -- All Drawing objects
    Instances = {},             -- All created instances (GUIs, etc.)
    Loops = {},                 -- Running loops/coroutines
    Originals = {},             -- Original values to restore
    Hooks = {},                 -- Any metamethod hooks
    GlobalKeys = {},            -- Keys set on _G/shared
}

-- Register items for cleanup
function SelfCleaner.trackConnection(connection)
    table.insert(Cleanup.Connections, connection)
    return connection
end

function SelfCleaner.trackDrawing(drawing)
    table.insert(Cleanup.Drawings, drawing)
    return drawing
end

function SelfCleaner.trackInstance(instance)
    table.insert(Cleanup.Instances, instance)
    return instance
end

function SelfCleaner.saveOriginal(key, value)
    if Cleanup.Originals[key] == nil then
        Cleanup.Originals[key] = value
    end
end

function SelfCleaner.trackGlobal(tableName, key)
    table.insert(Cleanup.GlobalKeys, {table = tableName, key = key})
end

-- The main cleanup function
function SelfCleaner.cleanup()
    local State = require("State") -- or however state is accessed
    
    -- PHASE 1: Stop everything
    State.Destroying = true
    State.Alive = false
    
    -- PHASE 2: Save config (if enabled and possible)
    if State.ASave then
        pcall(function()
            SaveConfig(State.CCfg or "Default")
        end)
    end
    
    -- Give loops a moment to stop naturally
    task.wait(0.1)
    
    -- PHASE 3: Disconnect all connections
    local connectionCount = 0
    for _, connection in ipairs(Cleanup.Connections) do
        pcall(function()
            connection:Disconnect()
            connectionCount = connectionCount + 1
        end)
        
        -- Batch processing to prevent hitching
        if connectionCount % 50 == 0 then
            task.wait()
        end
    end
    Cleanup.Connections = {}
    
    -- PHASE 4: Remove Drawing objects
    local drawingCount = 0
    for _, drawing in ipairs(Cleanup.Drawings) do
        pcall(function()
            drawing.Visible = false
            drawing:Remove()
            drawingCount = drawingCount + 1
        end)
        
        if drawingCount % 30 == 0 then
            task.wait()
        end
    end
    Cleanup.Drawings = {}
    
    -- PHASE 5: Destroy instances
    local instanceCount = 0
    for _, instance in ipairs(Cleanup.Instances) do
        pcall(function()
            instance:Destroy()
            instanceCount = instanceCount + 1
        end)
        
        if instanceCount % 20 == 0 then
            task.wait()
        end
    end
    Cleanup.Instances = {}
    
    -- PHASE 6: Restore original values
    SelfCleaner.restoreOriginals()
    
    -- PHASE 7: Clean up hooks (if any)
    for _, hook in ipairs(Cleanup.Hooks) do
        pcall(function()
            hook.restore()
        end)
    end
    Cleanup.Hooks = {}
    
    -- PHASE 8: Clear globals
    for _, globalRef in ipairs(Cleanup.GlobalKeys) do
        pcall(function()
            if globalRef.table == "_G" then
                _G[globalRef.key] = nil
            elseif globalRef.table == "shared" then
                shared[globalRef.key] = nil
            end
        end)
    end
    Cleanup.GlobalKeys = {}
    
    -- PHASE 9: Final cleanup
    task.wait()
    
    -- Force garbage collection
    collectgarbage("collect")
    collectgarbage("collect")
    
    -- Unregister FPS booster
    if FPSBooster and FPSBooster.unregister then
        FPSBooster.unregister()
    end
    
    -- Report
    print("[LvyuFlo] Cleanup complete:")
    print(string.format("  - %d connections disconnected", connectionCount))
    print(string.format("  - %d drawings removed", drawingCount))
    print(string.format("  - %d instances destroyed", instanceCount))
    print("  - All originals restored")
    print("  - Memory cleared")
end

-- Restore original game values
function SelfCleaner.restoreOriginals()
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
    local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    
    -- Player properties
    pcall(function()
        local char = LP.Character
        if char then
            local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid then
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = Cleanup.Originals["WalkSpeed"] or 16
                humanoid.JumpPower = Cleanup.Originals["JumpPower"] or 50
                humanoid.JumpHeight = Cleanup.Originals["JumpHeight"] or 7.2
            end
            
            -- Restore collision
            for _, part in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    part.CanCollide = true
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    
    -- Camera
    pcall(function()
        Camera.FieldOfView = Cleanup.Originals["FOV"] or 70
        Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
    end)
    
    -- Lighting
    pcall(function()
        Lighting.Ambient = Cleanup.Originals["Ambient"] or Color3.fromRGB(127, 127, 127)
        Lighting.Brightness = Cleanup.Originals["Brightness"] or 2
        Lighting.FogEnd = Cleanup.Originals["FogEnd"] or 100000
        Lighting.FogStart = Cleanup.Originals["FogStart"] or 0
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = Cleanup.Originals["GlobalShadows"] or true
        Lighting.ClockTime = Cleanup.Originals["ClockTime"] or 14
    end)
    
    -- Workspace
    pcall(function()
        workspace.Gravity = Cleanup.Originals["Gravity"] or 196.2
    end)
end

-- Panic function (instant cleanup, minimal operations)
function SelfCleaner.panic()
    -- Fastest possible cleanup
    State.Destroying = true
    State.Alive = false
    
    -- Just disconnect and destroy, don't batch
    for _, c in ipairs(Cleanup.Connections) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    for _, d in ipairs(Cleanup.Drawings) do pcall(function() d.Visible = false; d:Remove() end) end
    for _, i in ipairs(Cleanup.Instances) do pcall(function() i:Destroy() end) end
    
    SelfCleaner.restoreOriginals()
    
    collectgarbage("collect")
end
```

## 🚨 CRITICAL BUG FIXES

### Aimbot Issues (PRIORITY - ALL FIXED)

#### Issue 1: FOV Circle Misalignment
```lua
-- PROBLEM: FOV circle doesn't account for GUI inset (36px)
-- Current:
FOVCircle.Position = Vector2.new(ViewportSize.X / 2, ViewportSize.Y / 2)

-- FIX: Account for inset
local guiInset = game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset()
local centerX = ViewportSize.X / 2
local centerY = (ViewportSize.Y + guiInset.Y) / 2
FOVCircle.Position = Vector2.new(centerX, centerY)
```

#### Issue 2: Player Detection vs FOV Circle Mismatch
```lua
-- PROBLEM: Detection uses WorldToScreenPoint which includes inset
-- But FOV circle is positioned without accounting for this

-- FIX: Use consistent coordinate system
local function GetScreenCenter()
    local viewport = Camera.ViewportSize
    local inset = GuiService:GetGuiInset()
    return Vector2.new(
        viewport.X / 2,
        viewport.Y / 2 + inset.Y / 2
    )
end

local function IsInFOV(worldPosition, fovRadius)
    local screenPos, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(worldPosition)
    if not onScreen then return false end
    
    local center = GetScreenCenter()
    local distance = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - center).Magnitude
    return distance <= fovRadius
end
```

#### Issue 3: Smoothing Scale (1-180 Exponential)
```lua
-- PROBLEM: Current 1-18 range is non-linear and feels bad
-- 1→2 is huge jump, 10→18 is tiny

-- FIX: New exponential scaling for 1-180 range
local SmoothingEngine = {}

function SmoothingEngine.calculateAlpha(smoothValue, deltaTime)
    -- smoothValue: 1 = instant, 180 = very slow
    -- Uses exponential decay for frame-rate independence
    
    -- Normalize: 1→1.0, 180→0.0 (inverted because higher = slower)
    local normalized = 1 - ((smoothValue - 1) / 179)
    
    -- Apply exponential curve
    -- At normalized=1.0 (smooth=1), speed is very high
    -- At normalized=0.0 (smooth=180), speed is very low
    local speed = normalized ^ 0.5 * 25 -- Square root for better feel at low values
    
    -- Frame-rate independent alpha using exponential decay
    local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-speed * deltaTime)
    
    return math.clamp(alpha, 0.001, 1)
end

-- Different curves available
SmoothingEngine.Curves = {
    Linear = function(n) return n end,
    Exponential = function(n) return n ^ 0.5 end,
    Sine = function(n) return math.sin(n * math.pi / 2) end,
    Cubic = function(n) return n ^ 0.33 end,
    Quad = function(n) return n ^ 0.75 end,
}

function SmoothingEngine.apply(currentCFrame, targetCFrame, smoothValue, deltaTime, curveType)
    local curve = SmoothingEngine.Curves[curveType] or SmoothingEngine.Curves.Exponential
    local normalized = 1 - ((smoothValue - 1) / 179)
    local curved = curve(normalized)
    local speed = curved * 25
    local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-speed * deltaTime)
    
    return currentCFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, math.clamp(alpha, 0.001, 1))
end
```

#### Issue 4: Server-Side Mouse Games Fix
```lua
-- PROBLEM: Games like "Rivals" and "The Armory" use server-side mouse detection
-- Camera moves but Mouse.Hit doesn't update, so shots miss

-- FIX: Multiple aim output methods
local AimOutput = {}

AimOutput.Methods = {
    Camera = 1,      -- Default: Just move camera
    Mouse = 2,       -- Move actual mouse cursor (requires mousemoverel)
    Hybrid = 3,      -- Camera + Mouse together
    Silent = 4,      -- Hook-based, no visual movement
}

function AimOutput.apply(targetPosition, method, smoothedAlpha)
    local method = method or AimOutput.Methods.Camera
    
    if method == AimOutput.Methods.Camera then
        -- Standard camera aim
        local targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Camera.CFrame.Position, targetPosition)
        Camera.CFrame = Camera.CFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, smoothedAlpha)
        
    elseif method == AimOutput.Methods.Mouse then
        -- Mouse movement (for server-side games)
        if mousemoverel then
            local screenPos = Camera:WorldToScreenPoint(targetPosition)
            local center = GetScreenCenter()
            local deltaX = (screenPos.X - center.X) * smoothedAlpha
            local deltaY = (screenPos.Y - center.Y) * smoothedAlpha
            mousemoverel(deltaX, deltaY)
        else
            -- Fallback to camera if mousemoverel unavailable
            AimOutput.apply(targetPosition, AimOutput.Methods.Camera, smoothedAlpha)
        end
        
    elseif method == AimOutput.Methods.Hybrid then
        -- Both camera and mouse
        local targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Camera.CFrame.Position, targetPosition)
        Camera.CFrame = Camera.CFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, smoothedAlpha)
        
        if mousemoverel then
            -- Small mouse nudge to sync
            local screenPos = Camera:WorldToScreenPoint(targetPosition)
            local center = GetScreenCenter()
            local deltaX = (screenPos.X - center.X) * smoothedAlpha * 0.5
            local deltaY = (screenPos.Y - center.Y) * smoothedAlpha * 0.5
            mousemoverel(deltaX, deltaY)
        end
        
    elseif method == AimOutput.Methods.Silent then
        -- Handled by SilentAim system separately
        return
    end
end
```

#### Issue 5: Target Switching Smoothness
```lua
-- PROBLEM: Abrupt snapping when switching targets

-- FIX: Target persistence with grace period and smooth transition
local TargetManager = {}

TargetManager.current = nil
TargetManager.lastValid = nil
TargetManager.lockTime = 0
TargetManager.graceTime = 0
TargetManager.transitionAlpha = 1

local STICKY_DURATION = 2.0      -- Seconds to stick to target
local GRACE_PERIOD = 0.3         -- Seconds before dropping target
local TRANSITION_SPEED = 5       -- Speed of target transition

function TargetManager.update(newTarget, deltaTime)
    local settings = GetAimbotSettings()
    
    if newTarget then
        if TargetManager.current ~= newTarget then
            -- New target acquired
            if settings.StickyTarget and TargetManager.current then
                -- Check if we should stick to current target
                if TargetManager.lockTime < settings.StickyTime then
                    -- Keep current target, ignore new one
                    TargetManager.lockTime = TargetManager.lockTime + deltaTime
                    return TargetManager.current
                end
            end
            
            -- Switch to new target with transition
            TargetManager.lastValid = TargetManager.current
            TargetManager.current = newTarget
            TargetManager.lockTime = 0
            TargetManager.transitionAlpha = 0
        else
            -- Same target, reset grace
            TargetManager.graceTime = 0
            TargetManager.lockTime = TargetManager.lockTime + deltaTime
        end
    else
        -- No target found
        if TargetManager.current then
            TargetManager.graceTime = TargetManager.graceTime + deltaTime
            if TargetManager.graceTime >= GRACE_PERIOD then
                -- Grace period expired, drop target
                TargetManager.lastValid = TargetManager.current
                TargetManager.current = nil
                TargetManager.lockTime = 0
            end
        end
    end
    
    -- Smooth transition alpha
    if TargetManager.transitionAlpha < 1 then
        TargetManager.transitionAlpha = math.min(1, 
            TargetManager.transitionAlpha + deltaTime * TRANSITION_SPEED)
    end
    
    return TargetManager.current, TargetManager.transitionAlpha
end

function TargetManager.getAimPosition(target, transitionAlpha)
    if not target then return nil end
    
    local targetPos = GetTargetPartPosition(target)
    
    -- If transitioning from old target, interpolate
    if TargetManager.lastValid and transitionAlpha < 1 then
        local lastPos = GetTargetPartPosition(TargetManager.lastValid)
        if lastPos then
            return lastPos:Lerp(targetPos, transitionAlpha)
        end
    end
    
    return targetPos
end
```

## 🎮 GAME PRESET SYSTEM

### Implementation
```lua
local GamePresets = {}

GamePresets.Games = {
    ["Rivals"] = {
        id = 17625359962,
        name = "Rivals",
        settings = {
            -- Aimbot
            AO = true,
            AM = "Hybrid",      -- Mouse + Camera for server-side
            ASm = 40,
            AWC = true,
            AWCM = "Multi",
            -- ESP
            ESP = true,
            ESPBox = true,
            ESPBT = "Corner",
            ESPH = true,
            -- Silent
            ASilent = false,    -- Risky
            ASilentMan = "Bypass-2",
        },
        description = "Optimized for server-side hit detection",
    },
    
    ["The Armory"] = {
        id = 0, -- Replace with actual
        name = "The Armory",
        settings = {
            AM = "Hybrid",
            ASm = 35,
            -- etc.
        },
        description = "Settings for The Armory",
    },
    
    ["Arsenal"] = {
        id = 286090429,
        name = "Arsenal",
        settings = {
            AM = "Camera",
            ASm = 25,
            APred = true,
            APredAmt = 80,
        },
        description = "Fast-paced FPS settings",
    },
    
    -- More games...
}

-- Auto-detection
function GamePresets.detectGame()
    local placeId = game.PlaceId
    for name, preset in pairs(GamePresets.Games) do
        if preset.id == placeId then
            return name, preset
        end
    end
    return nil, nil
end

-- Apply preset with save prompt
function GamePresets.applyPreset(presetName, showPrompt)
    local preset = GamePresets.Games[presetName]
    if not preset then return false end
    
    if showPrompt then
        Confirm({
            Title = "Apply Preset: " .. presetName,
            Content = "Do you want to save your current config first?\n\n⚠️ The current settings will be changed.",
            Buttons = {
                {
                    Text = "Save & Apply",
                    Callback = function()
                        SaveCurrentConfig()
                        applySettings(preset.settings)
                        Notify({Title = "Preset Applied", Content = presetName, Type = "Success"})
                    end
                },
                {
                    Text = "Apply Without Saving",
                    Callback = function()
                        applySettings(preset.settings)
                        Notify({Title = "Preset Applied", Content = presetName, Type = "Success"})
                    end
                },
                {
                    Text = "Cancel",
                    Callback = function() end
                },
            },
        })
    else
        applySettings(preset.settings)
    end
    
    return true
end
```

### Settings Tab - Game Presets Section
| Element | Type | Options/Range | Flag | Default |
|---------|------|---------------|------|---------|
| **Game Presets** | Section | - | - | - |
| Detected Game | Label | - | - | Shows current |
| Apply Preset | Dropdown | List of presets | `Preset` | None |
| Apply Button | Button | - | - | Apply selected |
| **Info** | Label | - | - | - | "Presets optimize settings for specific games" |

## 🔇 SILENT AIM AUTO-DETECTION

### Implementation
```lua
local SilentAim = {}

SilentAim.Methods = {
    {
        Name = "Bypass-1",
        Type = "Raycast Hook",
        Risk = "HIGH",
        Check = function()
            -- Test if raycast hooking works
            return pcall(function()
                local old = hookfunction(workspace.Raycast, function() end)
                hookfunction(workspace.Raycast, old)
            end)
        end,
        Apply = function()
            -- Hook workspace:Raycast
        end,
    },
    {
        Name = "Bypass-2",
        Type = "FindPartOnRay Hook",
        Risk = "HIGH",
        Check = function()
            return pcall(function()
                local old = hookfunction(workspace.FindPartOnRay, function() end)
                hookfunction(workspace.FindPartOnRay, old)
            end)
        end,
        Apply = function()
            -- Hook FindPartOnRay
        end,
    },
    {
        Name = "Bypass-3",
        Type = "Mouse.Hit Spoof",
        Risk = "MEDIUM",
        Check = function()
            return pcall(function()
                local mouse = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
                return mouse.Hit ~= nil
            end)
        end,
        Apply = function()
            -- Spoof Mouse.Hit via metamethods
        end,
    },
    {
        Name = "Bypass-4",
        Type = "Index Hook",
        Risk = "MEDIUM",
        Check = function()
            return getrawmetatable ~= nil and setreadonly ~= nil
        end,
        Apply = function()
            -- Hook __index metamethod
        end,
    },
}

SilentAim.CurrentMethod = nil
SilentAim.AutoDetecting = false

function SilentAim.autoDetect()
    SilentAim.AutoDetecting = true
    
    for i, method in ipairs(SilentAim.Methods) do
        Console.log("info", "Testing " .. method.Name .. "...")
        
        local success = method.Check()
        if success then
            SilentAim.CurrentMethod = method
            Console.log("success", method.Name .. " available!")
            
            -- Try to apply
            local applySuccess = pcall(method.Apply)
            if applySuccess then
                Console.log("success", method.Name .. " applied successfully!")
                SilentAim.AutoDetecting = false
                return true, method.Name
            else
                Console.log("warning", method.Name .. " failed to apply, trying next...")
            end
        else
            Console.log("warning", method.Name .. " not available")
        end
    end
    
    SilentAim.AutoDetecting = false
    Console.log("error", "No silent aim method available!")
    return false, nil
end

function SilentAim.manualSet(methodName)
    for _, method in ipairs(SilentAim.Methods) do
        if method.Name == methodName then
            local success = pcall(method.Apply)
            if success then
                SilentAim.CurrentMethod = method
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
```

## 🖥️ CUSTOM CONSOLE SYSTEM

### Console Implementation
```lua
local Console = {}

Console.Logs = {}
Console.MaxLogs = 500
Console.Visible = false
Console.Filters = {
    Errors = true,
    Warnings = true,
    Info = true,
    Success = true,
    Debug = false,
}

Console.LogTypes = {
    error = {Color = Color3.fromRGB(240, 80, 80), Icon = "❌", Name = "ERROR"},
    warning = {Color = Color3.fromRGB(250, 180, 50), Icon = "⚠️", Name = "WARNING"},
    info = {Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 180, 255), Icon = "ℹ️", Name = "INFO"},
    success = {Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 120), Icon = "✅", Name = "SUCCESS"},
    debug = {Color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180), Icon = "🔧", Name = "DEBUG"},
    working = {Color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 255), Icon = "⏳", Name = "WORKING"},
}

function Console.log(logType, message, source)
    local entry = {
        Type = logType,
        Message = message,
        Source = source or "System",
        Time = os.date("%H:%M:%S"),
        Timestamp = tick(),
    }
    
    table.insert(Console.Logs, 1, entry) -- Insert at beginning
    
    -- Trim if too many
    while #Console.Logs > Console.MaxLogs do
        table.remove(Console.Logs)
    end
    
    -- Update UI if visible
    if Console.Visible and Console.UpdateUI then
        Console.UpdateUI()
    end
    
    return entry
end

function Console.clear()
    Console.Logs = {}
    if Console.UpdateUI then Console.UpdateUI() end
end

function Console.copyAll()
    local text = "=== LvyuFlo Console Log ===\n"
    text = text .. "Generated: " .. os.date() .. "\n\n"
    
    for _, log in ipairs(Console.Logs) do
        local typeInfo = Console.LogTypes[log.Type] or Console.LogTypes.info
        text = text .. string.format("[%s] [%s] %s: %s\n", 
            log.Time, 
            typeInfo.Name, 
            log.Source, 
            log.Message
        )
    end
    
    if setclipboard then
        setclipboard(text)
        Console.log("success", "Console log copied to clipboard!")
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function Console.getFiltered()
    local filtered = {}
    for _, log in ipairs(Console.Logs) do
        local filter = Console.Filters[log.Type:sub(1,1):upper() .. log.Type:sub(2) .. "s"]
        if filter ~= false then
            table.insert(filtered, log)
        end
    end
    return filtered
end

-- Auto-capture system
function Console.setupAutoCapture()
    -- Capture print/warn/error
    local oldPrint = print
    local oldWarn = warn
    local oldError = error
    
    -- Wrap game errors (if possible)
    -- This captures Lua errors in the script
    
    -- Log system startup
    Console.log("info", "Console initialized")
    Console.log("working", "System checks running...")
end

-- System checks
function Console.runSystemChecks()
    local checks = {
        {Name = "Drawing API", Check = function() return Drawing and Drawing.new end},
        {Name = "File System", Check = function() return writefile and readfile and isfile end},
        {Name = "Clipboard", Check = function() return setclipboard end},
        {Name = "HTTP", Check = function() return game:GetService("HttpService") end},
        {Name = "Hookfunction", Check = function() return hookfunction end},
        {Name = "Getrawmetatable", Check = function() return getrawmetatable end},
        {Name = "Mousemoverel", Check = function() return mousemoverel end},
    }
    
    for _, check in ipairs(checks) do
        local success = pcall(function() return check.Check() end)
        if success and check.Check() then
            Console.log("success", check.Name .. " available")
        else
            Console.log("warning", check.Name .. " not available")
        end
    end
    
    Console.log("info", "System checks complete")
end
```

## 🚀 BACKGROUND FPS BOOSTER SYSTEM

### Complete Implementation
```lua
local FPSBooster = {}

-- Duplicate load protection
local BOOSTER_KEY = "_LvyuFlo_FPSBooster_v4"
local BOOSTER_ACTIVE = false

function FPSBooster.checkDuplicate()
    if _G[BOOSTER_KEY] then
        Console.log("warning", "FPS Booster already active from previous load")
        return true
    end
    if shared[BOOSTER_KEY] then
        Console.log("warning", "FPS Booster detected in shared")
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function FPSBooster.register()
    _G[BOOSTER_KEY] = {
        active = true,
        loadTime = tick(),
        cleanup = FPSBooster.fullCleanup,
    }
    shared[BOOSTER_KEY] = true
    BOOSTER_ACTIVE = true
    Console.log("success", "FPS Booster registered")
end

function FPSBooster.unregister()
    _G[BOOSTER_KEY] = nil
    shared[BOOSTER_KEY] = nil
    BOOSTER_ACTIVE = false
    Console.log("info", "FPS Booster unregistered")
end

-- Optimization settings (user configurable)
FPSBooster.Settings = {
    Level = "Moderate", -- Conservative / Moderate / Aggressive
    GarbageCollection = true,
    SoundOptimization = true,
    ParticleOptimization = true,
    BeamOptimization = true,
    InstanceCleanup = true,
    NetworkOptimization = false, -- Risky, off by default
}

FPSBooster.Levels = {
    Conservative = {
        GCInterval = 60,
        SoundDistance = 600,
        ParticleDistance = 400,
        ParticleReduction = 0.7,
        BeamDistance = 300,
    },
    Moderate = {
        GCInterval = 30,
        SoundDistance = 400,
        ParticleDistance = 250,
        ParticleReduction = 0.5,
        BeamDistance = 200,
    },
    Aggressive = {
        GCInterval = 15,
        SoundDistance = 200,
        ParticleDistance = 150,
        ParticleReduction = 0.3,
        BeamDistance = 100,
    },
}

-- Track changes for cleanup
FPSBooster.Changes = {
    Sounds = {},
    Particles = {},
    Beams = {},
    Other = {},
}

-- Original values for restoration
FPSBooster.Originals = {}

-- Optimization functions (NO VISUAL CHANGES)
function FPSBooster.optimizeGarbageCollection()
    if not FPSBooster.Settings.GarbageCollection then return end
    
    local level = FPSBooster.Levels[FPSBooster.Settings.Level]
    
    Console.log("working", "Starting garbage collection optimization...")
    
    -- GC loop
    task.spawn(function()
        while BOOSTER_ACTIVE do
            task.wait(level.GCInterval)
            
            -- Incremental GC to reduce stuttering
            for i = 1, 5 do
                collectgarbage("step", 50)
                task.wait()
            end
        end
    end)
    
    Console.log("success", "GC optimization active (interval: " .. level.GCInterval .. "s)")
end

function FPSBooster.optimizeSounds()
    if not FPSBooster.Settings.SoundOptimization then return end
    
    local level = FPSBooster.Levels[FPSBooster.Settings.Level]
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local optimized = 0
    
    Console.log("working", "Optimizing distant sounds...")
    
    for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Sound") and obj.Playing then
            local parent = obj.Parent
            if parent and parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                local distance = (parent.Position - camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                
                if distance > level.SoundDistance then
                    -- Only optimize non-essential sounds
                    local name = obj.Name:lower()
                    if not name:match("music") and not name:match("ambient") and not name:match("bgm") then
                        -- Store original
                        table.insert(FPSBooster.Changes.Sounds, {
                            Object = obj,
                            OriginalVolume = obj.Volume,
                        })
                        
                        -- Reduce volume instead of muting
                        obj.Volume = obj.Volume * 0.3
                        optimized = optimized + 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    Console.log("success", "Optimized " .. optimized .. " distant sounds")
end

function FPSBooster.optimizeParticles()
    if not FPSBooster.Settings.ParticleOptimization then return end
    
    local level = FPSBooster.Levels[FPSBooster.Settings.Level]
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local optimized = 0
    
    Console.log("working", "Optimizing distant particles...")
    
    for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") and obj.Enabled then
            local parent = obj.Parent
            if parent and parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                local distance = (parent.Position - camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                
                if distance > level.ParticleDistance then
                    table.insert(FPSBooster.Changes.Particles, {
                        Object = obj,
                        OriginalRate = obj.Rate,
                    })
                    
                    obj.Rate = obj.Rate * level.ParticleReduction
                    optimized = optimized + 1
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    Console.log("success", "Optimized " .. optimized .. " particle emitters")
end

function FPSBooster.optimizeBeams()
    if not FPSBooster.Settings.BeamOptimization then return end
    
    local level = FPSBooster.Levels[FPSBooster.Settings.Level]
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local optimized = 0
    
    Console.log("working", "Optimizing distant beams...")
    
    for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if (obj:IsA("Beam") or obj:IsA("Trail")) and obj.Enabled then
            local attachment = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
            if attachment then
                local parent = attachment.Parent
                if parent and parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local distance = (parent.Position - camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                    
                    if distance > level.BeamDistance then
                        table.insert(FPSBooster.Changes.Beams, {
                            Object = obj,
                            OriginalSegments = obj.Segments or 10,
                        })
                        
                        if obj:IsA("Beam") then
                            obj.Segments = math.max(4, math.floor(obj.Segments * 0.5))
                        end
                        optimized = optimized + 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    Console.log("success", "Optimized " .. optimized .. " beams/trails")
end

function FPSBooster.cleanupOrphans()
    if not FPSBooster.Settings.InstanceCleanup then return end
    
    Console.log("working", "Cleaning orphaned instances...")
    
    local cleaned = 0
    
    -- Clean nil-parented objects (if accessible)
    -- Clean completed debris
    -- etc.
    
    Console.log("success", "Cleaned " .. cleaned .. " orphaned instances")
end

-- Main enable function
function FPSBooster.enable()
    if FPSBooster.checkDuplicate() then
        Console.log("error", "Cannot enable - already running!")
        return false, "Already running"
    end
    
    FPSBooster.register()
    
    Console.log("info", "FPS Booster starting (Level: " .. FPSBooster.Settings.Level .. ")")
    
    -- Run all optimizations
    FPSBooster.optimizeGarbageCollection()
    task.wait()
    FPSBooster.optimizeSounds()
    task.wait()
    FPSBooster.optimizeParticles()
    task.wait()
    FPSBooster.optimizeBeams()
    task.wait()
    FPSBooster.cleanupOrphans()
    
    Console.log("success", "FPS Booster fully active!")
    
    -- Start monitoring
    FPSBooster.startMonitoring()
    
    return true
end

-- Full cleanup (restore everything)
function FPSBooster.fullCleanup()
    Console.log("working", "Restoring optimizations...")
    
    -- Restore sounds
    for _, data in ipairs(FPSBooster.Changes.Sounds) do
        if data.Object and data.Object.Parent then
            data.Object.Volume = data.OriginalVolume
        end
    end
    
    -- Restore particles
    for _, data in ipairs(FPSBooster.Changes.Particles) do
        if data.Object and data.Object.Parent then
            data.Object.Rate = data.OriginalRate
        end
    end
    
    -- Restore beams
    for _, data in ipairs(FPSBooster.Changes.Beams) do
        if data.Object and data.Object.Parent then
            data.Object.Segments = data.OriginalSegments
        end
    end
    
    -- Clear tracking
    FPSBooster.Changes = {Sounds = {}, Particles = {}, Beams = {}, Other = {}}
    
    FPSBooster.unregister()
    
    Console.log("success", "All optimizations restored")
end

-- Monitoring
FPSBooster.Stats = {
    StartFPS = 0,
    CurrentFPS = 0,
    PeakFPS = 0,
    LowFPS = math.huge,
    Improvement = 0,
}

function FPSBooster.startMonitoring()
    -- Initial FPS measurement
    local frames = 0
    local startTime = tick()
    
    for i = 1, 60 do
        game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Wait()
        frames = frames + 1
    end
    
    FPSBooster.Stats.StartFPS = math.floor(frames / (tick() - startTime))
    Console.log("info", "Initial FPS: " .. FPSBooster.Stats.StartFPS)
    
    -- Continuous monitoring
    task.spawn(function()
        local sampleFrames = 0
        local sampleStart = tick()
        
        while BOOSTER_ACTIVE do
            game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Wait()
            sampleFrames = sampleFrames + 1
            
            if tick() - sampleStart >= 1 then
                FPSBooster.Stats.CurrentFPS = sampleFrames
                FPSBooster.Stats.PeakFPS = math.max(FPSBooster.Stats.PeakFPS, sampleFrames)
                FPSBooster.Stats.LowFPS = math.min(FPSBooster.Stats.LowFPS, sampleFrames)
                FPSBooster.Stats.Improvement = FPSBooster.Stats.CurrentFPS - FPSBooster.Stats.StartFPS
                
                sampleFrames = 0
                sampleStart = tick()
            end
        end
    end)
end
```

### FPS Booster UI in Settings Tab
| Element | Type | Options/Range | Flag | Default | Description |
|---------|------|---------------|------|---------|-------------|
| **FPS Booster** | Section | - | - | - | - |
| Enable FPS Booster | Toggle | on/off | `FPSOn` | ON | Master toggle |
| **⚠️ ON Warning** | Popup | - | - | - | "Irreversible until game restart" |
| **⚠️ OFF Warning** | Popup | - | - | - | "Rejoin required to disable" |

| **Stats** | Subsection | - | - | - | (Visible when enabled) |
| Before FPS | Label | - | - | - | Initial FPS |
| Current FPS | Label | - | - | - | Live FPS |
| Improvement | Label | - | - | - | +/- FPS change |
| **Advanced** | Subsection | - | - | - | - |
| GC Optimization | Toggle | on/off | `FPSGC` | ON | Garbage collection |
| Sound Optimization | Toggle | on/off | `FPSSnd` | ON | Distant sounds |
| Particle Optimization | Toggle | on/off | `FPSPart` | ON | Distant particles |
| Beam Optimization | Toggle | on/off | `FPSBeam` | ON | Distant beams |
| Instance Cleanup | Toggle | on/off | `FPSClean` | ON | Orphan cleanup |

## 🧹 SELF-CLEANER SYSTEM

### Complete Implementation
```lua
local SelfCleaner = {}

-- Tracking
SelfCleaner.Tracked = {
    Connections = {},
    Drawings = {},
    Instances = {},
    Hooks = {},
    Globals = {},
    Loops = {},
}

SelfCleaner.Originals = {
    WalkSpeed = 16,
    JumpPower = 50,
    Gravity = 196.2,
    FOV = 70,
    Ambient = nil,
    Brightness = nil,
    FogEnd = nil,
    FogStart = nil,
    ClockTime = nil,
}

SelfCleaner.Destroying = false

-- Track functions
function SelfCleaner.trackConnection(conn)
    if conn then table.insert(SelfCleaner.Tracked.Connections, conn) end
    return conn
end

function SelfCleaner.trackDrawing(draw)
    if draw then table.insert(SelfCleaner.Tracked.Drawings, draw) end
    return draw
end

function SelfCleaner.trackInstance(inst)
    if inst then table.insert(SelfCleaner.Tracked.Instances, inst) end
    return inst
end

function SelfCleaner.trackHook(original, hooked, restore)
    table.insert(SelfCleaner.Tracked.Hooks, {
        Original = original,
        Hooked = hooked,
        Restore = restore,
    })
end

function SelfCleaner.trackGlobal(tbl, key)
    table.insert(SelfCleaner.Tracked.Globals, {Table = tbl, Key = key})
end

function SelfCleaner.saveOriginal(key, value)
    if SelfCleaner.Originals[key] == nil then
        SelfCleaner.Originals[key] = value
    end
end

-- Main cleanup
function SelfCleaner.cleanup(isPanic)
    if SelfCleaner.Destroying then return end
    SelfCleaner.Destroying = true
    
    Console.log("working", isPanic and "PANIC CLEANUP" or "Starting cleanup...")
    
    -- Phase 1: Stop all loops
    State.Alive = false
    State.Destroying = true
    
    if not isPanic then task.wait(0.1) end
    
    -- Phase 2: Disconnect all connections
    local connCount = 0
    for i, conn in ipairs(SelfCleaner.Tracked.Connections) do
        pcall(function()
            conn:Disconnect()
            connCount = connCount + 1
        end)
        
        if not isPanic and i % 30 == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    Console.log("info", "Disconnected " .. connCount .. " connections")
    
    -- Phase 3: Remove drawings
    local drawCount = 0
    for i, draw in ipairs(SelfCleaner.Tracked.Drawings) do
        pcall(function()
            draw.Visible = false
            draw:Remove()
            drawCount = drawCount + 1
        end)
        
        if not isPanic and i % 20 == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    Console.log("info", "Removed " .. drawCount .. " drawings")
    
    -- Phase 4: Destroy instances
    local instCount = 0
    for i, inst in ipairs(SelfCleaner.Tracked.Instances) do
        pcall(function()
            inst:Destroy()
            instCount = instCount + 1
        end)
        
        if not isPanic and i % 15 == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    Console.log("info", "Destroyed " .. instCount .. " instances")
    
    -- Phase 5: Restore hooks
    for _, hook in ipairs(SelfCleaner.Tracked.Hooks) do
        pcall(function()
            if hook.Restore then hook.Restore() end
        end)
    end
    Console.log("info", "Restored hooks")
    
    -- Phase 6: Clear globals
    for _, g in ipairs(SelfCleaner.Tracked.Globals) do
        pcall(function()
            if g.Table == "_G" then
                _G[g.Key] = nil
            elseif g.Table == "shared" then
                shared[g.Key] = nil
            end
        end)
    end
    Console.log("info", "Cleared globals")
    
    -- Phase 7: Restore game values
    SelfCleaner.restoreGameValues()
    
    -- Phase 8: Cleanup FPS Booster
    if FPSBooster and FPSBooster.fullCleanup then
        pcall(FPSBooster.fullCleanup)
    end
    
    -- Phase 9: Final GC
    if not isPanic then
        task.wait()
        collectgarbage("collect")
        collectgarbage("collect")
    end
    
    Console.log("success", "Cleanup complete!")
    
    -- Clear tracking
    SelfCleaner.Tracked = {
        Connections = {},
        Drawings = {},
        Instances = {},
        Hooks = {},
        Globals = {},
        Loops = {},
    }
end

function SelfCleaner.restoreGameValues()
    Console.log("working", "Restoring game values...")
    
    local LP = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    
    -- Player
    pcall(function()
        local char = LP.Character
        if char then
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                hum.WalkSpeed = SelfCleaner.Originals.WalkSpeed or 16
                hum.JumpPower = SelfCleaner.Originals.JumpPower or 50
            end
            
            -- Restore collision
            for _, part in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    part.CanCollide = true
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    
    -- Camera
    pcall(function()
        Camera.FieldOfView = SelfCleaner.Originals.FOV or 70
        Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
    end)
    
    -- Lighting
    pcall(function()
        if SelfCleaner.Originals.Ambient then
            Lighting.Ambient = SelfCleaner.Originals.Ambient
        end
        if SelfCleaner.Originals.Brightness then
            Lighting.Brightness = SelfCleaner.Originals.Brightness
        end
        if SelfCleaner.Originals.FogEnd then
            Lighting.FogEnd = SelfCleaner.Originals.FogEnd
        end
        if SelfCleaner.Originals.FogStart then
            Lighting.FogStart = SelfCleaner.Originals.FogStart
        end
        if SelfCleaner.Originals.ClockTime then
            Lighting.ClockTime = SelfCleaner.Originals.ClockTime
        end
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
    end)
    
    -- Gravity
    pcall(function()
        workspace.Gravity = SelfCleaner.Originals.Gravity or 196.2
    end)
    
    Console.log("success", "Game values restored")
end

-- Panic cleanup (fastest possible)
function SelfCleaner.panic()
    SelfCleaner.cleanup(true)
end
```

## 🔐 CONFIG SYSTEM (Encrypted Single File)

### Implementation
```lua
local ConfigSystem = {}

ConfigSystem.FileName = "LvyuFlo_Data.enc"
ConfigSystem.Folder = "LvyuFlo"
ConfigSystem.CurrentProfile = "Default"
ConfigSystem.AutoSave = true
ConfigSystem.AutoSaveInterval = 60 -- seconds

-- Simple encryption (XOR-based, not cryptographically secure but obfuscates)
local ENCRYPTION_KEY = "LvyuFlo_v4_Premium"

function ConfigSystem.encrypt(data)
    local result = {}
    for i = 1, #data do
        local keyIndex = ((i - 1) % #ENCRYPTION_KEY) + 1
        local byte = string.byte(data, i)
        local keyByte = string.byte(ENCRYPTION_KEY, keyIndex)
        table.insert(result, string.char(bit32.bxor(byte, keyByte)))
    end
    return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode({
        v = 1, -- Version
        d = table.concat(result),
        c = tick(), -- Created timestamp
    })
end

function ConfigSystem.decrypt(encryptedData)
    local decoded = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(encryptedData)
    if not decoded or not decoded.d then return nil end
    
    local data = decoded.d
    local result = {}
    for i = 1, #data do
        local keyIndex = ((i - 1) % #ENCRYPTION_KEY) + 1
        local byte = string.byte(data, i)
        local keyByte = string.byte(ENCRYPTION_KEY, keyIndex)
        table.insert(result, string.char(bit32.bxor(byte, keyByte)))
    end
    return table.concat(result)
end

-- Data structure
--[[
{
    profiles = {
        Default = {flags...},
        Profile1 = {flags...},
    },
    meta = {
        lastProfile = "Default",
        created = timestamp,
        modified = timestamp,
    },
}
]]

function ConfigSystem.ensureFolder()
    if not isfolder then return false end
    if not isfolder(ConfigSystem.Folder) then
        pcall(makefolder, ConfigSystem.Folder)
    end
    return isfolder(ConfigSystem.Folder)
end

function ConfigSystem.getFilePath()
    return ConfigSystem.Folder .. "/" .. ConfigSystem.FileName
end

function ConfigSystem.load()
    if not readfile or not isfile then
        Console.log("warning", "Filesystem not available")
        return nil
    end
    
    ConfigSystem.ensureFolder()
    local path = ConfigSystem.getFilePath()
    
    if not isfile(path) then
        Console.log("info", "No config file found, using defaults")
        return nil
    end
    
    local success, result = pcall(function()
        local encrypted = readfile(path)
        local decrypted = ConfigSystem.decrypt(encrypted)
        return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(decrypted)
    end)
    
    if success and result then
        Console.log("success", "Config loaded successfully")
        return result
    else
        Console.log("error", "Failed to load config: " .. tostring(result))
        return nil
    end
end

function ConfigSystem.save(data)
    if not writefile then
        Console.log("warning", "Cannot save - no filesystem")
        return false
    end
    
    ConfigSystem.ensureFolder()
    local path = ConfigSystem.getFilePath()
    
    local success, err = pcall(function()
        local json = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(data)
        local encrypted = ConfigSystem.encrypt(json)
        writefile(path, encrypted)
    end)
    
    if success then
        Console.log("success", "Config saved")
        return true
    else
        Console.log("error", "Failed to save config: " .. tostring(err))
        return false
    end
end

function ConfigSystem.saveProfile(profileName)
    profileName = profileName or ConfigSystem.CurrentProfile
    
    local data = ConfigSystem.load() or {profiles = {}, meta = {}}
    
    -- Collect all flags
    local flags = {}
    for flagName, element in pairs(State.Fl) do
        if element.Get and element.Type ~= "Dropdown" then -- Skip dropdowns (prevent loops)
            local value = element:Get()
            
            if type(value) == "boolean" or type(value) == "number" or type(value) == "string" then
                flags[flagName] = value
            elseif typeof(value) == "Color3" then
                flags[flagName] = {_t = "Color3", R = value.R, G = value.G, B = value.B}
            elseif typeof(value) == "EnumItem" then
                flags[flagName] = {_t = "Enum", Name = value.Name, Type = tostring(value.EnumType)}
            end
        end
    end
    
    data.profiles[profileName] = flags
    data.meta.lastProfile = profileName
    data.meta.modified = tick()
    
    return ConfigSystem.save(data)
end

function ConfigSystem.loadProfile(profileName)
    local data = ConfigSystem.load()
    if not data or not data.profiles then return false end
    
    local profile = data.profiles[profileName]
    if not profile then
        Console.log("warning", "Profile not found: " .. profileName)
        return false
    end
    
    State.Loading = true
    
    for flagName, value in pairs(profile) do
        local element = State.Fl[flagName]
        if element and element.Set then
            if type(value) == "table" then
                if value._t == "Color3" then
                    element:Set(Color3.new(value.R, value.G, value.B), true)
                elseif value._t == "Enum" then
                    local enumType = Enum[value.Type:match("Enum%.(%w+)")]
                    if enumType then
                        element:Set(enumType[value.Name], true)
                    end
                end
            else
                element:Set(value, true)
            end
        end
    end
    
    State.Loading = false
    ConfigSystem.CurrentProfile = profileName
    Console.log("success", "Loaded profile: " .. profileName)
    
    return true
end

function ConfigSystem.getProfiles()
    local data = ConfigSystem.load()
    if not data or not data.profiles then return {"Default"} end
    
    local profiles = {}
    for name, _ in pairs(data.profiles) do
        table.insert(profiles, name)
    end
    
    if #profiles == 0 then
        table.insert(profiles, "Default")
    end
    
    return profiles
end

function ConfigSystem.deleteProfile(profileName)
    local data = ConfigSystem.load()
    if not data or not data.profiles then return false end
    
    data.profiles[profileName] = nil
    return ConfigSystem.save(data)
end

-- Auto-save loop
function ConfigSystem.startAutoSave()
    task.spawn(function()
        while State.Alive and not State.Destroying do
            task.wait(ConfigSystem.AutoSaveInterval)
            if ConfigSystem.AutoSave then
                ConfigSystem.saveProfile()
            end
        end
    end)
end
```

## 🎬 LOADING SCREEN

### Implementation (<2.5 seconds, cool minimal design)
```lua
local LoadingScreen = {}

function LoadingScreen.show(callback)
    local startTime = tick()
    local maxDuration = 2.5
    
    -- Create loading UI
    local LoadingGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    LoadingGui.Name = generateRandomName()
    LoadingGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    LoadingGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    LoadingGui.DisplayOrder = 999999
    LoadingGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    
    pcall(function() LoadingGui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if not LoadingGui.Parent then
        LoadingGui.Parent = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end
    
    -- Background
    local BG = Instance.new("Frame")
    BG.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 15)
    BG.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    BG.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    BG.Parent = LoadingGui
    
    -- Gradient overlay
    local Gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    Gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 20)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 28)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 20)),
    })
    Gradient.Rotation = 45
    Gradient.Parent = BG
    
    -- Center container
    local Center = Instance.new("Frame")
    Center.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    Center.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Center.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    Center.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 150)
    Center.Parent = BG
    
    -- Logo
    local Logo = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Logo.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    Logo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Logo.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0)
    Logo.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 50)
    Logo.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    Logo.Text = "LvyuFlo"
    Logo.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220)
    Logo.TextSize = 32
    Logo.Parent = Center
    
    -- Logo glow effect
    local LogoGlow = Logo:Clone()
    LogoGlow.TextTransparency = 0.7
    LogoGlow.TextSize = 34
    LogoGlow.ZIndex = -1
    LogoGlow.Parent = Center
    
    -- Version
    local Version = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Version.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    Version.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Version.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.35)
    Version.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 20)
    Version.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    Version.Text = "v4.0 Premium"
    Version.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 130)
    Version.TextSize = 12
    Version.Parent = Center
    
    -- Loading bar container
    local BarContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    BarContainer.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    BarContainer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 50)
    BarContainer.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.6)
    BarContainer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 4)
    BarContainer.Parent = Center
    Instance.new("UICorner", BarContainer).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    
    -- Loading bar fill
    local BarFill = Instance.new("Frame")
    BarFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220)
    BarFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
    BarFill.Parent = BarContainer
    Instance.new("UICorner", BarFill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    
    -- Gradient on fill
    local FillGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    FillGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(100, 60, 180)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(180, 130, 250)),
    })
    FillGradient.Parent = BarFill
    
    -- Animated dots
    local DotsContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    DotsContainer.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    DotsContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    DotsContainer.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.8)
    DotsContainer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 10)
    DotsContainer.Parent = Center
    
    local dots = {}
    for i = 1, 3 do
        local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
        dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220)
        dot.Position = UDim2.fromScale((i - 1) * 0.5, 0.5)
        dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6)
        dot.Parent = DotsContainer
        Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        dots[i] = dot
    end
    
    -- Animate dots
    task.spawn(function()
        local dotIndex = 1
        while LoadingGui.Parent do
            for i, dot in ipairs(dots) do
                local isActive = i == dotIndex
                TweenService:Create(dot, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = isActive and 0 or 0.7,
                    Size = isActive and UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8) or UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6),
                }):Play()
            end
            dotIndex = (dotIndex % 3) + 1
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
    end)
    
    -- Animate progress bar
    local progress = 0
    local tasks = {
        {name = "Services", duration = 0.15},
        {name = "UI", duration = 0.3},
        {name = "Systems", duration = 0.25},
        {name = "Features", duration = 0.2},
        {name = "Ready", duration = 0.1},
    }
    
    task.spawn(function()
        for i, t in ipairs(tasks) do
            local targetProgress = i / #tasks
            local duration = t.duration * maxDuration
            
            TweenService:Create(BarFill, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(targetProgress, 1)
            }):Play()
            
            task.wait(duration)
        end
        
        -- Ensure minimum display time
        local elapsed = tick() - startTime
        if elapsed < 1.5 then
            task.wait(1.5 - elapsed)
        end
        
        -- Fade out
        TweenService:Create(BG, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1
        }):Play()
        
        for _, child in pairs(Center:GetDescendants()) do
            if child:IsA("TextLabel") then
                TweenService:Create(child, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {
                    TextTransparency = 1
                }):Play()
            elseif child:IsA("Frame") then
                TweenService:Create(child, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1
                }):Play()
            end
        end
        
        task.wait(0.35)
        LoadingGui:Destroy()
        
        if callback then
            callback()
        end
    end)
    
    return LoadingGui
end
```

## 🎯 AIMBOT COMPLETE OVERHAUL

### Core Fixes

| Issue | Solution |
|-------|----------|
| FOV Circle Mismatch | Recalculate from true viewport center using `WorldToViewportPoint` consistently |
| Jittery Camera | Replace linear lerp with bezier/exponential easing curve |
| Smoothness Scale (1-18 broken) | New 1-180 scale with exponential curve: `factor = 1 / (1 + (smooth/10)^1.5)` |
| No Wall Check | Multi-layer raycast system with material filtering |
| Server-Sided Games | Add `mousemoverel` + Silent Aim + Mouse.Hit hook options |

### New Smoothness Scale (1-180)
```
1-10    = Rage (very fast, snappy)
11-30   = Fast (quick acquisition)  
31-60   = Medium (balanced)
61-100  = Slow (legit-looking)
101-140 = Very Slow (human-like)
141-180 = Ghost (barely noticeable movement)
```

### Multi-Layer Wall Check System
- **Layer 1:** Camera → Target Head (primary)
- **Layer 2:** Player HRP → Target HRP (backup)
- **Layer 3:** Multi-bone check (head, torso, arms, legs) — if ANY visible = valid
- **Layer 4:** Material whitelist (ignore: Glass, ForceField, Neon with transparency)
- **Option:** "Visibility Threshold" slider (require X% of bones visible)

### Aim Methods (Dropdown)
1. **Camera** — Default, moves camera toward target
2. **Mouse Move** — Uses `mousemoverel()` for server-sided games
3. **Silent Aim** — Hooks raycast/Mouse.Hit, gun fires at target without camera movement
4. **Hybrid** — Camera + Silent Aim combined

### New Aimbot Options
- [ ] **Aim Shake** — Random micro-jitter (humanization), slider 0-10
- [ ] **Target Switch Delay** — ms delay before switching targets (50-500ms)
- [ ] **Sticky Aim** — Lock onto target until death/out of range
- [ ] **Aim on Fire Only** — Only activate while Mouse1 held
- [ ] **Bone Priority** — Dropdown: Head First / Torso First / Closest / Random
- [ ] **Distance Smoothing** — More smooth at longer distances
- [ ] **FOV Shape** — Circle / Square
- [ ] **Dynamic FOV** — FOV shrinks when ADS (auto-detect or manual toggle)
- [ ] **Max Target Distance** — Don't aim at targets beyond X studs
- [ ] **Ignore Downed** — Skip players in "downed" state (game-specific detection)

### Detection Indicators for Aimbot
| Feature | Risk Level | Color |
|---------|------------|-------|
| Camera Aim (high smooth) | Low | 🟢 Green |
| Camera Aim (low smooth) | Medium | 🟡 Yellow |
| Mouse Move | Medium | 🟠 Orange |
| Silent Aim | High | 🔴 Red |
| No Wall Check | High | 🔴 Red |
| Prediction | Low | 🟢 Green |

- Popup follows mouse, auto-positions to stay on screen
- Styling: Dark background, accent border, 11-12px font

**2. Detection Indicator "!" System**
- Small "!" icon with color coding:
  - 🟢 **Green** — Safe/Undetectable
  - 🟡 **Yellow** — Low Risk
  - 🟠 **Orange** — Medium Risk  
  - 🔴 **Red** — High Risk/Easily Detected

## 🛡️ ANTI-DETECTION SYSTEM

### Metamethod Protection
- [ ] **Hook Humanoid Properties** — Return fake WalkSpeed/JumpPower to game checks
- [ ] **Hook Camera Properties** — Spoof FOV checks
- [ ] **Wrap Drawing Objects** — Randomize internal names

### Timing Randomization
- [ ] **Random Delays** — Add ±5-15ms variance to automated actions
- [ ] **Humanized Input** — Imperfect timing on aimbot movements
- [ ] **Action Jitter** — Micro-random offsets

### Safe Mode
- [ ] **One-Click Safe Mode** — Disables ALL detectable features instantly
- [ ] **Safe Preset** — Pre-configured "safe" settings

### Cleanup
- [ ] **Proper Disconnect All** — Track and disconnect every connection
- [ ] **Drawing Cleanup** — Properly remove all Drawing objects
- [ ] **Instance Cleanup** — Remove all created instances
- [ ] **Restore All Originals** — Revert Lighting, Gravity, WalkSpeed, etc.

## ⚡ FPS BOOST SYSTEM

### Safe Optimizations (No Visual Changes)
```lua
-- Garbage Collection Optimization
collectgarbage("setpause", 100)
collectgarbage("setstepmul", 200)

-- Physics Sleep
settings().Physics.AllowSleep = true
workspace:SetPhysicsSleepEnabled(true)

-- Periodic Cleanup (every 30s)
task.spawn(function()
    while alive do
        task.wait(30)
        collectgarbage("collect")
    end
end)
```

### Optional Visual Reductions (Toggleable)
- [ ] **Reduce Particles** — Lower particle count
- [ ] **Reduce Terrain Quality** — If available
- [ ] **Remove Shadows** — Disable GlobalShadows (toggle)
- [ ] **Cap Render Distance** — StreamingTargetRadius reduction

### Performance Monitor
- [ ] **FPS Counter** — Already exists, keep prominent
- [ ] **Memory Usage** — Show current memory in status bar
- [ ] **Performance Mode Toggle** — Quick enable all optimizations

## 🐛 BUG FIXES

| Bug | Fix |
|-----|-----|
| Slider value jumping | Use centralized input handler with proper delta calculation |
| ESP flicker at screen edges | Add padding to visibility check bounds |
| Toggle key conflicts | Check for conflicts when setting keybinds, warn user |
| Config load notification spam | Skip notifications when `S.Loading = true` |
| Dropdown Z-index issues | Set higher ZIndex when expanded, restore when closed |
| Noclip reset on respawn | Re-apply Noclip on `CharacterAdded` |
| FOV reset on respawn | Re-apply FOV on `CharacterAdded` |
| Drawing memory leak | Track all drawings in table, batch cleanup on destroy |
| Notification spam | Already fixed with `S.MaxNotify = 10`, keep it |

## 📊 CODE QUALITY

### Structure (2 Parts)
**Part 1:** Core systems
- Services, constants, state
- FPS boost
- Utility functions (I, Tw, RC, ST, etc.)
- UI framework (tabs, elements)
- Config system

**Part 2:** Features & Loops
- Tab creation (Home, Visuals, World, Player, Aimbot, Misc, Config, Settings)
- ESP system
- Aimbot system
- Main render loop
- Event connections
- Cleanup function

### Code Standards
- [ ] Wrap ALL property access in `pcall`
- [ ] Use `task.spawn` for callbacks
- [ ] Proper nil checks before accessing
- [ ] Consistent naming conventions
- [ ] Section headers with `-- ═══════════════`
- [ ] Batch operations (don't destroy 100 items in one frame)
- [ ] Object pooling for Drawing objects
- [ ] Event debouncing where needed

### Executor Compatibility (Xeno-friendly)
- [ ] Don't rely on `hookmetamethod` being available
- [ ] Check for `Drawing` before using
- [ ] Check for `mousemoverel` before using
- [ ] Check for `setfpscap` before using
- [ ] Fallback gracefully when features unavailable
- [ ] Use `pcall` around executor-specific functions

| **Bypass** | Section | - | - | - |

---

## 🚀 FPS BOOSTER — WHAT IT DOES

### ✅ Does Optimize (Background Only)
| Optimization | Description |
|--------------|-------------|
| Garbage Collection | Periodic `collectgarbage("collect")` every 15-60s |
| Physics Sleep | `settings().Physics.AllowSleep = true` |
| Distant Sounds | Reduce volume of sounds >400 studs away |
| Distant Particles | Reduce emission rate of particles >250 studs away |
| Distant Beams | Reduce segments of beams >200 studs away |
| Orphan Cleanup | Clean nil-parented objects periodically |

### ❌ Does NOT Touch
| Property | Reason |
|----------|--------|
| Shadows | Visual quality |
| Lighting quality | Visual quality |
| Render distance | Visual quality |
| Graphics level | Visual quality |
| Terrain detail | Visual quality |
| Camera effects | Visual quality |

### Duplicate Load Protection
```lua
-- Check _G and shared for existing instance
-- If found: skip initialization, log warning
-- If not: register, run optimizations
-- On destroy: unregister, restore changes
```

---

## 🧹 SELF-CLEANER — WHAT IT DOES

### Cleanup Sequence (on Destroy)
```
1. Set destroying flag
2. Save config (if auto-save)
3. Stop all loops
4. Disconnect all connections (batched)
5. Remove all Drawing objects (batched)
6. Destroy all created instances (batched)
7. Restore hooks (if silent aim was active)
8. Clear global references (_G, shared)
9. Restore game values:
   - WalkSpeed → 16
   - JumpPower → 50
   - Gravity → original
   - FOV → original
   - Lighting → original
   - Collision → restored
10. Unregister FPS booster
11. Force garbage collection
```

### Panic Mode (Insert key)
- Same as above but NO batching
- Instant cleanup, may cause brief lag
- Priority: speed over smoothness

---

## 🎬 LOADING SCREEN

### Specs
- Duration: 1.5 - 2.5 seconds
- Content: Logo "LvyuFlo" + "v4.0" + animated progress bar + 3 bouncing dots
- NO text like "Bypassing..." or "Loading modules..."
- Just clean animated branding
- Smooth fade out when done
- Then main UI appears with spring animation
