# LvyuFlo v4.0 — Part 1: Core & UI

```lua
--[[
    ╔═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
    ║                        LvyuFlo v4.0 — Part 1                       ║
    ║                         Core & UI System                            ║
    ║                     Premium Open Source Edition                      ║
    ║                                                                     ║
    ║  Sections:                                                          ║
    ║   1. Constants        7. Animation        13. Tooltip               ║
    ║   2. Services         8. FPS Booster      14. Confirm Dialog        ║
    ║   3. Console          9. Components       15. Config (Encrypted)    ║
    ║   4. Utilities       10. Window           16. Loading Screen        ║
    ║   5. State           11. Tabs                                       ║
    ║   6. Theme           12. Notifications                              ║
    ╚═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
]]

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 1: CONSTANTS & CONFIGURATION
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local VERSION = "4.0"
local SCRIPT_NAME = "LvyuFlo"
local CONFIG_FOLDER = "LvyuFlo"
local CONFIG_FILE = "LvyuFlo_Data.enc"
local ENCRYPTION_KEY = "LvyuFlo_v4_Premium_2025"
local BOOSTER_KEY = "_LvyuFlo_FPSBooster_v4"

local UI_CONSTANTS = {
    SIDEBAR_WIDTH = 160,
    TOPBAR_HEIGHT = 52,
    MIN_WIDTH = 640,
    MIN_HEIGHT = 460,
    CORNER_RADIUS = 8,
    ELEMENT_HEIGHT = 40,
    ELEMENT_HEIGHT_DESC = 54,
    SECTION_PAD = 6,
    ANIM_SPEED = 0.22,
    ANIM_SPEED_FAST = 0.12,
    ANIM_SPEED_SLOW = 0.35,
    SPRING_SPEED = 0.45,
    RIPPLE_DURATION = 0.5,
    TOOLTIP_DELAY = 0.5,
    MAX_NOTIFICATIONS = 8,
    NOTIFICATION_DURATION = 4,
    NOTIFICATION_WIDTH = 320,
}

local DEFAULT_KEYBINDS = {
    ToggleUI = Enum.KeyCode.RightControl,
    Panic = Enum.KeyCode.Insert,
}

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 2: SERVICES & EXECUTOR DETECTION
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
local Plrs = game:GetService("Players")
local Http = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Ltg = game:GetService("Lighting")
local GS = game:GetService("GuiService")
local LP = Plrs.LocalPlayer
local Cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
local Mse = LP:GetMouse()

local Ex = {
    Name = "Unknown",
    Drawing = false,
    FileSystem = false,
    Clipboard = false,
    MouseMoveRel = false,
    HookFunction = false,
    GetRawMeta = false,
    SetFPSCap = false,
}

pcall(function()
    if getexecutorname then
        Ex.Name = getexecutorname()
    elseif identifyexecutor then
        Ex.Name = identifyexecutor()
    end
end)

pcall(function() if Drawing and Drawing.new then Ex.Drawing = true end end)
pcall(function() if writefile and readfile and isfile then Ex.FileSystem = true end end)
pcall(function() if setclipboard then Ex.Clipboard = true end end)
pcall(function() if mousemoverel then Ex.MouseMoveRel = true end end)
pcall(function() if hookfunction then Ex.HookFunction = true end end)
pcall(function() if getrawmetatable then Ex.GetRawMeta = true end end)
pcall(function() if setfpscap then Ex.SetFPSCap = true end end)

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 3: CONSOLE SYSTEM (Runs before UI)
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local Console = {}
Console.Logs = {}
Console.MaxLogs = 300
Console.Visible = false
Console.UIUpdate = nil

Console.Types = {
    error = {Icon = "❌", Name = "ERROR", Color = Color3.fromRGB(240, 80, 80)},
    warning = {Icon = "⚠️", Name = "WARN", Color = Color3.fromRGB(250, 180, 50)},
    info = {Icon = "ℹ️", Name = "INFO", Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 180, 255)},
    success = {Icon = "✅", Name = "OK", Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 120)},
    working = {Icon = "⏳", Name = "WORK", Color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 255)},
}

function Console.log(logType, message, source)
    local entry = {
        Type = logType or "info",
        Message = tostring(message),
        Source = source or "System",
        Time = os.date("%H:%M:%S"),
        Tick = tick(),
    }
    table.insert(Console.Logs, 1, entry)
    while #Console.Logs > Console.MaxLogs do
        table.remove(Console.Logs)
    end
    if Console.UIUpdate then
        pcall(Console.UIUpdate)
    end
    return entry
end

function Console.clear()
    Console.Logs = {}
    if Console.UIUpdate then pcall(Console.UIUpdate) end
end

function Console.copyAll()
    if not Ex.Clipboard then return false end
    local lines = {"=== " .. SCRIPT_NAME .. " Console Log ===", "Time: " .. os.date(), ""}
    for _, log in ipairs(Console.Logs) do
        local t = Console.Types[log.Type] or Console.Types.info
        table.insert(lines, string.format("[%s] [%s] %s: %s", log.Time, t.Name, log.Source, log.Message))
    end
    pcall(setclipboard, table.concat(lines, "\n"))
    Console.log("success", "Log copied to clipboard")
    return true
end

function Console.runChecks()
    Console.log("working", "Running system checks...")
    local checks = {
        {"Drawing API", Ex.Drawing},
        {"File System", Ex.FileSystem},
        {"Clipboard", Ex.Clipboard},
        {"mousemoverel", Ex.MouseMoveRel},
        {"hookfunction", Ex.HookFunction},
        {"getrawmetatable", Ex.GetRawMeta},
        {"setfpscap", Ex.SetFPSCap},
    }
    for _, c in ipairs(checks) do
        if c[2] then
            Console.log("success", c[1] .. " available")
        else
            Console.log("warning", c[1] .. " not available")
        end
    end
    Console.log("info", "Executor: " .. Ex.Name)
    Console.log("success", "System checks complete")
end

Console.runChecks()

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 4: UTILITY FUNCTIONS
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local function RandomName()
    local chars = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"
    local name = ""
    for i = 1, math.random(8, 14) do
        local idx = math.random(1, #chars)
        name = name .. chars:sub(idx, idx)
    end
    return name .. tostring(math.random(100, 999))
end

local function Create(class, props, children)
    local obj = Instance.new(class)
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do
        if k ~= "Parent" then
            pcall(function() obj[k] = v end)
        end
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(children or {}) do
        child.Parent = obj
    end
    if props and props.Parent then
        obj.Parent = props.Parent
    end
    return obj
end

local function Tween(obj, goal, duration, style, direction)
    if not obj then return nil end
    local ok = pcall(function() return obj.Parent end)
    if not ok then return nil end
    duration = duration or UI_CONSTANTS.ANIM_SPEED
    style = style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint
    direction = direction or Enum.EasingDirection.Out
    local tw = TS:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(duration, style, direction), goal)
    tw:Play()
    return tw
end

local function AddCorner(parent, radius)
    return Create("UICorner", {
        CornerRadius = radius or UDim.new(0, UI_CONSTANTS.CORNER_RADIUS),
        Parent = parent,
    })
end

local function AddStroke(parent, color, thickness, transparency)
    return Create("UIStroke", {
        Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 60),
        Thickness = thickness or 1,
        Transparency = transparency or 0.5,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
        Parent = parent,
    })
end

local function AddPadding(parent, top, bottom, left, right)
    return Create("UIPadding", {
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, top or 0),
        PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, bottom or 0),
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, left or 0),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, right or 0),
        Parent = parent,
    })
end

local function AddListLayout(parent, padding, sortOrder)
    return Create("UIListLayout", {
        SortOrder = sortOrder or Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, padding or UI_CONSTANTS.SECTION_PAD),
        Parent = parent,
    })
end

local function FormatValue(value, increment)
    if increment >= 1 then
        return tostring(math.floor(value + 0.5))
    else
        local dp = math.max(0, math.ceil(-math.log10(increment)))
        return string.format("%." .. dp .. "f", value)
    end
end

local function Lerp(a, b, t)
    return a + (b - a) * t
end

local function GetViewportCenter()
    local viewport = Cam.ViewportSize
    local inset = GS:GetGuiInset()
    return Vector2.new(viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y / 2 + inset.Y / 2)
end

local function CloneColor(c3, alpha)
    return Color3.new(
        math.clamp(c3.R * (alpha or 1), 0, 1),
        math.clamp(c3.G * (alpha or 1), 0, 1),
        math.clamp(c3.B * (alpha or 1), 0, 1)
    )
end

local function HSVToColor(h, s, v)
    return Color3.fromHSV(h % 1, math.clamp(s, 0, 1), math.clamp(v, 0, 1))
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 5: STATE MANAGEMENT
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local State = {
    Alive = true,
    Destroying = false,
    Loading = false,
    Visible = true,
    Minimized = false,

    Tabs = {},
    Flags = {},
    Connections = {},
    Drawings = {},
    Instances = {},
    ActiveTab = nil,
    ActiveSlider = nil,

    ToggleKey = DEFAULT_KEYBINDS.ToggleUI,
    PanicKey = DEFAULT_KEYBINDS.Panic,

    NotifyCount = 0,
    NotifyPosition = "BR",

    SavedLocation = nil,
    ESP = {},
    ArmorData = nil,
    ArmorForce = false,

    Originals = {},

    InputState = {
        LeftClick = false,
        RightClick = false,
        MiddleClick = false,
        AimToggled = false,
    },
}

-- Save original values
pcall(function()
    State.Originals.Ambient = Ltg.Ambient
    State.Originals.Brightness = Ltg.Brightness
    State.Originals.FogEnd = Ltg.FogEnd
    State.Originals.FogStart = Ltg.FogStart
    State.Originals.ClockTime = Ltg.ClockTime
    State.Originals.GlobalShadows = Ltg.GlobalShadows
    State.Originals.FOV = Cam.FieldOfView
    State.Originals.Gravity = workspace.Gravity
end)

local function TrackConnection(conn)
    if conn then table.insert(State.Connections, conn) end
    return conn
end

local function TrackDrawing(draw)
    if draw then table.insert(State.Drawings, draw) end
    return draw
end

local function TrackInstance(inst)
    if inst then table.insert(State.Instances, inst) end
    return inst
end

local function NewDrawing(drawType)
    if not Ex.Drawing then return nil end
    local ok, obj = pcall(function() return Drawing.new(drawType) end)
    if ok and obj then
        return TrackDrawing(obj)
    end
    return nil
end

-- Input tracking
TrackConnection(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        State.InputState.LeftClick = true
    elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
        State.InputState.RightClick = true
    elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 then
        State.InputState.MiddleClick = true
    end
end))

TrackConnection(UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        State.InputState.LeftClick = false
        State.ActiveSlider = nil
    elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
        State.InputState.RightClick = false
    elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 then
        State.InputState.MiddleClick = false
    end
end))

-- Centralized slider movement handler
TrackConnection(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if State.ActiveSlider and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        if State.ActiveSlider.Update then
            State.ActiveSlider.Update(input)
        end
    end
end))

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 6: THEME SYSTEM
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local Theme = {
    BG = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 22),
    Side = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 30),
    Elem = Color3.fromRGB(23, 23, 38),
    ElemHover = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 50),
    Inner = Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 52),
    Accent = Color3.fromRGB(128, 90, 200),
    AccentHover = Color3.fromRGB(150, 112, 225),
    AccentDark = Color3.fromRGB(98, 60, 170),
    AccentLight = Color3.fromRGB(170, 140, 240),
    Text = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 244),
    SubText = Color3.fromRGB(142, 142, 165),
    DimText = Color3.fromRGB(85, 85, 110),
    Divider = Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 60),
    Good = Color3.fromRGB(67, 181, 129),
    Warn = Color3.fromRGB(250, 168, 26),
    Bad = Color3.fromRGB(237, 66, 69),
    Unstable = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 50),
    HealthHigh = Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 120),
    HealthMid = Color3.fromRGB(240, 180, 60),
    HealthLow = Color3.fromRGB(240, 80, 80),
    Armor = Color3.fromRGB(80, 160, 255),
    White = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
    Black = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
}

local function SetAccentColor(color)
    Theme.Accent = color
    local h, s, v = color:ToHSV()
    Theme.AccentHover = Color3.fromHSV(h, math.max(s - 0.1, 0), math.min(v + 0.1, 1))
    Theme.AccentDark = Color3.fromHSV(h, math.min(s + 0.1, 1), math.max(v - 0.15, 0))
    Theme.AccentLight = Color3.fromHSV(h, math.max(s - 0.25, 0), math.min(v + 0.2, 1))
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 7: ANIMATION SYSTEM
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local function Ripple(parent)
    if not parent or not parent.Parent then return end
    local circle = Create("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.White,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.78,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Parent = parent,
    })
    AddCorner(circle, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local maxSize = math.max(parent.AbsoluteSize.X, parent.AbsoluteSize.Y) * 2.5
    Tween(circle, {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(maxSize, maxSize),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    }, UI_CONSTANTS.RIPPLE_DURATION, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    task.delay(UI_CONSTANTS.RIPPLE_DURATION + 0.05, function()
        pcall(game.Destroy, circle)
    end)
end

local function SpringOpen(frame, targetSize, duration)
    duration = duration or UI_CONSTANTS.SPRING_SPEED
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0

    local tw1 = Tween(frame, {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
            targetSize.X.Offset * 1.05,
            targetSize.Y.Offset * 1.05
        ),
    }, duration * 0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
    if tw1 then tw1.Completed:Wait() end

    local tw2 = Tween(frame, {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
            targetSize.X.Offset * 0.98,
            targetSize.Y.Offset * 0.98
        ),
    }, duration * 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    if tw2 then tw2.Completed:Wait() end

    Tween(frame, {Size = targetSize}, duration * 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
end

local function SpringClose(frame, duration)
    duration = duration or UI_CONSTANTS.ANIM_SPEED_SLOW
    local currentSize = frame.Size

    local tw1 = Tween(frame, {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
            currentSize.X.Offset * 1.03,
            currentSize.Y.Offset * 1.03
        ),
    }, duration * 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    if tw1 then tw1.Completed:Wait() end

    local tw2 = Tween(frame, {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
    }, duration * 0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
    if tw2 then tw2.Completed:Wait() end
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 8: FPS BOOSTER (Runs immediately)
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local FPSBooster = {}
FPSBooster.Active = false
FPSBooster.Changes = {Sounds = {}, Particles = {}, Beams = {}}
FPSBooster.Stats = {StartFPS = 0, CurrentFPS = 0, Improvement = 0}

FPSBooster.Levels = {
    Conservative = {GCInterval = 60, SoundDist = 600, ParticleDist = 400, ParticleReduce = 0.7, BeamDist = 300},
    Moderate = {GCInterval = 30, SoundDist = 400, ParticleDist = 250, ParticleReduce = 0.5, BeamDist = 200},
    Aggressive = {GCInterval = 15, SoundDist = 200, ParticleDist = 150, ParticleReduce = 0.3, BeamDist = 100},
}
FPSBooster.CurrentLevel = "Moderate"

function FPSBooster.checkDuplicate()
    if _G[BOOSTER_KEY] or shared[BOOSTER_KEY] then
        Console.log("warning", "FPS Booster already running from previous load")
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function FPSBooster.register()
    _G[BOOSTER_KEY] = {active = true, time = tick(), cleanup = FPSBooster.cleanup}
    shared[BOOSTER_KEY] = true
    FPSBooster.Active = true
end

function FPSBooster.unregister()
    _G[BOOSTER_KEY] = nil
    shared[BOOSTER_KEY] = nil
    FPSBooster.Active = false
end

function FPSBooster.optimizeGC(level)
    pcall(function()
        collectgarbage("setpause", 100)
        collectgarbage("setstepmul", 200)
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while FPSBooster.Active and State.Alive do
            task.wait(level.GCInterval)
            for i = 1, 3 do
                collectgarbage("step", 80)
                task.wait()
            end
        end
    end)
    Console.log("success", "GC optimization active (interval: " .. level.GCInterval .. "s)")
end

function FPSBooster.optimizePhysics()
    pcall(function()
        settings().Physics.AllowSleep = true
    end)
    pcall(function()
        workspace:SetAttribute("PhysicsSleep", true)
    end)
    Console.log("success", "Physics sleep enabled")
end

function FPSBooster.optimizeSounds(level)
    local count = 0
    for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Sound") and obj.Playing then
            local parent = obj.Parent
            if parent and parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                local dist = (parent.Position - Cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                if dist > level.SoundDist then
                    local name = obj.Name:lower()
                    if not name:match("music") and not name:match("ambient") and not name:match("bgm") and not name:match("theme") then
                        table.insert(FPSBooster.Changes.Sounds, {Obj = obj, OrigVol = obj.Volume})
                        obj.Volume = obj.Volume * 0.2
                        count = count + 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if count > 0 then Console.log("success", "Optimized " .. count .. " distant sounds") end
end

function FPSBooster.optimizeParticles(level)
    local count = 0
    for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") and obj.Enabled then
            local parent = obj.Parent
            if parent and parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                local dist = (parent.Position - Cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                if dist > level.ParticleDist then
                    table.insert(FPSBooster.Changes.Particles, {Obj = obj, OrigRate = obj.Rate})
                    obj.Rate = math.max(1, obj.Rate * level.ParticleReduce)
                    count = count + 1
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if count > 0 then Console.log("success", "Optimized " .. count .. " distant particles") end
end

function FPSBooster.optimizeBeams(level)
    local count = 0
    for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Beam") then
            local a0 = obj.Attachment0
            if a0 and a0.Parent and a0.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                local dist = (a0.Parent.Position - Cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                if dist > level.BeamDist then
                    table.insert(FPSBooster.Changes.Beams, {Obj = obj, OrigSeg = obj.Segments})
                    obj.Segments = math.max(3, math.floor(obj.Segments * 0.5))
                    count = count + 1
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if count > 0 then Console.log("success", "Optimized " .. count .. " distant beams") end
end

function FPSBooster.measureFPS(samples)
    samples = samples or 30
    local frames = 0
    local startTime = tick()
    for i = 1, samples do
        RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
        frames = frames + 1
    end
    return math.floor(frames / (tick() - startTime))
end

function FPSBooster.startMonitoring()
    task.spawn(function()
        local sampleFrames, sampleStart = 0, tick()
        while FPSBooster.Active and State.Alive do
            RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
            sampleFrames = sampleFrames + 1
            if tick() - sampleStart >= 1 then
                FPSBooster.Stats.CurrentFPS = sampleFrames
                FPSBooster.Stats.Improvement = sampleFrames - FPSBooster.Stats.StartFPS
                sampleFrames = 0
                sampleStart = tick()
            end
        end
    end)
end

function FPSBooster.enable()
    if FPSBooster.checkDuplicate() then return false end

    Console.log("info", "FPS Booster starting (" .. FPSBooster.CurrentLevel .. ")")

    FPSBooster.Stats.StartFPS = FPSBooster.measureFPS(30)
    Console.log("info", "Initial FPS: " .. FPSBooster.Stats.StartFPS)

    FPSBooster.register()

    local level = FPSBooster.Levels[FPSBooster.CurrentLevel]
    FPSBooster.optimizeGC(level)
    task.wait()
    FPSBooster.optimizePhysics()
    task.wait()
    FPSBooster.optimizeSounds(level)
    task.wait()
    FPSBooster.optimizeParticles(level)
    task.wait()
    FPSBooster.optimizeBeams(level)

    FPSBooster.startMonitoring()
    Console.log("success", "FPS Booster active!")
    return true
end

function FPSBooster.cleanup()
    Console.log("working", "Restoring FPS Booster changes...")
    for _, d in ipairs(FPSBooster.Changes.Sounds) do
        pcall(function() if d.Obj and d.Obj.Parent then d.Obj.Volume = d.OrigVol end end)
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(FPSBooster.Changes.Particles) do
        pcall(function() if d.Obj and d.Obj.Parent then d.Obj.Rate = d.OrigRate end end)
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(FPSBooster.Changes.Beams) do
        pcall(function() if d.Obj and d.Obj.Parent then d.Obj.Segments = d.OrigSeg end end)
    end
    FPSBooster.Changes = {Sounds = {}, Particles = {}, Beams = {}}
    FPSBooster.unregister()
    Console.log("success", "FPS Booster restored")
end

-- Auto-enable FPS Booster on load
task.spawn(function()
    FPSBooster.enable()
end)

local NotifHolder = Create("Frame", {
    Name = "NotifHolder",
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Size = UDim2.new(0, UI_CONSTANTS.NOTIFICATION_WIDTH, 1, -40),
    Position = UDim2.new(1, -(UI_CONSTANTS.NOTIFICATION_WIDTH + 10), 1, -20),
    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
    Parent = ScreenGui,
})
Create("UIListLayout", {
    SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    Padding = UDim.new(0, 8),
    VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom,
    HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,
    Parent = NotifHolder,
})

local function UpdateNotifPosition()
    local pos = State.NotifyPosition or "BR"
    if pos == "BR" then
        NotifHolder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -(UI_CONSTANTS.NOTIFICATION_WIDTH + 10), 1, -20)
        NotifHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
    elseif pos == "BL" then
        NotifHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -20)
        NotifHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
    elseif pos == "TR" then
        NotifHolder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -(UI_CONSTANTS.NOTIFICATION_WIDTH + 10), 0, 20)
        NotifHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    elseif pos == "TL" then
        NotifHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 20)
        NotifHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    end
end
UpdateNotifPosition()

local function Notify(opts)
    if State.Destroying or State.Loading then return end
    if State.NotifyCount >= UI_CONSTANTS.MAX_NOTIFICATIONS then return end

    opts = opts or {}
    local title = opts.Title or ""
    local content = opts.Content or ""
    local duration = opts.Duration or UI_CONSTANTS.NOTIFICATION_DURATION
    local notifType = opts.Type or "Info"
    local accentColor = ({
        Info = Theme.Accent,
        Success = Theme.Good,
        Warning = Theme.Warn,
        Error = Theme.Bad,
    })[notifType] or Theme.Accent

    State.NotifyCount = State.NotifyCount + 1

    local notifFrame = Create("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.BG,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 72),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Parent = NotifHolder,
    })
    AddCorner(notifFrame, UDim.new(0, 10))
    AddStroke(notifFrame, accentColor, 1, 0.4)

    Create("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = accentColor,
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -8),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
        Parent = notifFrame,
    })

    Create("TextLabel", {
        Text = title,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        TextSize = 14,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 20),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 10),
        Parent = notifFrame,
    })

    Create("TextLabel", {
        Text = content,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
        TextSize = 12,
        TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextWrapped = true,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 28),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 32),
        Parent = notifFrame,
    })

    local progressBar = Create("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = accentColor,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -3),
        Parent = notifFrame,
    })

    notifFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, 60, 0, 0)
    Tween(notifFrame, {Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)}, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
    Tween(progressBar, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 3)}, duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)

    task.delay(duration, function()
        State.NotifyCount = math.max(0, State.NotifyCount - 1)
        if not notifFrame or not notifFrame.Parent then return end
        local tw = Tween(notifFrame, {Position = UDim2.new(1, 60, 0, 0)}, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        if tw then tw.Completed:Wait() end
        pcall(game.Destroy, notifFrame)
    end)
end

local function ShowTooltip(text, posX, posY)
    TooltipLabel.Text = text
    local screenSize = ScreenGui.AbsoluteSize
    local tipX = math.min(posX + 15, screenSize.X - 260)
    local tipY = math.min(posY + 15, screenSize.Y - 80)
    TooltipFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(tipX, tipY)
    TooltipFrame.Visible = true
end

local function HideTooltip()
    TooltipFrame.Visible = false
end

-- SECTION 12: CONFIRM DIALOG
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local function ConfirmDialog(title, message, onYes, onNo)
    if State.Destroying then return end

    local overlay = Create("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Black,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        ZIndex = 9000,
        Parent = ScreenGui,
    })
    Tween(overlay, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.4}, 0.25)

    local dialog = Create("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Side,
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        ZIndex = 9001,
        Parent = overlay,
    })
    AddCorner(dialog, UDim.new(0, 12))
    AddStroke(dialog, Theme.Accent, 2, 0.2)

    local shadow = Create("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Black,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.6,
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 20, 1, 20),
        ZIndex = 9000,
        Parent = dialog,
    })
    AddCorner(shadow, UDim.new(0, 16))

    local content = Create("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        ZIndex = 9002,
        Parent = dialog,
    })
    AddPadding(content, 20, 20, 20, 20)
    AddListLayout(content, 12)

    Create("TextLabel", {
        Text = title,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        TextSize = 16,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22),
        ZIndex = 9003,
        Parent = content,
    })

    Create("TextLabel", {
        Text = message,
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
        TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
        TextWrapped = true,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        ZIndex = 9003,
        Parent = content,
    })

    local buttonFrame = Create("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
        ZIndex = 9003,
        Parent = content,
    })

    local cancelBtn = Create("TextButton", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
        Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 1, 0),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
        Text = "Cancel",
        TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
        TextSize = 13,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        ZIndex = 9004,
        Parent = buttonFrame,
    })
    AddCorner(cancelBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))

    local confirmBtn = Create("TextButton", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.52, 0, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 1, 0),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
        Text = "Confirm",
        TextColor3 = Theme.White,
        TextSize = 13,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        ZIndex = 9004,
        Parent = buttonFrame,
    })
    AddCorner(confirmBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))

    cancelBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(cancelBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Divider}, 0.12) end)
    cancelBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(cancelBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner}, 0.12) end)
    confirmBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(confirmBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.AccentHover}, 0.12) end)
    confirmBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(confirmBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent}, 0.12) end)

    task.spawn(function() SpringOpen(dialog, UDim2.fromOffset(380, 0), 0.4) end)

    local closed = false
    local function closeDialog()
        if closed then return end
        closed = true
        task.spawn(function()
            Tween(overlay, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.25)
            SpringClose(dialog, 0.3)
            task.wait(0.35)
            pcall(game.Destroy, overlay)
        end)
    end

    cancelBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Ripple(cancelBtn)
        closeDialog()
        if onNo then task.spawn(onNo) end
    end)

    confirmBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Ripple(confirmBtn)
        closeDialog()
        if onYes then task.spawn(onYes) end
    end)

    overlay.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            local mPos = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
            local dPos = dialog.AbsolutePosition
            local dSize = dialog.AbsoluteSize
            if mPos.X < dPos.X or mPos.X > dPos.X + dSize.X or mPos.Y < dPos.Y or mPos.Y > dPos.Y + dSize.Y then
                closeDialog()
                if onNo then task.spawn(onNo) end
            end
        end
    end)
end

    Parent = Topbar,
})

local accentGrad = Create("UIGradient", {
    Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.AccentDark),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Theme.AccentHover),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.AccentDark),
    }),
    Parent = accentLine,
})

task.spawn(function()
    while accentGrad and accentGrad.Parent and State.Alive and not State.Destroying do
        Tween(accentGrad, {Offset = Vector2.new(1, 0)}, 2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
        task.wait(2)
        if accentGrad and State.Alive then accentGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0) end
    end
end)

        Tween(logoIcon, {TextTransparency = 0}, 1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)

-- Topbar buttons
local function MakeTopbarButton(text, xOffset, hoverColor)
    local btn = Create("TextButton", {
        Text = text,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        TextSize = 14,
        TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, xOffset, 0, 12),
        ZIndex = 4,
        Parent = Topbar,
    })
    AddCorner(btn, UDim.new(0, 6))
    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(btn, {BackgroundTransparency = 0, TextColor3 = hoverColor}, 0.12) end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(btn, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = Theme.SubText}, 0.12) end)
    return btn
end

local hideBtn = MakeTopbarButton("□", -40, Theme.Accent)
local miniBtn = MakeTopbarButton("—", -72, Theme.Warn)

-- FPS counter
local fpsLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
    Text = "--",
    Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
    TextSize = 11,
    TextColor3 = Theme.DimText,
    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
    BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
    BackgroundTransparency = 0.7,
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 28),
    Position = UDim2.new(1, -148, 0, 12),
    ZIndex = 4,
    Parent = Topbar,
})
AddCorner(fpsLabel, UDim.new(0, 6))

task.spawn(function()
    local lastTime, frameCount = tick(), 0
    while State.Alive and not State.Destroying do
        frameCount = frameCount + 1
        if tick() - lastTime >= 1 then
            local fps = math.floor(frameCount / (tick() - lastTime))
            if fpsLabel and fpsLabel.Parent then
                fpsLabel.Text = tostring(fps)
                fpsLabel.TextColor3 = fps > 50 and Theme.Good or fps > 30 and Theme.Warn or Theme.Bad
            end
            frameCount = 0
            lastTime = tick()
        end
        RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
end)

-- Drag system
local dragTarget = MainFrame.Position
local dragging = false
local dragStart, dragStartPos

Topbar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        dragging = true
        dragStart = input.Position
        dragStartPos = dragTarget
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)
    end
end)

TrackConnection(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        dragTarget = UDim2.new(
            dragStartPos.X.Scale, dragStartPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
            dragStartPos.Y.Scale, dragStartPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
        )
    end
end))

TrackConnection(RS.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not State.Alive or State.Destroying then return end
    local cx = MainFrame.Position.X.Offset
    local cy = MainFrame.Position.Y.Offset
    MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
        dragTarget.X.Scale, cx + (dragTarget.X.Offset - cx) * 0.12,
        dragTarget.Y.Scale, cy + (dragTarget.Y.Offset - cy) * 0.12
    )
end))

-- Minimize/Hide
miniBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    Ripple(miniBtn)
    State.Minimized = not State.Minimized
    if State.Minimized then
        Tween(MainFrame, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(WindowSize.X.Offset, UI_CONSTANTS.TOPBAR_HEIGHT)}, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        miniBtn.Text = "+"
    else
        MainFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
        task.spawn(function() SpringOpen(MainFrame, WindowSize, 0.45) end)
        miniBtn.Text = "—"
    end
end)

hideBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    Ripple(hideBtn)
    State.Visible = false
    task.spawn(function() SpringClose(MainFrame, 0.35) end)
end)

local function ToggleUI()
    State.Visible = not State.Visible
    task.spawn(function()
        if State.Visible then
            ScreenGui.Enabled = true
            MainFrame.Visible = true
            MainFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
            SpringOpen(MainFrame, State.Minimized and UDim2.fromOffset(WindowSize.X.Offset, UI_CONSTANTS.TOPBAR_HEIGHT) or WindowSize, 0.45)
        else
            SpringClose(MainFrame, 0.35)
        end
    end)
end

    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8),
    ZIndex = 3,
    Parent = Sidebar,
})
AddCorner(searchWrapper, UDim.new(0, 6))

local searchBox = Create("TextBox", {
    PlaceholderText = "🔍 Search...",
    PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.DimText,
    Text = "",
    Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
    TextSize = 12,
    TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,

    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, 0),
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0),
    ZIndex = 4,
    Parent = searchWrapper,
})

    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -82),
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 42),
    ScrollBarThickness = 2,
    ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent,
    ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.4,
    CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
    AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
    BorderSizePixel = 0,
    ZIndex = 3,
    Parent = Sidebar,
})
AddListLayout(tabScroll, 4)

    Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, -UI_CONSTANTS.TOPBAR_HEIGHT),
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, UI_CONSTANTS.TOPBAR_HEIGHT),
    BorderSizePixel = 0,
    ZIndex = 2,

})
```
