    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Size = UDim2.new(1, -SW - 1, 1, -UI_CONSTANTS.TOPBAR_HEIGHT),
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SW + 1, UI_CONSTANTS.TOPBAR_HEIGHT),
    ClipsDescendants = true,
    ZIndex = 2,
    Parent = MainFrame,
})

-- Global search handler
searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
    local query = searchBox.Text:lower()
    for _, tab in ipairs(State.Tabs) do
        if tab._contentFrame then
            for _, child in ipairs(tab._contentFrame:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Frame") then
                    local titleLabel = child:FindFirstChild("Title")
                    child.Visible = not titleLabel or query == "" or titleLabel.Text:lower():find(query, 1, true) ~= nil
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 15: TAB SYSTEM & COMPONENT LIBRARY
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local Library = {}

function Library:CreateTab(name, icon)
    local Tab = {_elements = {}, _order = 0}

    local tabButton = Create("TextButton", {
        Text = "",
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34),
        LayoutOrder = #State.Tabs + 1,
        ZIndex = 4,
        Parent = tabScroll,
    })
    AddCorner(tabButton, UDim.new(0, 6))

    local indicator = Create("Frame", {
        Name = "Ind",
        BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = 5,
        Parent = tabButton,
    })
    AddCorner(indicator, UDim.new(0, 2))

    local iconLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
        Text = icon or "📁",
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
        TextSize = 15,
        TextColor3 = Theme.DimText,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 34),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0),
        ZIndex = 5,
        Parent = tabButton,
    })

    local nameLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
        Text = name,
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
        TextSize = 13,
        TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 0),
        ZIndex = 5,
        Parent = tabButton,
    })

    local contentFrame = Create("ScrollingFrame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        ScrollBarThickness = 3,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent,
        ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.3,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 3,
        Parent = ContentArea,
    })
    AddListLayout(contentFrame, 6)
    AddPadding(contentFrame, 10, 10, 10, 10)

    Tab._contentFrame = contentFrame
    Tab._button = tabButton

    local function ActivateTab()
        for _, otherTab in ipairs(State.Tabs) do
            if otherTab ~= Tab then
                otherTab._contentFrame.Visible = false
                Tween(otherTab._button, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.2)
                local otherInd = otherTab._button:FindFirstChild("Ind")
                if otherInd then Tween(otherInd, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 0)}, 0.2) end
                for _, ch in ipairs(otherTab._button:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then
                        Tween(ch, {TextColor3 = ch.TextSize >= 15 and Theme.DimText or Theme.SubText}, 0.2)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        contentFrame.Visible = true
        contentFrame.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
        Tween(tabButton, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.88}, 0.2)
        Tween(indicator, {Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 18)}, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
        Tween(iconLabel, {TextColor3 = Theme.Accent}, 0.2)
        Tween(nameLabel, {TextColor3 = Theme.Text}, 0.2)
        State.ActiveTab = Tab
    end

    tabButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() ActivateTab(); Ripple(tabButton) end)
    tabButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if State.ActiveTab ~= Tab then Tween(tabButton, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.92}, 0.12) end end)
    tabButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if State.ActiveTab ~= Tab then Tween(tabButton, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.12) end end)

    if #State.Tabs == 0 then task.defer(ActivateTab) end
    table.insert(State.Tabs, Tab)

    local function GetParent(config)
        return (config and config.Section and config.Section._frame) or contentFrame
    end

    local function NextOrder()
        Tab._order = Tab._order + 1
        return Tab._order
    end

    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- Component: Section
    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function Tab:CreateSection(config)
        config = config or {}
        local section = {}
        local sectionFrame = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            LayoutOrder = NextOrder(),
            Parent = contentFrame,
        })

        Create("TextLabel", {
            Text = "◆ " .. (config.Name or "SECTION"):upper(),
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            TextSize = 10,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Accent,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0),
            Parent = sectionFrame,
        })

        Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Divider,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 19),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Parent = sectionFrame,
        })

        local sectionContent = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            Parent = sectionFrame,
        })
        AddListLayout(sectionContent, 6)

        section._frame = sectionContent
        return section
    end

    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- Component: Divider
    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function Tab:CreateDivider()
        Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Divider,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 1),
            LayoutOrder = NextOrder(),
            Parent = contentFrame,
        })
    end

    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- Component: Label
    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function Tab:CreateLabel(config)
        config = config or {}
        local labelFrame = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22),
            LayoutOrder = NextOrder(),
            Parent = GetParent(config),
        })
        local label = Create("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Title",
            Text = config.Text or "",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = config.Color or Theme.SubText,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0),
            Parent = labelFrame,
        })
        return {
            Frame = labelFrame,
            Set = function(_, text, color)
                label.Text = text
                if color then label.TextColor3 = color end
            end,
        }
    end

    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- Component: ColorInfo
    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function Tab:CreateColorInfo(text, color)
        local frame = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38),
            LayoutOrder = NextOrder(),
            Parent = GetParent({}),
        })
        AddCorner(frame)
        AddStroke(frame, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.6)

        Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = color or Theme.Accent,
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -8),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
            Parent = frame,
        })

        local lbl = Create("TextLabel", {
            Text = text,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
            TextSize = 12,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 0),
            Parent = frame,
        })

        return {
            Frame = frame,
            Set = function(_, newText) lbl.Text = newText end,
        }
    end

    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- Component: Button
    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function Tab:CreateButton(config)
        config = config or {}
        local callback = config.Callback or function() end
        local desc = config.Description
        local h = desc and UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT_DESC or 38

        local frame = Create("Frame", {
            Name = config.Name or "Button",
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h),
            LayoutOrder = NextOrder(),
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            Parent = GetParent(config),
        })
        AddCorner(frame)
        AddStroke(frame, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.6)

        Create("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Title",
            Text = config.Name or "Button",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 14,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 18),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, desc and 6 or 10),
            Parent = frame,
        })

        if desc then
            Create("TextLabel", {
                Text = desc,
                Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
                TextSize = 11,
                TextColor3 = Theme.DimText,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 14),
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 26),
                Parent = frame,
            })
        end

        Create("TextLabel", {
            Text = "→",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            TextSize = 18,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Accent,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, h),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0),
            Parent = frame,
        })

        local btn = Create("TextButton", {
            Text = "",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            ZIndex = 2,
            Parent = frame,
        })

        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ElemHover}, 0.12) end)
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem}, 0.12) end)
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Ripple(frame); task.spawn(callback) end)

        return {
            Frame = frame,
            Set = function(_, newName)
                local t = frame:FindFirstChild("Title")
                if t then t.Text = newName end
            end,
        }
    end

    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- Component: Toggle
    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function Tab:CreateToggle(config)
        config = config or {}
        local callback = config.Callback or function() end
        local flag = config.Flag or config.Name or "Toggle"
        local enabled = config.Default or false
        local desc = config.Description
        local tooltip = config.Tooltip
        local unstable = config.Unstable
        local h = desc and UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT_DESC or UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT

        local frame = Create("Frame", {
            Name = config.Name or "Toggle",
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h),
            LayoutOrder = NextOrder(),
            Parent = GetParent(config),
        })
        AddCorner(frame)
        AddStroke(frame, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.6)

        local titleText = config.Name or "Toggle"
        if unstable then titleText = "⚠️ " .. titleText end

        Create("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Title",
            Text = titleText,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 14,
            TextColor3 = unstable and Theme.Unstable or Theme.Text,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 18),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, desc and 6 or 11),
            Parent = frame,
        })

        if desc then
            Create("TextLabel", {
                Text = desc,
                Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
                TextSize = 11,
                TextColor3 = Theme.DimText,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 14),
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 26),
                Parent = frame,
            })
        end

        -- Tooltip "?" button
        if tooltip then
            local tipBtn = Create("TextButton", {
                BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0.5, -9),
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                Text = "?",
                TextColor3 = Theme.DimText,
                TextSize = 10,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                ZIndex = 5,
                Parent = frame,
            })
            AddCorner(tipBtn, UDim.new(1, 0))

            local hovering = false
            tipBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                hovering = true
                Tween(tipBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent, TextColor3 = Theme.White}, 0.15)
                task.spawn(function()
                    local start = tick()
                    while hovering and State.Alive do
                        if tick() - start >= UI_CONSTANTS.TOOLTIP_DELAY then
                            ShowTooltip(tooltip, tipBtn.AbsolutePosition.X, tipBtn.AbsolutePosition.Y)
                            break
                        end
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                end)
            end)
            tipBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                hovering = false
                HideTooltip()
                Tween(tipBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner, TextColor3 = Theme.DimText}, 0.15)
            end)
        end

        -- Switch
        local switchBg = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 22),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, 0),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            ZIndex = 2,
            Parent = frame,
        })
        AddCorner(switchBg, UDim.new(1, 0))

        local knob = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SubText,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            ZIndex = 3,
            Parent = switchBg,
        })
        AddCorner(knob, UDim.new(1, 0))

        local function UpdateToggle(value, skipCallback)
            enabled = value
            if enabled then
                Tween(knob, {Position = UDim2.new(1, -19, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.White}, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                Tween(switchBg, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent}, 0.15)
            else
                Tween(knob, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SubText}, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                Tween(switchBg, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner}, 0.15)
            end
            if not skipCallback then
                task.spawn(function() pcall(callback, enabled) end)
            end
        end

        local clickBtn = Create("TextButton", {
            Text = "",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            ZIndex = 4,
            Parent = frame,
        })

        clickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() UpdateToggle(not enabled) end)

        -- Right-click reset
        clickBtn.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
            UpdateToggle(config.Default or false)
        end)

        frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ElemHover}, 0.12) end)
        frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem}, 0.12) end)

        if config.Default then UpdateToggle(true, true) end

        local obj = {
            Frame = frame,
            Type = "Toggle",
            Flag = flag,
            Default = config.Default or false,
            Set = function(_, v, skipCb) UpdateToggle(v, skipCb) end,
            Get = function() return enabled end,
        }
        State.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- Component: Slider
    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function Tab:CreateSlider(config)
        config = config or {}
        local callback = config.Callback or function() end
        local flag = config.Flag or config.Name or "Slider"
        local min = config.Min or 0
        local max = config.Max or 100
        local increment = config.Increment or 1
        local suffix = config.Suffix or ""
        local desc = config.Description
        local h = desc and 64 or 54

        local value = math.clamp(config.Default or min, min, max)

        local frame = Create("Frame", {
            Name = config.Name or "Slider",
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h),
            LayoutOrder = NextOrder(),
            Parent = GetParent(config),
        })
        AddCorner(frame)
        AddStroke(frame, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.6)

        Create("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Title",
            Text = config.Name or "Slider",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 14,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 18),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, desc and 6 or 8),
            Parent = frame,
        })

        if desc then
            Create("TextLabel", {
                Text = desc,
                Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
                TextSize = 11,
                TextColor3 = Theme.DimText,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 14),
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 24),
                Parent = frame,
            })
        end

        local valueLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
            Text = FormatValue(value, increment) .. suffix,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Accent,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(0.4, -14, 0, 18),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, desc and 6 or 8),
            Parent = frame,
        })

        local trackY = desc and 44 or 34
        local track = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 8),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, trackY),
            ZIndex = 2,
            Parent = frame,
        })
        AddCorner(track, UDim.new(1, 0))

        local pct = (value - min) / (max - min)
        local fill = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent,
            Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0),
            ZIndex = 3,
            Parent = track,
        })
        AddCorner(fill, UDim.new(1, 0))
        Create("UIGradient", {
            Color = ColorSequence.new(Theme.AccentDark, Theme.Accent),
            Parent = fill,
        })

        local sliderKnob = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.White,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
            Position = UDim2.new(pct, -8, 0.5, 0),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            ZIndex = 4,
            Parent = track,
        })
        AddCorner(sliderKnob, UDim.new(1, 0))

        local sliderData = {}
        sliderData.Update = function(input)
            if not track or not track.Parent then return end
            local trackPos = track.AbsolutePosition
            local trackSize = track.AbsoluteSize
            if trackSize.X <= 0 then return end
            local rx = math.clamp((input.Position.X - trackPos.X) / trackSize.X, 0, 1)
            value = math.floor((min + (max - min) * rx) / increment + 0.5) * increment
            value = math.clamp(value, min, max)
            local p = (value - min) / (max - min)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(p, 0, 1, 0)
            sliderKnob.Position = UDim2.new(p, -8, 0.5, 0)
            valueLabel.Text = FormatValue(value, increment) .. suffix
            task.spawn(function() pcall(callback, value) end)
        end

        local clickArea = Create("TextButton", {
            Text = "",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, 24),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-8, -8),
            ZIndex = 5,
            Parent = track,
        })

        clickArea.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                State.ActiveSlider = sliderData
                sliderData.Update(input)
            end
        end)

        -- Right-click reset
        clickArea.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
            local defaultVal = config.Default or min
            value = defaultVal
            local p = (value - min) / (max - min)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(p, 0, 1, 0)
            sliderKnob.Position = UDim2.new(p, -8, 0.5, 0)
            valueLabel.Text = FormatValue(value, increment) .. suffix
            task.spawn(function() pcall(callback, value) end)
        end)

        frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ElemHover}, 0.12) end)
        frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem}, 0.12) end)

        local obj = {
            Frame = frame,
            Type = "Slider",
            Flag = flag,
            Default = config.Default or min,
            Set = function(_, v, skipCb)
                value = math.clamp(v, min, max)
                local p = (value - min) / (max - min)
                fill.Size = UDim2.new(p, 0, 1, 0)
                sliderKnob.Position = UDim2.new(p, -8, 0.5, 0)
                valueLabel.Text = FormatValue(value, increment) .. suffix
                if not skipCb then task.spawn(function() pcall(callback, value) end) end
            end,
            Get = function() return value end,
        }
        State.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- Component: Dropdown
    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function Tab:CreateDropdown(config)
        config = config or {}
        local callback = config.Callback or function() end
        local flag = config.Flag or config.Name or "Dropdown"
        local options = config.Options or {}
        local selected = config.Default or (options[1] or "")
        local isOpen = false

        local frame = Create("Frame", {
            Name = config.Name or "Dropdown",
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT),
            LayoutOrder = NextOrder(),
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            ZIndex = 3,
            Parent = GetParent(config),
        })
        AddCorner(frame)
        AddStroke(frame, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.6)

        Create("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Title",
            Text = config.Name or "Dropdown",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 14,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0),
            ZIndex = 4,
            Parent = frame,
        })

        local selectedLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
            Text = tostring(selected),
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            TextSize = 12,
            TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(0.45, -40, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 0, 0),
            ZIndex = 4,
            Parent = frame,
        })

        local arrow = Create("TextLabel", {
            Text = "▼",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            TextSize = 10,
            TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 0),
            ZIndex = 4,
            Parent = frame,
        })

        local optionsWrapper = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 0),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 46),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            ZIndex = 5,
            Parent = frame,
        })
        local optionsLayout = AddListLayout(optionsWrapper, 3)

        local function MakeOption(name)
            local isSelected = selected == name
            local optBtn = Create("TextButton", {
                Name = name,
                Text = "",
                BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30),
                ZIndex = 6,
                Parent = optionsWrapper,
            })
            AddCorner(optBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))

            local check = Create("Frame", {
                Name = "Check",
                BackgroundColor3 = isSelected and Theme.Accent or Theme.Inner,
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, 0),
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
                ZIndex = 7,
                Parent = optBtn,
            })
            AddCorner(check, UDim.new(1, 0))
            AddStroke(check, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.5)

            local checkMark = Create("TextLabel", {
                Text = "✓",
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                TextSize = 10,
                TextColor3 = Theme.White,
                TextTransparency = isSelected and 0 or 1,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
                ZIndex = 8,
                Parent = check,
            })

            Create("TextLabel", {
                Text = name,
                Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
                TextSize = 12,
                TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 0),
                ZIndex = 7,
                Parent = optBtn,
            })

            optBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(optBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Divider}, 0.1) end)
            optBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(optBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner}, 0.1) end)

            optBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                selected = name
                selectedLabel.Text = name
                for _, child in ipairs(optionsWrapper:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local ck = child:FindFirstChild("Check")
                        if ck then
                            local cm = ck:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                            ck.BackgroundColor3 = child.Name == name and Theme.Accent or Theme.Inner
                            if cm then cm.TextTransparency = child.Name == name and 0 or 1 end
                        end
                    end
                end
                task.spawn(function() pcall(callback, selected) end)
            end)
        end

        for _, opt in ipairs(options) do MakeOption(opt) end

        local toggleBtn = Create("TextButton", {
            Text = "",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT),
            ZIndex = 5,
            Parent = frame,
        })

        toggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            isOpen = not isOpen
            if isOpen then
                frame.ClipsDescendants = false
                Tween(frame, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48 + optionsLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10)}, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                Tween(arrow, {Rotation = 180}, 0.2)
            else
                Tween(frame, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT)}, 0.22)
                Tween(arrow, {Rotation = 0}, 0.2)
                task.delay(0.25, function() if frame then frame.ClipsDescendants = true end end)
            end
        end)

        toggleBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ElemHover}, 0.12) end)
        toggleBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem}, 0.12) end)

        -- Right-click reset
        toggleBtn.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
            local defaultVal = config.Default or (options[1] or "")
            selected = defaultVal
            selectedLabel.Text = defaultVal
            for _, child in ipairs(optionsWrapper:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("TextButton") then
                    local ck = child:FindFirstChild("Check")
                    if ck then
                        local cm = ck:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                        ck.BackgroundColor3 = child.Name == defaultVal and Theme.Accent or Theme.Inner
                        if cm then cm.TextTransparency = child.Name == defaultVal and 0 or 1 end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        local obj = {
            Frame = frame,
            Type = "Dropdown",
            Flag = flag,
            Default = config.Default or (options[1] or ""),
            Set = function(_, v, skipCb)
                selected = v
                selectedLabel.Text = v
                for _, child in ipairs(optionsWrapper:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local ck = child:FindFirstChild("Check")
                        if ck then
                            local cm = ck:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                            ck.BackgroundColor3 = child.Name == v and Theme.Accent or Theme.Inner
                            if cm then cm.TextTransparency = child.Name == v and 0 or 1 end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if not skipCb then task.spawn(function() pcall(callback, selected) end) end
            end,
            Get = function() return selected end,
            Refresh = function(_, newOptions)
                for _, child in ipairs(optionsWrapper:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("TextButton") then child:Destroy() end
                end
                options = newOptions
                for _, opt in ipairs(options) do MakeOption(opt) end
            end,
        }
        State.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- Component: Input
    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function Tab:CreateInput(config)
        config = config or {}
        local callback = config.Callback or function() end
        local flag = config.Flag or config.Name or "Input"

        local frame = Create("Frame", {
            Name = config.Name or "Input",
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT),
            LayoutOrder = NextOrder(),
            Parent = GetParent(config),
        })
        AddCorner(frame)
        AddStroke(frame, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.6)

        Create("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Title",
            Text = config.Name or "Input",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 14,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0),
            Parent = frame,
        })

        local boxWrapper = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
            Size = UDim2.new(0.52, -12, 0, 26),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 0.5, 0),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            ZIndex = 2,
            Parent = frame,
        })
        AddCorner(boxWrapper, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local boxStroke = AddStroke(boxWrapper, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.6)

        local textBox = Create("TextBox", {
            PlaceholderText = config.Placeholder or "Type...",
            PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.DimText,
            Text = config.Default or "",
            Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 0),
            ZIndex = 3,
            Parent = boxWrapper,
        })

        textBox.Focused:Connect(function()
            Tween(boxStroke, {Color = Theme.Accent, Transparency = 0.2}, 0.15)
        end)
        textBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            Tween(boxStroke, {Color = Theme.Divider, Transparency = 0.6}, 0.15)
            task.spawn(function() pcall(callback, textBox.Text) end)
        end)

        frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ElemHover}, 0.12) end)
        frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem}, 0.12) end)

        local obj = {
            Frame = frame,
            Type = "Input",
            Flag = flag,
            Default = config.Default or "",
            Set = function(_, v, skipCb)
                textBox.Text = tostring(v)
                if not skipCb then task.spawn(function() pcall(callback, v) end) end
            end,
            Get = function() return textBox.Text end,
        }
        State.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- Component: Keybind
    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function Tab:CreateKeybind(config)
        config = config or {}
        local callback = config.Callback or function() end
        local flag = config.Flag or config.Name or "Keybind"
        local key = config.Default or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
        local listening = false
        local noCallback = config.NoCallback

        local frame = Create("Frame", {
            Name = config.Name or "Keybind",
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT),
            LayoutOrder = NextOrder(),
            Parent = GetParent(config),
        })
        AddCorner(frame)
        AddStroke(frame, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.6)

        Create("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Title",
            Text = config.Name or "Keybind",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 14,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0),
            Parent = frame,
        })

        local keyButton = Create("TextButton", {
            Text = key == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "None" or key.Name,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 12,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Accent,
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 24),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -82, 0.5, 0),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            ZIndex = 2,
            Parent = frame,
        })
        AddCorner(keyButton, UDim.new(0, 6))
        AddStroke(keyButton, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.5)

        keyButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            listening = true
            keyButton.Text = "..."
            Tween(keyButton, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent, TextColor3 = Theme.White}, 0.12)
        end)

        -- Right-click to clear
        keyButton.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
            key = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
            keyButton.Text = "None"
            Tween(keyButton, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner, TextColor3 = Theme.Accent}, 0.12)
        end)

        TrackConnection(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
            if listening then
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                        key = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
                        keyButton.Text = "None"
                    else
                        key = input.KeyCode
                        keyButton.Text = key.Name
                    end
                    listening = false
                    Tween(keyButton, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner, TextColor3 = Theme.Accent}, 0.12)
                    if config.OnChanged then task.spawn(function() pcall(config.OnChanged, key) end) end
                end
            elseif not noCallback and not gameProcessed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                if input.KeyCode == key and key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
                    task.spawn(callback)
                end
            end
        end))

        frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ElemHover}, 0.12) end)
        frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem}, 0.12) end)

        local obj = {
            Frame = frame,
            Type = "Keybind",
            Flag = flag,
            Default = config.Default or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown,
            Set = function(_, k)
                key = k
                keyButton.Text = k == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "None" or k.Name
            end,
            Get = function() return key end,
        }
        State.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    -- Component: ColorPicker
    -- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function Tab:CreateColorPicker(config)
        config = config or {}
        local callback = config.Callback or function() end
        local flag = config.Flag or config.Name or "Color"
        local color = config.Default or Theme.Accent
        local isOpen = false

        local frame = Create("Frame", {
            Name = config.Name or "Color",
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT),
            LayoutOrder = NextOrder(),
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            Parent = GetParent(config),
        })
        AddCorner(frame)
        AddStroke(frame, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.6)

        Create("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Title",
            Text = config.Name or "Color",
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 14,
            TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0),
            ZIndex = 4,
            Parent = frame,
        })

        local preview = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = color,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 20),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0.5, 0),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            ZIndex = 4,
            Parent = frame,
        })
        AddCorner(preview, UDim.new(0, 4))
        AddStroke(preview, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.3)

        -- HSV Sliders (simple approach)
        local h, s, v = color:ToHSV()

        local slidersFrame = Create("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 80),
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 46),
            ZIndex = 5,
            Parent = frame,
        })
        AddListLayout(slidersFrame, 4)

        local function MakeColorSlider(label, initialVal, onChange)
            local sliderFrame = Create("Frame", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22),
                ZIndex = 6,
                Parent = slidersFrame,
            })

            Create("TextLabel", {
                Text = label,
                Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
                TextSize = 11,
                TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 22),
                ZIndex = 7,
                Parent = sliderFrame,
            })

            local slTrack = Create("Frame", {
                BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 8),
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 7),
                ZIndex = 6,
                Parent = sliderFrame,
            })
            AddCorner(slTrack, UDim.new(1, 0))

            local slFill = Create("Frame", {
                BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent,
                Size = UDim2.new(initialVal, 0, 1, 0),
                ZIndex = 7,
                Parent = slTrack,
            })
            AddCorner(slFill, UDim.new(1, 0))

            local slKnob = Create("Frame", {
                BackgroundColor3 = Theme.White,
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12),
                Position = UDim2.new(initialVal, -6, 0.5, 0),
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
                ZIndex = 8,
                Parent = slTrack,
            })
            AddCorner(slKnob, UDim.new(1, 0))

            local slData = {}
            slData.Update = function(input)
                local pos = slTrack.AbsolutePosition
                local size = slTrack.AbsoluteSize
                if size.X <= 0 then return end
                local rx = math.clamp((input.Position.X - pos.X) / size.X, 0, 1)
                slFill.Size = UDim2.new(rx, 0, 1, 0)
                slKnob.Position = UDim2.new(rx, -6, 0.5, 0)
                onChange(rx)
            end

            local slBtn = Create("TextButton", {
                Text = "",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 8, 0, 18),
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-4, -5),
                ZIndex = 9,
                Parent = slTrack,
            })

            slBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    State.ActiveSlider = slData
                    slData.Update(input)
                end
            end)

            return {
                Set = function(val)
                    slFill.Size = UDim2.new(val, 0, 1, 0)
                    slKnob.Position = UDim2.new(val, -6, 0.5, 0)
                end,
            }
        end

        local function UpdateColor()
            color = Color3.fromHSV(h % 1, math.clamp(s, 0, 1), math.clamp(v, 0, 1))
            preview.BackgroundColor3 = color
            task.spawn(function() pcall(callback, color) end)
        end

        local hSlider = MakeColorSlider("H", h, function(val) h = val; UpdateColor() end)
        local sSlider = MakeColorSlider("S", s, function(val) s = val; UpdateColor() end)
        local vSlider = MakeColorSlider("V", v, function(val) v = val; UpdateColor() end)

        local toggleBtn = Create("TextButton", {
            Text = "",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT),
            ZIndex = 5,
            Parent = frame,
        })

        toggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            isOpen = not isOpen
            if isOpen then
                frame.ClipsDescendants = false
                Tween(frame, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT + 90)}, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
            else
                Tween(frame, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI_CONSTANTS.ELEMENT_HEIGHT)}, 0.22)
                task.delay(0.25, function() if frame then frame.ClipsDescendants = true end end)
            end
        end)

        frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.ElemHover}, 0.12) end)
        frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(frame, {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Elem}, 0.12) end)

        local obj = {
            Frame = frame,
            Type = "ColorPicker",
            Flag = flag,
            Default = config.Default or Theme.Accent,
            Set = function(_, c, skipCb)
                color = c
                h, s, v = c:ToHSV()
                preview.BackgroundColor3 = c
                hSlider.Set(h)
                sSlider.Set(s)
                vSlider.Set(v)
                if not skipCb then task.spawn(function() pcall(callback, color) end) end
            end,
            Get = function() return color end,
        }
        State.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    return Tab
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 16: CONFIG SYSTEM (Encrypted Single File)
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local ConfigSystem = {}
ConfigSystem.CurrentProfile = "Default"
ConfigSystem.AutoSave = true
ConfigSystem.AutoSaveInterval = 60

local function XOREncrypt(data, key)
    local result = {}
    for i = 1, #data do
        local keyIdx = ((i - 1) % #key) + 1
        table.insert(result, string.char(bit32.bxor(string.byte(data, i), string.byte(key, keyIdx))))
    end
    return table.concat(result)
end

local function XORDecrypt(data, key)
    return XOREncrypt(data, key) -- XOR is symmetric
end

function ConfigSystem.ensureFolder()
    if not Ex.FileSystem then return false end
    pcall(function()
        if not isfolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) then makefolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) end
    end)
    return true
end

function ConfigSystem.getPath()
    return CONFIG_FOLDER .. "/" .. CONFIG_FILE
end

function ConfigSystem.loadAll()
    if not Ex.FileSystem then return nil end
    ConfigSystem.ensureFolder()
    local path = ConfigSystem.getPath()
    if not isfile(path) then return nil end

    local success, result = pcall(function()
        local raw = readfile(path)
        local wrapper = Http:JSONDecode(raw)
        if wrapper and wrapper.d then
            local decrypted = XORDecrypt(wrapper.d, ENCRYPTION_KEY)
            return Http:JSONDecode(decrypted)
        end
        return nil
    end)

    if success and result then
        return result
    else
        Console.log("error", "Config load failed: " .. tostring(result))
        return nil
    end
end

function ConfigSystem.saveAll(data)
    if not Ex.FileSystem then return false end
    ConfigSystem.ensureFolder()
    local path = ConfigSystem.getPath()

    local success, err = pcall(function()
        local json = Http:JSONEncode(data)
        local encrypted = XOREncrypt(json, ENCRYPTION_KEY)
        local wrapper = Http:JSONEncode({v = 1, d = encrypted, t = tick()})
        writefile(path, wrapper)
    end)

    if not success then
        Console.log("error", "Config save failed: " .. tostring(err))
    end
    return success
end

function ConfigSystem.saveProfile(profileName)
    profileName = profileName or ConfigSystem.CurrentProfile
    local allData = ConfigSystem.loadAll() or {profiles = {}, meta = {}}

    local flags = {}
    for flagName, element in pairs(State.Flags) do
        if element.Get then
            local value = element:Get()
            if type(value) == "boolean" or type(value) == "number" or type(value) == "string" then
                flags[flagName] = value
            elseif typeof(value) == "Color3" then
                flags[flagName] = {_t = "C3", R = value.R, G = value.G, B = value.B}
            elseif typeof(value) == "EnumItem" then
                flags[flagName] = {_t = "EK", N = value.Name}
            end
        end
    end

    allData.profiles = allData.profiles or {}
    allData.profiles[profileName] = flags
    allData.meta = allData.meta or {}
    allData.meta.lastProfile = profileName
    allData.meta.modified = tick()

    return ConfigSystem.saveAll(allData)
end

function ConfigSystem.loadProfile(profileName)
    if State.Loading then return false end
    State.Loading = true

    local allData = ConfigSystem.loadAll()
    if not allData or not allData.profiles then
        State.Loading = false
        return false
    end

    local profile = allData.profiles[profileName]
    if not profile then
        Console.log("warning", "Profile not found: " .. profileName)
        State.Loading = false
        return false
    end

    for flagName, value in pairs(profile) do
        local element = State.Flags[flagName]
        if element and element.Set then
            pcall(function()
                if type(value) == "table" then
                    if value._t == "C3" then
                        element:Set(Color3.new(value.R, value.G, value.B), true)
                    elseif value._t == "EK" then
                        element:Set(Enum.KeyCode[value.N], true)
                    end
                else
                    element:Set(value, true)
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    ConfigSystem.CurrentProfile = profileName
    State.Loading = false
    Console.log("success", "Loaded profile: " .. profileName)
    return true
end

function ConfigSystem.getProfiles()
    local allData = ConfigSystem.loadAll()
    if not allData or not allData.profiles then return {"Default"} end
    local profiles = {}
    for name in pairs(allData.profiles) do
        table.insert(profiles, name)
    end
    if #profiles == 0 then table.insert(profiles, "Default") end
    return profiles
end

function ConfigSystem.deleteProfile(profileName)
    local allData = ConfigSystem.loadAll()
    if not allData or not allData.profiles then return false end
    allData.profiles[profileName] = nil
    return ConfigSystem.saveAll(allData)
end

function ConfigSystem.exportProfile(profileName)
    if not Ex.Clipboard then return false end
    local allData = ConfigSystem.loadAll()
    if not allData or not allData.profiles then return false end
    local profile = allData.profiles[profileName or ConfigSystem.CurrentProfile]
    if not profile then return false end
    pcall(setclipboard, Http:JSONEncode(profile))
    return true
end

function ConfigSystem.importProfile(jsonString, profileName)
    local success, data = pcall(Http.JSONDecode, Http, jsonString)
    if not success or type(data) ~= "table" then return false end
    local allData = ConfigSystem.loadAll() or {profiles = {}, meta = {}}
    allData.profiles[profileName or "Imported"] = data
    return ConfigSystem.saveAll(allData)
end

function ConfigSystem.startAutoSave()
    task.spawn(function()
        while State.Alive and not State.Destroying do
            task.wait(ConfigSystem.AutoSaveInterval)
            if ConfigSystem.AutoSave and not State.Destroying then
                ConfigSystem.saveProfile()
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 17: DESTROY / SELF-CLEANER
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local function DestroyAll(isPanic)
    if State.Destroying then return end
    State.Destroying = true
    State.Alive = false

    Console.log("working", isPanic and "PANIC CLEANUP" or "Starting cleanup...")

    -- Save config
    if ConfigSystem.AutoSave and not isPanic then
        pcall(function() ConfigSystem.saveProfile() end)
    end

    task.wait(isPanic and 0 or 0.1)

    -- Disconnect connections
    local batchSize = isPanic and 999 or 30
    local count = 0
    for _, conn in pairs(State.Connections) do
        pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
        count = count + 1
        if not isPanic and count % batchSize == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    State.Connections = {}

    -- Remove drawings
    count = 0
    for _, draw in ipairs(State.Drawings) do
        pcall(function() draw.Visible = false end)
        pcall(function() draw:Remove() end)
        count = count + 1
        if not isPanic and count % 20 == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    State.Drawings = {}

    -- Destroy ESP instances
    for _, esp in pairs(State.ESP) do
        pcall(function() if esp.Folder then esp.Folder:Destroy() end end)
    end
    State.ESP = {}

    -- Restore originals
    pcall(function()
        Ltg.Ambient = State.Originals.Ambient
        Ltg.Brightness = State.Originals.Brightness
        Ltg.FogEnd = State.Originals.FogEnd
        Ltg.FogStart = State.Originals.FogStart or 0
        Ltg.GlobalShadows = State.Originals.GlobalShadows
        if State.Originals.ClockTime then Ltg.ClockTime = State.Originals.ClockTime end
        Cam.FieldOfView = State.Originals.FOV
        workspace.Gravity = State.Originals.Gravity or 196
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
            local hum = LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            hum.WalkSpeed = 16
            hum.JumpPower = 50
        end
        if LP.Character then
            for _, part in pairs(LP.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    part.CanCollide = true
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- Cleanup FPS Booster
    pcall(function() FPSBooster.cleanup() end)

    -- Clear globals
    _G[BOOSTER_KEY] = nil
    shared[BOOSTER_KEY] = nil

    task.wait(isPanic and 0 or 0.1)

    -- Destroy GUI
    pcall(game.Destroy, ScreenGui)

    -- Final GC
    collectgarbage("collect")
    collectgarbage("collect")

    Console.log("success", "Cleanup complete")
end

-- Keybind handlers
TrackConnection(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed then return end
    if input.KeyCode == State.ToggleKey then
        ToggleUI()
    end
    if input.KeyCode == State.PanicKey then
        DestroyAll(true)
    end
end))

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 18: LOADING SCREEN
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local function ShowLoadingScreen(onComplete)
    local loadingGui = Create("ScreenGui", {
        Name = RandomName(),
        ResetOnSpawn = false,
        DisplayOrder = 999999,
        IgnoreGuiInset = true,
    })
    pcall(function() loadingGui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if not loadingGui.Parent then loadingGui.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end

    local bg = Create("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 15),
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        Parent = loadingGui,
    })

    Create("UIGradient", {
        Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 20)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 28)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 20)),
        }),
        Rotation = 45,
        Parent = bg,
    })

    local center = Create("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 120),
        Parent = bg,
    })

    local logo = Create("TextLabel", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 40),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        Text = SCRIPT_NAME,
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220),
        TextSize = 32,
        Parent = center,
    })

    -- Logo glow
    local logoGlow = logo:Clone()
    logoGlow.TextTransparency = 0.7
    logoGlow.TextSize = 34
    logoGlow.ZIndex = -1
    logoGlow.Parent = center

    Create("TextLabel", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.38),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 16),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
        Text = "v" .. VERSION .. " Premium",
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 130),
        TextSize = 12,
        Parent = center,
    })

    -- Progress bar
    local barBg = Create("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 50),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.6),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 4),
        Parent = center,
    })
    AddCorner(barBg, UDim.new(1, 0))

    local barFill = Create("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220),
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
        Parent = barBg,
    })
    AddCorner(barFill, UDim.new(1, 0))
    Create("UIGradient", {
        Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(100, 60, 180)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(180, 130, 250)),
        }),
        Parent = barFill,
    })

    -- Bouncing dots
    local dotsContainer = Create("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.8),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 10),
        Parent = center,
    })

    local dots = {}
    for i = 1, 3 do
        local dot = Create("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220),
            Position = UDim2.fromScale((i - 1) * 0.5, 0.5),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6),
            Parent = dotsContainer,
        })
        AddCorner(dot, UDim.new(1, 0))
        dots[i] = dot
    end

    -- Animate dots
    task.spawn(function()
        local dotIdx = 1
        while loadingGui.Parent do
            for i, dot in ipairs(dots) do
                local active = i == dotIdx
                Tween(dot, {
                    BackgroundTransparency = active and 0 or 0.7,
                    Size = active and UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8) or UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6),
                }, 0.15)
            end
            dotIdx = (dotIdx % 3) + 1
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)

    -- Progress animation
    task.spawn(function()
        local stages = {0.2, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9, 1.0}
        local durations = {0.3, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2}
        local totalTime = 0

        for i, target in ipairs(stages) do
            local dur = durations[i]
            Tween(barFill, {Size = UDim2.fromScale(target, 1)}, dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
            task.wait(dur)
            totalTime = totalTime + dur
        end

        -- Ensure minimum display
        local elapsed = totalTime
        if elapsed < 1.5 then task.wait(1.5 - elapsed) end

        -- Fade out
        for _, child in pairs(center:GetDescendants()) do
            if child:IsA("TextLabel") then
                Tween(child, {TextTransparency = 1}, 0.3)
            elseif child:IsA("Frame") then
                Tween(child, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.3)
            end
        end
        Tween(bg, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}, 0.3)

        task.wait(0.35)
        pcall(game.Destroy, loadingGui)

        if onComplete then onComplete() end
    end)
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 2: ARMOR DETECTION
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local function DetectArmor()
    for _, p in ipairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        local c = p.Character
        if c then
            local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then
                for _, name in ipairs({"Armor", "Shield", "Vest", "Protection", "Shields"}) do
                    local v = h:GetAttribute(name)
                    if v and type(v) == "number" then
                        State.ArmorData = {type = "attr", name = name}
                        Console.log("success", "Armor detected: " .. name)
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    Console.log("info", "No armor system detected")
end

local function GetArmor(player)
    if not State.ArmorData and not State.ArmorForce then return nil end
    local c = player.Character
    if not c then return nil end
    local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not h then return nil end
    if State.ArmorData and State.ArmorData.type == "attr" then
        return h:GetAttribute(State.ArmorData.name)
    end
    for _, name in ipairs({"Armor", "Shield", "Vest"}) do
        local v = h:GetAttribute(name)
        if v and type(v) == "number" then return v end
    end
    return nil
end

DetectArmor()

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 3: TAB DEFINITIONS
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

-- ═══════ HOME TAB ═══════
local HomeTab = Lib:CreateTab("Home", "🏠")
HomeTab:CreateSection({Name = "Welcome"})
local toggleKeyLabel = HomeTab:CreateLabel({
    Text = 'Press "' .. State.ToggleKey.Name .. '" to toggle UI',
    Color = Theme.SubText,
})
HomeTab:CreateLabel({Text = "Executor: " .. Ex.Name, Color = Theme.Accent})
HomeTab:CreateLabel({Text = "Insert = Panic Destroy", Color = Theme.DimText})
HomeTab:CreateDivider()
HomeTab:CreateSection({Name = "Quick Actions"})
HomeTab:CreateButton({
    Name = "Copy Discord",
    Callback = function()
        if Ex.Clipboard then pcall(setclipboard, "discord.gg/lvyuflo") end
        Notify({Title = "Copied!", Content = "Discord link copied", Type = "Success"})
    end,
})
HomeTab:CreateButton({
    Name = "Rejoin Server",
    Callback = function()
        pcall(function()
            game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId, LP)
        end)
    end,
})
HomeTab:CreateButton({
    Name = "Server Hop",
    Callback = function()
        pcall(function()
            local data = Http:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(
                "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
            ))
            for _, server in pairs(data.data) do
                if server.id ~= game.JobId then
                    game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, server.id, LP)
                    break
                end
            end
        end)
    end,
})

-- ═══════ MOVEMENT TAB ═══════
local MoveTab = Lib:CreateTab("Movement", "🏃")

-- Speed
local speedSection = MoveTab:CreateSection({Name = "Speed"})
MoveTab:CreateSlider({Name = "Walk Speed", Min = 16, Max = 250, Default = 16, Increment = 1, Flag = "WS", Section = speedSection,
    Callback = function(v)
        pcall(function() LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").WalkSpeed = v end)
    end,
})
MoveTab:CreateKeybind({Name = "Speed Keybind", Flag = "WSKey", Default = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, NoCallback = true,
    Callback = function()
        local f = State.Flags.WS
        if f then
            local current = f:Get()
            if current > 16 then f:Set(16) else f:Set(100) end
        end
    end,
    Section = speedSection,
})

-- Jump
local jumpSection = MoveTab:CreateSection({Name = "Jump"})
MoveTab:CreateSlider({Name = "Jump Power", Min = 50, Max = 350, Default = 50, Increment = 5, Flag = "JP", Section = jumpSection,
    Callback = function(v)
        pcall(function() LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").JumpPower = v end)
    end,
})
MoveTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Infinite Jump", Flag = "IJ", Section = jumpSection})
MoveTab:CreateKeybind({Name = "Infinite Jump Keybind", Flag = "IJKey", Default = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, NoCallback = true,
    Callback = function()
        local f = State.Flags.IJ
        if f then f:Set(not f:Get()) end
    end,
    Section = jumpSection,
})
MoveTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Bunny Hop", Flag = "BHop", Section = jumpSection,
    Tooltip = "Auto-jumps while holding spacebar",
})

-- Fly
local flySection = MoveTab:CreateSection({Name = "Fly"})
MoveTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Enable Fly", Flag = "FlyOn", Section = flySection,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            -- Disable normal noclip when flying
            local nc = State.Flags.NCOn
            if nc and nc:Get() then nc:Set(false) end
        end
    end,
})
MoveTab:CreateKeybind({Name = "Fly Keybind", Flag = "FlyKey", Default = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, NoCallback = true,
    Callback = function()
        local f = State.Flags.FlyOn
        if f then f:Set(not f:Get()) end
    end,
    Section = flySection,
})
MoveTab:CreateSlider({Name = "Fly Speed", Min = 10, Max = 500, Default = 50, Increment = 5, Flag = "FlySpd", Section = flySection})
MoveTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Noclip While Flying", Flag = "FlyNC", Section = flySection})

-- Noclip
local noclipSection = MoveTab:CreateSection({Name = "Noclip"})
MoveTab:CreateLabel({Text = "Disabled when Fly is active", Color = Theme.DimText, Section = noclipSection})
MoveTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Enable Noclip", Flag = "NCOn", Section = noclipSection,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            local fly = State.Flags.FlyOn
            if fly and fly:Get() then
                State.Flags.NCOn:Set(false, true)
                Notify({Title = "Noclip", Content = "Disabled — Fly is active", Type = "Warning"})
            end
        end
    end,
})
MoveTab:CreateKeybind({Name = "Noclip Keybind", Flag = "NCKey", Default = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, NoCallback = true,
    Callback = function()
        local f = State.Flags.NCOn
        if f then f:Set(not f:Get()) end
    end,
    Section = noclipSection,
})

-- Gravity
local gravSection = MoveTab:CreateSection({Name = "Gravity"})
MoveTab:CreateSlider({Name = "Gravity", Min = 10, Max = 400, Default = math.floor(State.Originals.Gravity or 196), Increment = 1, Flag = "GV", Section = gravSection,
    Callback = function(v) workspace.Gravity = v end,
})
MoveTab:CreateButton({Name = "Reset Gravity", Section = gravSection, Callback = function()
    workspace.Gravity = State.Originals.Gravity or 196
    local f = State.Flags.GV
    if f then f:Set(math.floor(State.Originals.Gravity or 196), true) end
end})

-- FOV

    Callback = function(v) Cam.FieldOfView = v end,
})
MoveTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Freecam", Flag = "FreeCam", Section = fovSection})

    Callback = function()
        local f = State.Flags.FreeCam
        if f then f:Set(not f:Get()) end
    end,
    Section = fovSection,
})

MoveTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Third Person Lock", Flag = "TP3P", Section = fovSection})

-- Teleport
local tpSection = MoveTab:CreateSection({Name = "Teleport"})
local savedLocLabel = MoveTab:CreateColorInfo("No location saved", Theme.Warn)
MoveTab:CreateButton({Name = "Save Location", Section = tpSection, Callback = function()
    if LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        local p = LP.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
        State.SavedLocation = p
        savedLocLabel:Set(string.format("📍 %d, %d, %d", p.X, p.Y, p.Z))
    end
end})
MoveTab:CreateButton({Name = "TP to Saved", Section = tpSection, Callback = function()
    if State.SavedLocation then
        pcall(function() LP.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(State.SavedLocation + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) end)
    else
        Notify({Title = "Error", Content = "No location saved", Type = "Error"})
    end
end})
MoveTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Click TP (Ctrl+Click)", Flag = "CTP", Section = tpSection})

-- ═══════ WORLD TAB ═══════
local WorldTab = Lib:CreateTab("World", "🌍")

local lightSection = WorldTab:CreateSection({Name = "Lighting"})
WorldTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Fullbright", Flag = "FB", Section = lightSection,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            Ltg.Ambient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1); Ltg.Brightness = 2; Ltg.FogEnd = 1e5; Ltg.GlobalShadows = false
        else
            Ltg.Ambient = State.Originals.Ambient; Ltg.Brightness = State.Originals.Brightness
            Ltg.FogEnd = State.Originals.FogEnd; Ltg.GlobalShadows = true
        end
    end,
})
WorldTab:CreateKeybind({Name = "Fullbright Keybind", Flag = "FBKey", Default = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, NoCallback = true,
    Callback = function()
        local f = State.Flags.FB
        if f then f:Set(not f:Get()) end
    end,
    Section = lightSection,
})
WorldTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Night Vision", Flag = "NV", Section = lightSection,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            Ltg.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 120); Ltg.Brightness = 3; Ltg.FogEnd = 1e5
        else
            Ltg.Ambient = State.Originals.Ambient; Ltg.Brightness = State.Originals.Brightness
            Ltg.FogEnd = State.Originals.FogEnd
        end
    end,
})
WorldTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Remove Fog", Flag = "NF", Section = lightSection,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then Ltg.FogEnd = 999999; Ltg.FogStart = 999999
        else Ltg.FogEnd = State.Originals.FogEnd; Ltg.FogStart = State.Originals.FogStart or 0 end
    end,
})
WorldTab:CreateSlider({Name = "Brightness", Min = 0, Max = 5, Default = 2, Increment = 0.5, Flag = "Br", Section = lightSection,
    Callback = function(v) Ltg.Brightness = v end,
})
WorldTab:CreateSlider({Name = "Time of Day", Min = 0, Max = 24, Default = 14, Increment = 0.5, Suffix = "h", Flag = "ToD", Section = lightSection,
    Callback = function(v) Ltg.ClockTime = v end,
})
WorldTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Freeze Time", Flag = "FrT", Section = lightSection})
WorldTab:CreateColorPicker({Name = "Custom Ambient", Default = State.Originals.Ambient or Color3.fromRGB(127, 127, 127), Flag = "CAm", Section = lightSection,
    Callback = function(c) Ltg.Ambient = c end,
})

local postFXSection = WorldTab:CreateSection({Name = "Post Processing"})
WorldTab:CreateButton({Name = "Remove All PostFX", Section = postFXSection, Callback = function()
    for _, v in pairs(Ltg:GetChildren()) do if v:IsA("PostEffect") then v:Destroy() end end
    for _, v in pairs(Cam:GetChildren()) do if v:IsA("PostEffect") then v:Destroy() end end
    Notify({Title = "Done", Content = "Removed all post-processing effects", Type = "Success"})
end})
WorldTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Remove Blur", Flag = "RmBlur", Section = postFXSection,
    Callback = function(v) for _, e in pairs(Ltg:GetChildren()) do if e:IsA("BlurEffect") then e.Enabled = not v end end
        for _, e in pairs(Cam:GetChildren()) do if e:IsA("BlurEffect") then e.Enabled = not v end end end,
})
WorldTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Remove Bloom", Flag = "RmBloom", Section = postFXSection,
    Callback = function(v) for _, e in pairs(Ltg:GetChildren()) do if e:IsA("BloomEffect") then e.Enabled = not v end end end,
})
WorldTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Remove DoF", Flag = "RmDoF", Section = postFXSection,
    Callback = function(v) for _, e in pairs(Ltg:GetChildren()) do if e:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then e.Enabled = not v end end end,
})

local perfWorldSection = WorldTab:CreateSection({Name = "Performance"})
WorldTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Remove Particles", Flag = "NoPart", Section = perfWorldSection,
    Callback = function(v)
        for _, e in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if e:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or e:IsA("Fire") or e:IsA("Smoke") or e:IsA("Sparkles") then
                e.Enabled = not v
            end
        end
    end,
})
WorldTab:CreateToggle({Name = "Remove Decals", Flag = "NoDec", Section = perfWorldSection,
    Callback = function(v)
        for _, e in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if e:IsA("Decal") or e:IsA("Texture") then
                e.Transparency = v and 1 or 0
            end
        end
    end,
})

local envSection = WorldTab:CreateSection({Name = "Environment"})
WorldTab:CreateButton({Name = "Reset Environment", Section = envSection, Callback = function()
    Ltg.Ambient = State.Originals.Ambient; Ltg.Brightness = State.Originals.Brightness
    Ltg.FogEnd = State.Originals.FogEnd; Ltg.FogStart = State.Originals.FogStart or 0
    Ltg.GlobalShadows = true; Cam.FieldOfView = State.Originals.FOV
    workspace.Gravity = State.Originals.Gravity or 196
    for _, flagName in ipairs({"FB", "NV", "NF", "FrT", "RmBlur", "RmBloom", "RmDoF", "NoPart", "NoDec"}) do
        local f = State.Flags[flagName]
        if f then f:Set(false, true) end
    end
    Notify({Title = "Reset", Content = "Environment restored", Type = "Success"})
end})

        local p = LP.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
        pcall(setclipboard, string.format("%.1f, %.1f, %.1f", p.X, p.Y, p.Z))
        Notify({Title = "Copied!", Type = "Success"})

    pcall(function()
        local d = Http:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"))
        for _, s in pairs(d.data) do
            if s.id ~= game.JobId then
                game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, s.id, LP)
                break
            end
        end
    end)
end})
