        local name = State.Flags.CfgName and State.Flags.CfgName:Get() or "Default"
        if name == "" then name = "Default" end
        Config.saveProfile(name)
        Config.CurrentProfile = name
        Notify({Title = "Saved!", Content = name, Type = "Success"})
    end})

    local cfgProfiles = Config.getProfiles()
    local cfgDropdown = ConfigTab:CreateDropdown({Name = "Load Config", Options = #cfgProfiles > 0 and cfgProfiles or {"None"}, Default = "None", Section = cfgSection,
        Callback = function(v)
            if v ~= "None" and not State.Loading then
                Config.loadProfile(v)
                Notify({Title = "Loaded!", Content = v, Type = "Success"})
            end
        end,
    })

    ConfigTab:CreateButton({Name = "Refresh List", Section = cfgSection, Callback = function()
        local list = Config.getProfiles()
        cfgDropdown:Refresh(#list > 0 and list or {"None"})
    end})

    ConfigTab:CreateButton({Name = "Delete Config", Section = cfgSection, Callback = function()
        local name = cfgDropdown:Get()
        if name and name ~= "None" then
            Confirm("Delete?", "Delete config '" .. name .. "'?", function()
                Config.deleteProfile(name)
                local list = Config.getProfiles()
                cfgDropdown:Refresh(#list > 0 and list or {"None"})
                Notify({Title = "Deleted", Content = name, Type = "Success"})
            end)
        end
    end})

    ConfigTab:CreateDivider()
    local cfgIOSection = ConfigTab:CreateSection({Name = "Import / Export"})

    ConfigTab:CreateButton({Name = "Export to Clipboard", Section = cfgIOSection, Callback = function()
        if Config.exportProfile() then
            Notify({Title = "Exported!", Content = "Config copied to clipboard", Type = "Success"})
        else
            Notify({Title = "Error", Content = "Failed to export", Type = "Error"})
        end
    end})

    ConfigTab:CreateButton({Name = "Import from Clipboard", Section = cfgIOSection, Callback = function()
        -- This would need a text input, simplified:
        Notify({Title = "Import", Content = "Paste JSON into Config Name, then save", Type = "Info"})
    end})

    ConfigTab:CreateDivider()
    local presetSection = ConfigTab:CreateSection({Name = "Presets"})

    ConfigTab:CreateButton({Name = "🟢 Legit Preset", Section = presetSection, Callback = function()
        Confirm("Apply Legit Preset?", "This will change your aimbot & ESP settings to safe values.", function()
            local safeSettings = {AO = true, ASm = 90, AWC = true, ASFOV = false, ASilent = false, ESP = true, ESPBox = true, ESPBT = "Corner", ESPChams = false, FlyOn = false}
            for flag, val in pairs(safeSettings) do
                local f = State.Flags[flag]
                if f then f:Set(val, true) end
            end
            Notify({Title = "Applied", Content = "Legit preset loaded", Type = "Success"})
        end)
    end})

    ConfigTab:CreateButton({Name = "🔴 Rage Preset", Section = presetSection, Callback = function()
        Confirm("Apply Rage Preset?", "This will set aggressive settings. Use at your own risk.", function()
            local rageSettings = {AO = true, ASm = 5, AWC = true, AFOV = 300, ESP = true, ESPBox = true, ESPSkel = true, ESPTr = true}
            for flag, val in pairs(rageSettings) do
                local f = State.Flags[flag]
                if f then f:Set(val, true) end
            end
            Notify({Title = "Applied", Content = "Rage preset loaded", Type = "Warning"})
        end)
    end})

    ConfigTab:CreateButton({Name = "👁️ Visuals Only", Section = presetSection, Callback = function()
        Confirm("Apply Visuals Preset?", "Enables only visual features, disables aimbot.", function()
            local visSettings = {AO = false, ASilent = false, FlyOn = false, NCOn = false, ESP = true, ESPBox = true, ESPN = true, ESPH = true, ESPD = true}
            for flag, val in pairs(visSettings) do

                if f then f:Set(val, true) end

    [17625359962] = "Rivals",
    [286090429] = "Arsenal",
    [5765841637] = "The Armory",
}
if gamePresets[game.PlaceId] then
    detectedGame = gamePresets[game.PlaceId]
end

    SettingsTab:CreateButton({Name = "Apply " .. detectedGame .. " Preset", Section = presetSection2, Callback = function()
        Confirm("Apply " .. detectedGame .. " Preset?",
            "Do you want to save your current config first?\n\n⚠️ Current aimbot settings will be changed.", function()
            Config.saveProfile("Pre-Preset_Backup")
            -- Game-specific settings
            if detectedGame == "Rivals" or detectedGame == "The Armory" then
                local settings = {AM = "Hybrid", ASm = 40, AWC = true, AWCM = "Multi-Ray"}
                for flag, val in pairs(settings) do
                    local f = State.Flags[flag]
                    if f then f:Set(val, true) end
                end
            elseif detectedGame == "Arsenal" then
                local settings = {AM = "Camera", ASm = 25, APred = true, APredAmt = 80}
                for flag, val in pairs(settings) do
                    local f = State.Flags[flag]
                    if f then f:Set(val, true) end
                end
            end
            Notify({Title = "Preset Applied", Content = detectedGame .. " (backup saved)", Type = "Success"})
        end)
    end})
end

    TextSize = 11,
    TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
    BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
    AutoButtonColor = false,

    Text = "Copy All",
    Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
    TextSize = 11,
    TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
    BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Inner,
    AutoButtonColor = false,
    Size = UDim2.new(0.3, -4, 1, 0),
    Position = UDim2.new(0.35, 0, 0, 0),
    Parent = consoleBtnFrame,
})
AddCorner(copyConsoleBtn, UDim.new(0, 4))

clearConsoleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    Console.clear()
    for _, child in ipairs(consoleScroll:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("TextLabel") then child:Destroy() end
    end
end)

copyConsoleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    Console.copyAll()
end)

-- Console UI updater
Console.UIUpdate = function()
    if not State.Flags.ShowCon or not State.Flags.ShowCon:Get() then return end

    -- Rebuild console display
    for _, child in ipairs(consoleScroll:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("TextLabel") or child:IsA("TextButton") then child:Destroy() end
    end

    for i, log in ipairs(Console.Logs) do
        if i > 50 then break end -- Show max 50 lines
        local logType = Console.Types[log.Type] or Console.Types.info
        local logLine = Create("TextButton", {
            Text = string.format("[%s] %s %s", log.Time, logType.Icon, log.Message),
            Font = Enum.Font.Code,
            TextSize = 11,
            TextColor3 = logType.Color,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextWrapped = true,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            LayoutOrder = i,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            Parent = consoleScroll,
        })
        -- Click to copy single line
        logLine.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if Ex.Clipboard then
                pcall(setclipboard, log.Message)
                Notify({Title = "Copied", Content = "Log entry copied", Type = "Success", Duration = 1.5})
            end
        end)
    end
end

-- Show/hide console
if State.Flags.ShowCon then
    -- Watch for toggle changes
    local origCb = State.Flags.ShowCon.Set
    local oldSet = State.Flags.ShowCon.Set
end

-- We'll handle console visibility in a simpler way
task.spawn(function()
    while State.Alive and not State.Destroying do
        task.wait(0.5)
        local showCon = State.Flags.ShowCon and State.Flags.ShowCon:Get()
        if consoleFrame.Visible ~= (showCon == true) then
            consoleFrame.Visible = showCon == true
            if showCon then Console.UIUpdate() end
        end
    end
end)

    Confirm("Enable Safe Mode?", "This will disable: Silent Aim, Chams, Speed hacks, Fly, Noclip", function()
        local riskyFlags = {"ASilent", "ESPChams", "FlyOn", "NCOn", "FlyNC"}
        for _, flag in ipairs(riskyFlags) do
            local f = State.Flags[flag]
            if f then f:Set(false, true) end
        end
        local wsF = State.Flags.WS
        if wsF then wsF:Set(16, true) end
        local jpF = State.Flags.JP
        if jpF then jpF:Set(50, true) end
        pcall(function()
            LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").WalkSpeed = 16
            LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").JumpPower = 50
        end)
        Notify({Title = "Safe Mode", Content = "All risky features disabled", Type = "Success"})
    end)
end})

    Confirm("Reset Everything?", "This will reset ALL settings to defaults.", function()
        for flag, elem in pairs(State.Flags) do
            if elem.Default ~= nil and elem.Set then
                pcall(function() elem:Set(elem.Default, true) end)
            end
        end
        Ltg.Ambient = State.Originals.Ambient; Ltg.Brightness = State.Originals.Brightness
        Ltg.FogEnd = State.Originals.FogEnd; Cam.FieldOfView = State.Originals.FOV

            LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").WalkSpeed = 16
            LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").JumpPower = 50
        end)
        Notify({Title = "Reset", Content = "All settings restored to defaults", Type = "Success"})
    end)
end})

-- Skeleton bone connections
local SKELETON_CONNECTIONS = {
    {"Head", "UpperTorso"},
    {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"},
    {"UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm"},
    {"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm"},
    {"LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand"},
    {"UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm"},
    {"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm"},
    {"RightLowerArm", "RightHand"},
    {"LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg"},
    {"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg"},
    {"LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"},
    {"LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg"},
    {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg"},
    {"RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"},
}

local function CreateESP(player)
    if player == LP then return end
    local esp = {Player = player}

    local folder = Create("Folder", {Name = RandomName(), Parent = Lib.ScreenGui})
    esp.Folder = folder

    -- Billboard GUI for names/distance/flags
    local bb = Create("BillboardGui", {
        AlwaysOnTop = true,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 70),
        StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0),
        Parent = folder,
    })
    local bbContent = Create("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = bb})

    esp.NameLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        Text = player.Name,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
        TextSize = 13,
        TextStrokeColor3 = Theme.Black,
        TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3,
        Parent = bbContent,
    })

    esp.DistLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 15),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
        Text = "",
        TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
        TextSize = 11,
        TextStrokeColor3 = Theme.Black,
        TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3,
        Parent = bbContent,
    })

    esp.InfoLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 27),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12),
        Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
        Text = "",
        TextColor3 = Theme.DimText,
        TextSize = 10,
        TextStrokeColor3 = Theme.Black,
        TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3,
        Parent = bbContent,
    })

    esp.HPLabel = Create("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 39),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 11),
        Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
        Text = "",
        TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
        TextSize = 10,
        TextStrokeColor3 = Theme.Black,
        TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3,
        Parent = bbContent,
    })

    esp.BB = bb

    -- Drawing elements
    if Ex.Drawing then
        -- Box lines (8 for corners, 4 for full)
        esp.Box = {}
        for j = 1, 8 do
            esp.Box[j] = NewDrawing("Line")
            if esp.Box[j] then esp.Box[j].Visible = false; esp.Box[j].Thickness = 1 end
        end
        -- Box outline
        esp.BoxOutline = {}
        for j = 1, 8 do
            esp.BoxOutline[j] = NewDrawing("Line")
            if esp.BoxOutline[j] then esp.BoxOutline[j].Visible = false; esp.BoxOutline[j].Thickness = 3; esp.BoxOutline[j].Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0) end
        end

        -- Health bar
        esp.HBar = NewDrawing("Line"); if esp.HBar then esp.HBar.Visible = false; esp.HBar.Thickness = 2 end
        esp.HBarBg = NewDrawing("Line"); if esp.HBarBg then esp.HBarBg.Visible = false; esp.HBarBg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40); esp.HBarBg.Thickness = 2 end

        -- Armor bar
        esp.ABar = NewDrawing("Line"); if esp.ABar then esp.ABar.Visible = false; esp.ABar.Color = Theme.Armor; esp.ABar.Thickness = 2 end
        esp.ABarBg = NewDrawing("Line"); if esp.ABarBg then esp.ABarBg.Visible = false; esp.ABarBg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40); esp.ABarBg.Thickness = 2 end

        -- Tracer
        esp.Tracer = NewDrawing("Line"); if esp.Tracer then esp.Tracer.Visible = false; esp.Tracer.Thickness = 1 end

        -- Skeleton
        esp.Skeleton = {}
        for j = 1, #SKELETON_CONNECTIONS do
            esp.Skeleton[j] = NewDrawing("Line")
            if esp.Skeleton[j] then esp.Skeleton[j].Visible = false; esp.Skeleton[j].Thickness = 1 end
        end

        -- Off-screen arrow
        esp.Arrow = NewDrawing("Triangle")
        if esp.Arrow then esp.Arrow.Visible = false; esp.Arrow.Filled = true end
    end

    -- Chams highlight
    esp.Highlight = nil

    State.ESP[player] = esp
end

local function RemoveESP(player)
    local esp = State.ESP[player]
    if not esp then return end

    -- Hide all drawing objects
    if esp.Box then for j = 1, 8 do if esp.Box[j] then pcall(function() esp.Box[j].Visible = false end) end end end
    if esp.BoxOutline then for j = 1, 8 do if esp.BoxOutline[j] then pcall(function() esp.BoxOutline[j].Visible = false end) end end end
    if esp.HBar then pcall(function() esp.HBar.Visible = false end) end
    if esp.HBarBg then pcall(function() esp.HBarBg.Visible = false end) end
    if esp.ABar then pcall(function() esp.ABar.Visible = false end) end
    if esp.ABarBg then pcall(function() esp.ABarBg.Visible = false end) end
    if esp.Tracer then pcall(function() esp.Tracer.Visible = false end) end
    if esp.Skeleton then for j = 1, #SKELETON_CONNECTIONS do if esp.Skeleton[j] then pcall(function() esp.Skeleton[j].Visible = false end) end end end
    if esp.Arrow then pcall(function() esp.Arrow.Visible = false end) end
    if esp.Highlight then pcall(game.Destroy, esp.Highlight) end

    pcall(function() esp.Folder:Destroy() end)
    State.ESP[player] = nil
end

local function HideAllESP(esp)
    if esp.Box then for j = 1, 8 do if esp.Box[j] then esp.Box[j].Visible = false end end end
    if esp.BoxOutline then for j = 1, 8 do if esp.BoxOutline[j] then esp.BoxOutline[j].Visible = false end end end
    if esp.HBar then esp.HBar.Visible = false end
    if esp.HBarBg then esp.HBarBg.Visible = false end
    if esp.ABar then esp.ABar.Visible = false end
    if esp.ABarBg then esp.ABarBg.Visible = false end
    if esp.Tracer then esp.Tracer.Visible = false end
    if esp.Skeleton then for j = 1, #SKELETON_CONNECTIONS do if esp.Skeleton[j] then esp.Skeleton[j].Visible = false end end end
    if esp.Arrow then esp.Arrow.Visible = false end
    esp.BB.Enabled = false
end

-- Initialize ESP for existing players
for _, p in ipairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do CreateESP(p) end
TrackConnection(Plrs.PlayerAdded:Connect(CreateESP))
TrackConnection(Plrs.PlayerRemoving:Connect(RemoveESP))

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 5: AIMBOT SYSTEM
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local Aimbot = {}
Aimbot.CurrentTarget = nil
Aimbot.LastTargetTime = 0
Aimbot.StickyTarget = nil
Aimbot.StickyLockTime = 0
Aimbot.GraceTimer = 0
Aimbot.IsActive = false

-- Smoothing curves
Aimbot.Curves = {
    Linear = function(n) return n end,
    Exponential = function(n) return n ^ 0.5 end,
    Sine = function(n) return math.sin(n * math.pi / 2) end,
}

function Aimbot.calculateSmoothing(smoothValue, deltaTime, curveType)
    local curve = Aimbot.Curves[curveType] or Aimbot.Curves.Exponential
    local normalized = 1 - ((smoothValue - 1) / 179)
    local curved = curve(normalized)
    local speed = curved * 25
    local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-speed * deltaTime)
    return math.clamp(alpha, 0.001, 1)
end

-- Multi-layer wall check
local RaycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
RaycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
RaycastParams.IgnoreWater = true

function Aimbot.buildIgnoreList()
    local ignoreList = {}
    if LP.Character then table.insert(ignoreList, LP.Character) end

    -- Add accessories, tools, etc.
    for _, p in ipairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if p.Character then
            for _, child in ipairs(p.Character:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Accessory") or child:IsA("Tool") then
                    table.insert(ignoreList, child)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return ignoreList
end

function Aimbot.isPartVisible(origin, target, character)
    local ignoreList = Aimbot.buildIgnoreList()
    if character then table.insert(ignoreList, character) end
    RaycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignoreList

    local direction = (target - origin).Unit * (target - origin).Magnitude
    local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, direction, RaycastParams)

    if not result then return true end -- Nothing hit = visible

    local ignoreTrans = State.Flags.AWCTrans and State.Flags.AWCTrans:Get()
    local ignoreNoCol = State.Flags.AWCNoCol and State.Flags.AWCNoCol:Get()

    if result.Instance then
        if ignoreTrans and result.Instance.Transparency >= 0.5 then return true end
        if ignoreNoCol and not result.Instance.CanCollide then return true end
        if result.Instance.Material == Enum.Material.ForceField then return true end
        if result.Instance.Material == Enum.Material.Glass and ignoreTrans then return true end
    end

    return false
end

function Aimbot.wallCheck(character)
    if not (State.Flags.AWC and State.Flags.AWC:Get()) then return true end

    local origin = Cam.CFrame.Position
    local method = State.Flags.AWCM and State.Flags.AWCM:Get() or "Multi-Ray"
    local threshold = (State.Flags.AWCThresh and State.Flags.AWCThresh:Get() or 50) / 100

    local checkPoints = {}
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not root then return false end

    if method == "Simple" then
        checkPoints = {head and head.Position or root.Position}
    elseif method == "Multi-Ray" then
        checkPoints = {root.Position}
        if head then table.insert(checkPoints, head.Position) end
        table.insert(checkPoints, root.Position + Vector3.new(1.5, 0, 0))
        table.insert(checkPoints, root.Position + Vector3.new(-1.5, 0, 0))
        table.insert(checkPoints, root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
    elseif method == "Full Body" then
        for _, partName in ipairs({"Head", "HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso",
            "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm", "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperLeg",
            "LeftLowerArm", "RightLowerArm", "LeftLowerLeg", "RightLowerLeg",
            "LeftHand", "RightHand", "LeftFoot", "RightFoot"}) do
            local part = character:FindFirstChild(partName)
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                table.insert(checkPoints, part.Position)
            end
        end
    end

    if #checkPoints == 0 then return false end

    local visibleCount = 0
    for _, point in ipairs(checkPoints) do
        if Aimbot.isPartVisible(origin, point, character) then
            visibleCount = visibleCount + 1
        end
    end

    return (visibleCount / #checkPoints) >= threshold
end

function Aimbot.closestBone(character)
    local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
    local center = GetViewportCenter()

    for _, name in ipairs({"Head", "UpperTorso", "HumanoidRootPart", "LowerTorso",
        "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm", "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperLeg"}) do
        local part = character:FindFirstChild(name)
        if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
            local sp, onScreen = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
            if onScreen then
                local dist = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude
                if dist < bestDist then bestDist = dist; best = part end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

function Aimbot.getTarget()
    local fov = State.Flags.AFOV and State.Flags.AFOV:Get() or 80
    local tc = State.Flags.ATC and State.Flags.ATC:Get()
    local ignoreFriends = State.Flags.AIF and State.Flags.AIF:Get()
    local ignoreDead = State.Flags.AID and State.Flags.AID:Get()
    local maxDist = State.Flags.AMaxD and State.Flags.AMaxD:Get() or 1000
    local targetPart = State.Flags.APt and State.Flags.APt:Get() or "Head"
    local priority = State.Flags.APri and State.Flags.APri:Get() or "Closest to Crosshair"
    local center = GetViewportCenter()
    local myRoot = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return nil end

    local best, bestScore = nil, math.huge

    for _, plr in ipairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr == LP then continue end
        local char = plr.Character
        if not char then continue end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then continue end
        if ignoreDead and hum.Health <= 0 then continue end
        if tc and plr.Team and LP.Team and plr.Team == LP.Team then continue end
        if ignoreFriends and LP:IsFriendsWith(plr.UserId) then continue end

        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then continue end
        local worldDist = (root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
        if worldDist > maxDist then continue end

        -- Get target part
        local part
        if targetPart == "Closest Bone" then
            part = Aimbot.closestBone(char)
        else
            part = char:FindFirstChild(targetPart) or char:FindFirstChild("Head") or root
        end
        if not part then continue end

        local sp, onScreen = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
        if not onScreen then continue end

        local screenDist = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude
        if screenDist > fov then continue end

        -- Wall check
        if not Aimbot.wallCheck(char) then continue end

        -- Priority scoring
        local score
        if priority == "Closest to Crosshair" then
            score = screenDist
        elseif priority == "Closest Distance" then
            score = worldDist
        elseif priority == "Lowest HP" then
            score = hum.Health
        else
            score = screenDist
        end

        if score < bestScore then
            bestScore = score
            best = {Part = part, Character = char, Player = plr, Distance = worldDist}
        end
    end

    return best
end

function Aimbot.predictPosition(part, character)
    if not (State.Flags.APred and State.Flags.APred:Get()) then return part.Position end
    local amount = (State.Flags.APredAmt and State.Flags.APredAmt:Get() or 100) / 100
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return part.Position end
    local vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
    if vel.Magnitude < 3 then return part.Position end
    local scaledAmount = amount * math.clamp(vel.Magnitude / 50, 0.3, 1.5)
    return part.Position + (vel * scaledAmount * 0.06)
end

function Aimbot.isAimActive()
    if not (State.Flags.AO and State.Flags.AO:Get()) then return false end
    local act = State.Flags.AAct and State.Flags.AAct:Get() or "Hold"

    if act == "Always" then return true
    elseif act == "Toggle" then return State.InputState.AimToggled
    else -- Hold
        local key = State.Flags.AK and State.Flags.AK:Get() or Enum.KeyCode.Q
        local mb = State.Flags.AMB and State.Flags.AMB:Get() or "Right"
        local keyDown = false
        pcall(function() keyDown = key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and UIS:IsKeyDown(key) end)
        if keyDown then return true end
        if mb == "Left" and State.InputState.LeftClick then return true end
        if mb == "Right" and State.InputState.RightClick then return true end
        if mb == "Middle" and State.InputState.MiddleClick then return true end
    end
    return false
end

-- Toggle mode handler
TrackConnection(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    local act = State.Flags.AAct and State.Flags.AAct:Get()
    if act == "Toggle" then
        local key = State.Flags.AK and State.Flags.AK:Get() or Enum.KeyCode.Q
        if input.KeyCode == key then
            State.InputState.AimToggled = not State.InputState.AimToggled
        end
    end
end))

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 6: SILENT AIM SYSTEM
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local SilentAim = {}
SilentAim.Active = false
SilentAim.CurrentMethod = nil
SilentAim.Hooks = {}

SilentAim.Methods = {
    {Name = "Bypass-1", Check = function() return Ex.HookFunction end},
    {Name = "Bypass-2", Check = function() return Ex.HookFunction end},
    {Name = "Bypass-3", Check = function() return Ex.GetRawMeta end},
    {Name = "Bypass-4", Check = function() return Ex.GetRawMeta end},
}

function SilentAim.getTarget()
    if not SilentAim.Active then return nil end
    local silentFOV = State.Flags.ASilentFOV and State.Flags.ASilentFOV:Get() or 150
    local hitChance = State.Flags.ASilentHC and State.Flags.ASilentHC:Get() or 100

    if math.random(1, 100) > hitChance then return nil end

    local center = GetViewportCenter()
    local best, bestDist = nil, silentFOV
    local tc = State.Flags.ATC and State.Flags.ATC:Get()
    local myRoot = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return nil end

    for _, plr in ipairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr == LP then continue end
        local char = plr.Character
        if not char then continue end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then continue end
        if tc and plr.Team and LP.Team and plr.Team == LP.Team then continue end

        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not head then continue end

        local sp, onScreen = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position)
        if not onScreen then continue end

        local dist = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Magnitude
        if dist < bestDist then bestDist = dist; best = head end
    end

    return best
end

-- Silent aim is complex and executor-dependent
-- We provide the framework, actual hooking done per method
function SilentAim.enable()
    if not (State.Flags.ASilent and State.Flags.ASilent:Get()) then return end
    local autoDetect = State.Flags.ASilentAuto and State.Flags.ASilentAuto:Get()

    if autoDetect then
        for _, method in ipairs(SilentAim.Methods) do
            if method.Check() then
                SilentAim.CurrentMethod = method
                Console.log("success", "Silent Aim: " .. method.Name .. " available")
                break
            end
        end
        if not SilentAim.CurrentMethod then
            Console.log("error", "Silent Aim: No compatible method found")
            return
        end
    else
        local manual = State.Flags.ASilentMan and State.Flags.ASilentMan:Get() or "Bypass-1"
        for _, method in ipairs(SilentAim.Methods) do
            if method.Name == manual then
                SilentAim.CurrentMethod = method
                break
            end
        end
    end

    SilentAim.Active = true
    Console.log("success", "Silent Aim enabled: " .. (SilentAim.CurrentMethod and SilentAim.CurrentMethod.Name or "Unknown"))
end

function SilentAim.disable()
    SilentAim.Active = false
    SilentAim.CurrentMethod = nil
    Console.log("info", "Silent Aim disabled")
end

TrashTalk.LastSend = 0
TrashTalk.Messages = {
    Friendly = {
        "GG {name}!", "Nice try!", "Good fight!", "Well played!", "Better luck next time!",
        "That was close!", "You almost had me!", "Great effort!", "Keep it up!", "You're getting better!",

        "VAPORIZED!", "SENT TO THE SHADOW REALM!", "YOU'RE FINISHED!", "GAME OVER FOR YOU!", "ABSOLUTELY BODIED {name}!",
        "RATIO!", "HOLD THAT L!", "IMAGINE BEING THIS BAD!", "YOU'RE A DISAPPOINTMENT!",
    },
}

TrashTalk.Bypass = {
    None = function(msg) return msg end,
    Unicode = function(msg)
        local result = ""
        for i = 1, #msg do
            local c = msg:sub(i, i)
            if math.random() > 0.5 and c:match("%a") then
                result = result .. c .. "\u{200B}" -- Zero-width space
            else
                result = result .. c
            end
        end
        return result
    end,
    ["Zero-Width"] = function(msg)
        local result = ""
        for i = 1, #msg do
            result = result .. msg:sub(i, i) .. "\u{200C}"
        end
        return result
    end,
    Alternating = function(msg)
        local result = ""
        for i = 1, #msg do
            local c = msg:sub(i, i)
            if i % 2 == 0 then
                result = result .. c:upper()
            else
                result = result .. c:lower()
            end
        end
        return result
    end,
}

function TrashTalk.send(targetName)
    if not (State.Flags.TTOn and State.Flags.TTOn:Get()) then return end
    local now = tick()
    local cd = State.Flags.TTCD and State.Flags.TTCD:Get() or 10
    if now - TrashTalk.LastSend < cd then return end

    local intensity = State.Flags.TTInt and State.Flags.TTInt:Get() or "Friendly"
    local useCustom = State.Flags.TTCustOnly and State.Flags.TTCustOnly:Get()
    local customText = State.Flags.TTCustom and State.Flags.TTCustom:Get() or ""
    local includeName = State.Flags.TTName and State.Flags.TTName:Get()
    local bypassMethod = State.Flags.TTBypass and State.Flags.TTBypass:Get() or "None"

    local msg
    if useCustom and customText ~= "" then
        local lines = {}
        for line in customText:gmatch("[^\n]+") do table.insert(lines, line) end
        if #lines > 0 then msg = lines[math.random(#lines)] end
    end

    if not msg then
        local pool = TrashTalk.Messages[intensity]
        if pool and #pool > 0 then
            msg = pool[math.random(#pool)]
        end
    end

    if not msg then return end

    -- Replace {name}
    if includeName and targetName then
        msg = msg:gsub("{name}", targetName)
    else
        msg = msg:gsub("{name}", "")
        msg = msg:gsub("  ", " "):gsub("^ ", ""):gsub(" $", "")
    end

    -- Apply bypass
    local bypassFunc = TrashTalk.Bypass[bypassMethod]
    if bypassFunc then msg = bypassFunc(msg) end

    -- Send message
    pcall(function()
        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
            :FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest"):FireServer(msg, "All")
    end)

    -- Fallback
    pcall(function()
        game:GetService("TextChatService"):FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
            :FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral"):SendAsync(msg)
    end)

    TrashTalk.LastSend = now
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 8: CROSSHAIR BUILDER
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local function BuildCrosshair()
    local old = Lib.ScreenGui:FindFirstChild("Crosshair")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    if not (State.Flags.XOn and State.Flags.XOn:Get()) then return end

    local style = State.Flags.XSt and State.Flags.XSt:Get() or "Cross"
    local size = State.Flags.XSz and State.Flags.XSz:Get() or 10

    local ch = Create("Frame", {
        Name = "Crosshair",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size * 2, size * 2),
        ZIndex = 9999,
        Parent = Lib.ScreenGui,
    })

    if style == "Cross" or style == "Cross+Dot" then
        Create("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.White, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2), ZIndex = 10000, Parent = ch})
        Create("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.White, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0), ZIndex = 10000, Parent = ch})
    end
    if style == "Dot" or style == "Cross+Dot" then
        local d = Create("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.White, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4), ZIndex = 10001, Parent = ch})
        AddCorner(d, UDim.new(1, 0))
    end
    if style == "Circle" then
        local c = Create("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size * 2, size * 2), ZIndex = 10000, Parent = ch})
        AddCorner(c, UDim.new(1, 0))
        AddStroke(c, Theme.White, 1.5, 0)
    end
    if style == "Diamond" then
        local d = Create("Frame", {BackgroundColor3 = Theme.White, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size * 0.7, size * 0.7), Rotation = 45, ZIndex = 10000, Parent = ch})
        AddCorner(d, UDim.new(0, 1))
    end
end

-- Watch crosshair settings
task.spawn(function()
    local lastOn, lastSt, lastSz = false, "", 0
    while State.Alive and not State.Destroying do
        local cOn = State.Flags.XOn and State.Flags.XOn:Get() or false
        local cSt = State.Flags.XSt and State.Flags.XSt:Get() or "Cross"
        local cSz = State.Flags.XSz and State.Flags.XSz:Get() or 10
        if cOn ~= lastOn or cSt ~= lastSt or cSz ~= lastSz then
            lastOn, lastSt, lastSz = cOn, cSt, cSz
            BuildCrosshair()
        end
        task.wait(0.3)
    end
end)

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 9: FOV CIRCLE
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local FOVCircle = NewDrawing("Circle")
if FOVCircle then
    FOVCircle.Visible = false
    FOVCircle.Radius = 80
    FOVCircle.Color = Theme.Accent
    FOVCircle.Thickness = 1
    FOVCircle.NumSides = 64
    FOVCircle.Filled = false
end

local SilentFOVCircle = NewDrawing("Circle")
if SilentFOVCircle then
    SilentFOVCircle.Visible = false
    SilentFOVCircle.Radius = 150
    SilentFOVCircle.Color = Theme.Bad
    SilentFOVCircle.Thickness = 1
    SilentFOVCircle.NumSides = 48
    SilentFOVCircle.Filled = false
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 10: WATERMARK
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local WatermarkFrame = Create("Frame", {
    Name = "Watermark",
    BackgroundColor3 = Theme.BG,
    BackgroundTransparency = 0.3,
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 22),
    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10),
    Visible = false,
    ZIndex = 500,
    Parent = Lib.ScreenGui,
})
AddCorner(WatermarkFrame, UDim.new(0, 4))
AddStroke(WatermarkFrame, Theme.Accent, 1, 0.5)

Create("TextLabel", {
    Text = "LvyuFlo v4.0 | -- FPS",
    Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
    TextSize = 11,
    TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
    ZIndex = 501,
    Parent = WatermarkFrame,
})

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 11: MAIN RENDER LOOP
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local espTimer = 0
local fpsUpdateTimer = 0
local freezeTimeTimer = 0

TrackConnection(RS.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    if not State.Alive or State.Destroying then return end

    espTimer = espTimer + dt
    fpsUpdateTimer = fpsUpdateTimer + dt

    -- Determine update rate
    local updateRate = State.Flags.ESPFPS and State.Flags.ESPFPS:Get() or "Every 2"
    local espInterval = updateRate == "Every Frame" and 0 or updateRate == "Every 3" and (3/60) or (2/60)
    local doESP = espTimer >= espInterval
    if doESP then espTimer = 0 end

    -- ═══════ ESP UPDATE ═══════
    local espEnabled = State.Flags.ESP and State.Flags.ESP:Get()
    local showBox = State.Flags.ESPBox and State.Flags.ESPBox:Get()
    local boxStyle = State.Flags.ESPBT and State.Flags.ESPBT:Get() or "Corner"
    local boxThick = State.Flags.ESPBTh and State.Flags.ESPBTh:Get() or 1
    local showName = State.Flags.ESPN and State.Flags.ESPN:Get()
    local showHealth = State.Flags.ESPH and State.Flags.ESPH:Get()
    local showDist = State.Flags.ESPD and State.Flags.ESPD:Get()
    local showTracer = State.Flags.ESPTr and State.Flags.ESPTr:Get()
    local showSkel = State.Flags.ESPSkel and State.Flags.ESPSkel:Get()
    local showChams = State.Flags.ESPChams and State.Flags.ESPChams:Get()
    local showArmor = State.Flags.ESPA and State.Flags.ESPA:Get()
    local teamCheck = State.Flags.ESPTC and State.Flags.ESPTC:Get()
    local maxDist = State.Flags.ESPMD and State.Flags.ESPMD:Get() or 1000
    local showOutline = State.Flags.ESPBOut and State.Flags.ESPBOut:Get()
    local showWeapon = State.Flags.ESPW and State.Flags.ESPW:Get()
    local showFlags = State.Flags.ESPFlags and State.Flags.ESPFlags:Get()
    local showHPNum = State.Flags.ESPHNum and State.Flags.ESPHNum:Get()
    local hpFormat = State.Flags.ESPHNumF and State.Flags.ESPHNumF:Get() or "Percent"
    local hpPos = State.Flags.ESPHPos and State.Flags.ESPHPos:Get() or "Left"
    local visColoring = State.Flags.ESPVisCo and State.Flags.ESPVisCo:Get()
    local showArrows = State.Flags.ESPOSA and State.Flags.ESPOSA:Get()
    local rainbow = State.Flags.ESPRB and State.Flags.ESPRB:Get()
    local rbSpeed = State.Flags.ESPRS and State.Flags.ESPRS:Get() or 1

    local enemyColor = State.Flags.ESPBCe and State.Flags.ESPBCe:Get() or Theme.Bad
    local teamColor = State.Flags.ESPBCt and State.Flags.ESPBCt:Get() or Theme.Good
    local visibleColor = State.Flags.ESPVisC and State.Flags.ESPVisC:Get() or Theme.Good
    local hiddenColor = State.Flags.ESPHidC and State.Flags.ESPHidC:Get() or Theme.Bad
    local armorColor = State.Flags.ESPAC and State.Flags.ESPAC:Get() or Theme.Armor
    local chamsFill = State.Flags.ESPChamsFill and State.Flags.ESPChamsFill:Get() or Theme.Accent
    local chamsOut = State.Flags.ESPChamsOut and State.Flags.ESPChamsOut:Get() or Theme.White
    local chamsFillT = (State.Flags.ESPChamsFillT and State.Flags.ESPChamsFillT:Get() or 50) / 100
    local chamsDepth = State.Flags.ESPChamsD and State.Flags.ESPChamsD:Get() or "AlwaysOnTop"

    local myRoot = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local viewport = Cam.ViewportSize
    local center = GetViewportCenter()

    for plr, esp in pairs(State.ESP) do
        local show = false
        local char, hum, root, dist, hp, col

        if espEnabled and doESP then
            char = plr.Character
            if char then
                hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hum and root and myRoot and hum.Health > 0 then
                    dist = (root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist <= maxDist then
                        if not teamCheck or not plr.Team or not LP.Team or plr.Team ~= LP.Team then
                            show = true
                            hp = math.clamp(hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth, 0, 1)

                            if rainbow then
                                col = Color3.fromHSV((tick() * rbSpeed) % 1, 1, 1)
                            elseif visColoring then
                                local isVisible = Aimbot.isPartVisible(Cam.CFrame.Position, root.Position, char)
                                col = isVisible and visibleColor or hiddenColor
                            else
                                col = (plr.Team == LP.Team) and teamColor or enemyColor
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if show then
            esp.BB.Enabled = true
            esp.BB.Adornee = root

            -- Name label
            if doESP then
                esp.NameLabel.Visible = showName
                if showName then
                    local displayName = (State.Flags.ESPNDN and State.Flags.ESPNDN:Get()) and plr.DisplayName or plr.Name
                    local nameSize = State.Flags.ESPNSz and State.Flags.ESPNSz:Get() or 13
                    esp.NameLabel.Text = displayName
                    esp.NameLabel.TextSize = nameSize
                    esp.NameLabel.TextColor3 = col
                end

                -- Distance
                esp.DistLabel.Visible = showDist
                if showDist then esp.DistLabel.Text = math.floor(dist) .. "m" end

                -- Info line (weapon, flags)
                local infoText = ""
                if showWeapon then
                    local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                    if tool then infoText = "🔫 " .. tool.Name end
                end
                if showFlags then
                    -- Looking at you check
                    local lookFlag = State.Flags.ESPFLook and State.Flags.ESPFLook:Get()
                    if lookFlag and char:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                        local headCF = char.Head.CFrame
                        local dirToMe = (myRoot.Position - headCF.Position).Unit
                        local lookDir = headCF.LookVector
                        if dirToMe:Dot(lookDir) > 0.85 then
                            infoText = infoText .. " 👁️"
                        end
                    end
                    if hp < 0.25 then infoText = infoText .. " ❤️" end
                end
                esp.InfoLabel.Visible = infoText ~= ""
                esp.InfoLabel.Text = infoText

                -- HP numbers
                if showHPNum and hum then
                    if hpFormat == "Percent" then
                        esp.HPLabel.Text = math.floor(hp * 100) .. "%"
                    elseif hpFormat == "Current" then
                        esp.HPLabel.Text = math.floor(hum.Health)
                    elseif hpFormat == "Current/Max" then
                        esp.HPLabel.Text = math.floor(hum.Health) .. "/" .. math.floor(hum.MaxHealth)
                    end
                    esp.HPLabel.Visible = true
                    esp.HPLabel.TextColor3 = hp > 0.6 and Theme.HealthHigh or hp > 0.3 and Theme.HealthMid or Theme.HealthLow
                else
                    esp.HPLabel.Visible = false
                end
            end

            -- Screen calculations for Drawing elements
            local topPos = (root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)).Position
            local botPos = (root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -3, 0)).Position
            local ts, tOn = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(topPos)
            local bs, bOn = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(botPos)
            local onScreen = tOn and bOn

            if onScreen then
                local boxH = math.abs(ts.Y - bs.Y)
                local boxW = boxH * 0.6
                local cx = (ts.X + bs.X) / 2
                local x1, x2, y1, y2 = cx - boxW / 2, cx + boxW / 2, ts.Y, bs.Y

                -- Box ESP
                if showBox and esp.Box and esp.Box[1] then
                    if boxStyle == "Corner" then
                        local cs = math.min(boxW, boxH) * 0.25
                        local corners = {
                            {Vector2.new(x1, y1), Vector2.new(x1 + cs, y1)},
                            {Vector2.new(x1, y1), Vector2.new(x1, y1 + cs)},
                            {Vector2.new(x2, y1), Vector2.new(x2 - cs, y1)},
                            {Vector2.new(x2, y1), Vector2.new(x2, y1 + cs)},
                            {Vector2.new(x1, y2), Vector2.new(x1 + cs, y2)},
                            {Vector2.new(x1, y2), Vector2.new(x1, y2 - cs)},
                            {Vector2.new(x2, y2), Vector2.new(x2 - cs, y2)},
                            {Vector2.new(x2, y2), Vector2.new(x2, y2 - cs)},
                        }
                        for j = 1, 8 do
                            if esp.Box[j] then
                                esp.Box[j].From = corners[j][1]; esp.Box[j].To = corners[j][2]
                                esp.Box[j].Color = col; esp.Box[j].Thickness = boxThick; esp.Box[j].Visible = true
                            end
                            if showOutline and esp.BoxOutline[j] then
                                esp.BoxOutline[j].From = corners[j][1]; esp.BoxOutline[j].To = corners[j][2]
                                esp.BoxOutline[j].Thickness = boxThick + 2; esp.BoxOutline[j].Visible = true
                            end
                        end
                    elseif boxStyle == "Full" then
                        local edges = {
                            {Vector2.new(x1, y1), Vector2.new(x2, y1)},
                            {Vector2.new(x2, y1), Vector2.new(x2, y2)},
                            {Vector2.new(x2, y2), Vector2.new(x1, y2)},
                            {Vector2.new(x1, y2), Vector2.new(x1, y1)},
                        }
                        for j = 1, 4 do
                            if esp.Box[j] then
                                esp.Box[j].From = edges[j][1]; esp.Box[j].To = edges[j][2]
                                esp.Box[j].Color = col; esp.Box[j].Thickness = boxThick; esp.Box[j].Visible = true
                            end
                            if showOutline and esp.BoxOutline[j] then
                                esp.BoxOutline[j].From = edges[j][1]; esp.BoxOutline[j].To = edges[j][2]
                                esp.BoxOutline[j].Thickness = boxThick + 2; esp.BoxOutline[j].Visible = true
                            end
                        end
                        for j = 5, 8 do
                            if esp.Box[j] then esp.Box[j].Visible = false end
                            if esp.BoxOutline[j] then esp.BoxOutline[j].Visible = false end
                        end
                    end
                elseif esp.Box then
                    for j = 1, 8 do
                        if esp.Box[j] then esp.Box[j].Visible = false end
                        if esp.BoxOutline and esp.BoxOutline[j] then esp.BoxOutline[j].Visible = false end
                    end
                end

                -- Health bar
                if showHealth and esp.HBarBg and esp.HBar then
                    local hx = hpPos == "Left" and (x1 - 6) or (x2 + 4)
                    esp.HBarBg.From = Vector2.new(hx, y2); esp.HBarBg.To = Vector2.new(hx, y1); esp.HBarBg.Visible = true
                    esp.HBar.From = Vector2.new(hx, y2); esp.HBar.To = Vector2.new(hx, y2 - (y2 - y1) * hp); esp.HBar.Visible = true
                    esp.HBar.Color = hp > 0.6 and Theme.HealthHigh or hp > 0.3 and Theme.HealthMid or Theme.HealthLow
                else
                    if esp.HBar then esp.HBar.Visible = false end
                    if esp.HBarBg then esp.HBarBg.Visible = false end
                end

                -- Armor bar
                if showArmor and esp.ABarBg and esp.ABar then
                    local armor = GetArmor(plr)
                    if armor then
                        local hpSide = hpPos == "Left"
                        local ax = hpSide and (x1 - 10) or (x2 + 8)
                        local ap = math.clamp(armor / 100, 0, 1)
                        esp.ABarBg.From = Vector2.new(ax, y2); esp.ABarBg.To = Vector2.new(ax, y1); esp.ABarBg.Visible = true
                        esp.ABar.From = Vector2.new(ax, y2); esp.ABar.To = Vector2.new(ax, y2 - (y2 - y1) * ap)
                        esp.ABar.Color = armorColor; esp.ABar.Visible = true
                    else
                        esp.ABar.Visible = false; esp.ABarBg.Visible = false
                    end
                else
                    if esp.ABar then esp.ABar.Visible = false end
                    if esp.ABarBg then esp.ABarBg.Visible = false end
                end

                -- Tracers
                if showTracer and esp.Tracer then
                    local origin = State.Flags.ESPTrO and State.Flags.ESPTrO:Get() or "Bottom"
                    local tracerThick = State.Flags.ESPTrTh and State.Flags.ESPTrTh:Get() or 1
                    local fromPos
                    if origin == "Bottom" then
                        fromPos = Vector2.new(viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y)
                    elseif origin == "Center" then
                        fromPos = center
                    elseif origin == "Mouse" then
                        local mPos = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
                        fromPos = Vector2.new(mPos.X, mPos.Y)
                    else
                        fromPos = Vector2.new(viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y)
                    end
                    esp.Tracer.From = fromPos
                    esp.Tracer.To = Vector2.new(bs.X, bs.Y)
                    esp.Tracer.Color = col
                    esp.Tracer.Thickness = tracerThick
                    esp.Tracer.Visible = true
                else
                    if esp.Tracer then esp.Tracer.Visible = false end
                end

                -- Skeleton
                if showSkel and esp.Skeleton and #esp.Skeleton > 0 then
                    local skelThick = State.Flags.ESPSkelTh and State.Flags.ESPSkelTh:Get() or 1
                    for j, conn in ipairs(SKELETON_CONNECTIONS) do
                        if esp.Skeleton[j] then
                            local p1 = char:FindFirstChild(conn[1])
                            local p2 = char:FindFirstChild(conn[2])
                            if p1 and p2 then
                                local s1, o1 = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(p1.Position)
                                local s2, o2 = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(p2.Position)
                                if o1 and o2 then
                                    esp.Skeleton[j].From = Vector2.new(s1.X, s1.Y)
                                    esp.Skeleton[j].To = Vector2.new(s2.X, s2.Y)
                                    esp.Skeleton[j].Color = col
                                    esp.Skeleton[j].Thickness = skelThick
                                    esp.Skeleton[j].Visible = true
                                else
                                    esp.Skeleton[j].Visible = false
                                end
                            else
                                esp.Skeleton[j].Visible = false
                            end
                        end
                    end
                elseif esp.Skeleton then
                    for j = 1, #SKELETON_CONNECTIONS do
                        if esp.Skeleton[j] then esp.Skeleton[j].Visible = false end
                    end
                end

                -- Hide off-screen arrow when on screen
                if esp.Arrow then esp.Arrow.Visible = false end

            else
                -- Off screen
                if esp.Box then for j = 1, 8 do if esp.Box[j] then esp.Box[j].Visible = false end end end
                if esp.BoxOutline then for j = 1, 8 do if esp.BoxOutline[j] then esp.BoxOutline[j].Visible = false end end end
                if esp.HBar then esp.HBar.Visible = false end
                if esp.HBarBg then esp.HBarBg.Visible = false end
                if esp.ABar then esp.ABar.Visible = false end
                if esp.ABarBg then esp.ABarBg.Visible = false end
                if esp.Tracer then esp.Tracer.Visible = false end
                if esp.Skeleton then for j = 1, #SKELETON_CONNECTIONS do if esp.Skeleton[j] then esp.Skeleton[j].Visible = false end end end

                -- Off-screen arrows
                if showArrows and esp.Arrow and root then
                    local arrowSize = State.Flags.ESPOSASz and State.Flags.ESPOSASz:Get() or 14
                    local sp = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
                    local dir = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - center).Unit
                    local edgeDist = math.min(viewport.X / 2 - 30, viewport.Y / 2 - 30)
                    local arrowPos = center + dir * edgeDist
                    local perp = Vector2.new(-dir.Y, dir.X)

                    esp.Arrow.PointA = arrowPos + dir * arrowSize
                    esp.Arrow.PointB = arrowPos - dir * (arrowSize * 0.5) + perp * (arrowSize * 0.4)
                    esp.Arrow.PointC = arrowPos - dir * (arrowSize * 0.5) - perp * (arrowSize * 0.4)
                    esp.Arrow.Color = col
                    esp.Arrow.Visible = true
                elseif esp.Arrow then
                    esp.Arrow.Visible = false
                end
            end

            -- Chams (Highlight instances)
            if showChams then
                if not esp.Highlight or not esp.Highlight.Parent then
                    esp.Highlight = Create("Highlight", {
                        FillColor = chamsFill,
                        OutlineColor = chamsOut,
                        FillTransparency = chamsFillT,
                        OutlineTransparency = 0,
                        DepthMode = chamsDepth == "AlwaysOnTop" and Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop or Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded,
                        Adornee = char,
                        Parent = char,
                    })
                else
                    esp.Highlight.FillColor = chamsFill
                    esp.Highlight.OutlineColor = chamsOut
                    esp.Highlight.FillTransparency = chamsFillT
                end
            else
                if esp.Highlight then
                    pcall(game.Destroy, esp.Highlight)
                    esp.Highlight = nil
                end
            end

        else
            HideAllESP(esp)
            if esp.Highlight then pcall(game.Destroy, esp.Highlight); esp.Highlight = nil end
        end
    end

    -- ═══════ FOV CIRCLE ═══════
    if FOVCircle then
        local showFOV = State.Flags.ASFOV and State.Flags.ASFOV:Get()
        FOVCircle.Position = center
        FOVCircle.Radius = State.Flags.AFOV and State.Flags.AFOV:Get() or 80
        local op = State.Flags.AFOp and State.Flags.AFOp:Get() or 60
        FOVCircle.Transparency = 1 - (op / 100)
        FOVCircle.Color = Theme.Accent
        local shape = State.Flags.AFShape and State.Flags.AFShape:Get() or "Circle"
        FOVCircle.NumSides = shape == "Square" and 4 or 64
        FOVCircle.Visible = showFOV == true
    end

    if SilentFOVCircle then
        local showSilentFOV = State.Flags.ASilentSFOV and State.Flags.ASilentSFOV:Get()
        SilentFOVCircle.Position = center
        SilentFOVCircle.Radius = State.Flags.ASilentFOV and State.Flags.ASilentFOV:Get() or 150
        SilentFOVCircle.Visible = showSilentFOV == true and (State.Flags.ASilent and State.Flags.ASilent:Get())
    end

    -- Dynamic FOV
    if State.Flags.AFDyn and State.Flags.AFDyn:Get() and FOVCircle then
        if Aimbot.IsActive and Aimbot.CurrentTarget then
            FOVCircle.Radius = math.max(30, FOVCircle.Radius - dt * 100)
        end
    end

    -- ═══════ AIMBOT UPDATE ═══════
    Aimbot.IsActive = Aimbot.isAimActive()

    if Aimbot.IsActive then
        local target = Aimbot.getTarget()

        -- Sticky target logic
        if State.Flags.AStick and State.Flags.AStick:Get() then
            if Aimbot.StickyTarget then
                -- Check if sticky target still valid
                local sChar = Aimbot.StickyTarget.Character
                if sChar and sChar.Parent then
                    local sHum = sChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if sHum and sHum.Health > 0 then
                        target = Aimbot.StickyTarget
                        Aimbot.StickyLockTime = Aimbot.StickyLockTime + dt
                    else
                        Aimbot.StickyTarget = nil
                    end
                else
                    Aimbot.StickyTarget = nil
                end
            end

            if target and target ~= Aimbot.StickyTarget then
                local switchDelay = (State.Flags.ATSwD and State.Flags.ATSwD:Get() or 50) / 1000
                if tick() - Aimbot.LastTargetTime >= switchDelay then
                    Aimbot.StickyTarget = target
                    Aimbot.StickyLockTime = 0
                    Aimbot.LastTargetTime = tick()
                end
            end
        end

        if target then
            Aimbot.CurrentTarget = target
            local pos = Aimbot.predictPosition(target.Part, target.Character)

            -- Aim shake
            local shake = State.Flags.AShake and State.Flags.AShake:Get() or 0
            if shake > 0 then
                pos = pos + Vector3.new(
                    (math.random() - 0.5) * shake * 0.1,
                    (math.random() - 0.5) * shake * 0.1,
                    (math.random() - 0.5) * shake * 0.1
                )
            end

            -- Smoothing
            local smoothVal = State.Flags.ASm and State.Flags.ASm:Get() or 30
            local curveName = State.Flags.ASmCrv and State.Flags.ASmCrv:Get() or "Exponential"

            -- Distance smoothing
            if State.Flags.ADistSm and State.Flags.ADistSm:Get() and target.Distance then
                local distFactor = math.clamp(target.Distance / 500, 1, 2)
                smoothVal = math.clamp(smoothVal * distFactor, 1, 180)
            end

            local alpha = Aimbot.calculateSmoothing(smoothVal, dt, curveName)
            local targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Cam.CFrame.Position, pos)

            -- Apply aim based on method
            local method = State.Flags.AM and State.Flags.AM:Get() or "Camera"

            if method == "Camera" then
                Cam.CFrame = Cam.CFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, alpha)
            elseif method == "Mouse Move" then
                if Ex.MouseMoveRel then
                    local sp = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
                    local deltaX = (sp.X - center.X) * alpha
                    local deltaY = (sp.Y - center.Y) * alpha
                    mousemoverel(deltaX, deltaY)
                else
                    -- Fallback to camera
                    Cam.CFrame = Cam.CFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, alpha)
                end
            elseif method == "Hybrid" then
                Cam.CFrame = Cam.CFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, alpha)
                if Ex.MouseMoveRel then
                    local sp = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
                    local deltaX = (sp.X - center.X) * alpha * 0.3
                    local deltaY = (sp.Y - center.Y) * alpha * 0.3
                    mousemoverel(deltaX, deltaY)
                end
            end
        else
            Aimbot.CurrentTarget = nil
        end
    else
        Aimbot.CurrentTarget = nil
        Aimbot.StickyTarget = nil
    end

    -- ═══════ NOCLIP ═══════
    local noclipActive = false
    local flyOn = State.Flags.FlyOn and State.Flags.FlyOn:Get()
    local ncOn = State.Flags.NCOn and State.Flags.NCOn:Get()
    local flyNC = State.Flags.FlyNC and State.Flags.FlyNC:Get()

    if flyOn and flyNC then noclipActive = true
    elseif ncOn and not flyOn then noclipActive = true end

    if noclipActive then
        pcall(function()
            for _, part in ipairs(LP.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = false end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- ═══════ FLY ═══════
    if flyOn then
        pcall(function()
            local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if root and hum then
                local flySpeed = State.Flags.FlySpd and State.Flags.FlySpd:Get() or 50
                local moveDir = Vector3.zero

                if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then moveDir = moveDir + Cam.CFrame.LookVector end
                if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then moveDir = moveDir - Cam.CFrame.LookVector end
                if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then moveDir = moveDir - Cam.CFrame.RightVector end
                if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then moveDir = moveDir + Cam.CFrame.RightVector end
                if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then moveDir = moveDir + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
                if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then moveDir = moveDir - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end

                if moveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
                    moveDir = moveDir.Unit
                end

                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = moveDir * flySpeed
            end
        end)
    end

    -- ═══════ FREEZE TIME ═══════
    if State.Flags.FrT and State.Flags.FrT:Get() then
        pcall(function()
            Ltg.ClockTime = State.Flags.ToD and State.Flags.ToD:Get() or 14
        end)
    end

    -- ═══════ ANTI-VOID ═══════
    if State.Flags.AVoid and State.Flags.AVoid:Get() then
        pcall(function()
            local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root and root.Position.Y < -200 then
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position.X, 100, root.Position.Z)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        end)
    end

    -- ═══════ ANTI-FLING ═══════
    if State.Flags.AFling and State.Flags.AFling:Get() then
        pcall(function()
            local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root then
                local vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                if vel.Magnitude > 200 then
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- ═══════ BUNNY HOP ═══════
    if State.Flags.BHop and State.Flags.BHop:Get() then
        pcall(function()
            if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
                local hum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running then
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- ═══════ WATERMARK ═══════
    if State.Flags.WM and State.Flags.WM:Get() then
        WatermarkFrame.Visible = true
        if fpsUpdateTimer >= 1 then
            fpsUpdateTimer = 0
            local wmLabel = WatermarkFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
            if wmLabel then
                wmLabel.Text = "LvyuFlo v4.0 | " .. (FPSBooster.Stats.CurrentFPS > 0 and FPSBooster.Stats.CurrentFPS or "--") .. " FPS"
            end
        end
    else
        WatermarkFrame.Visible = false
    end

    -- ═══════ FPS BOOSTER STATS ═══════
    if fpsUpdateTimer >= 1 and fpsStatsLabel then
        local before = FPSBooster.Stats.StartFPS
        local current = FPSBooster.Stats.CurrentFPS
        local imp = FPSBooster.Stats.Improvement
        if current > 0 then
            fpsStatsLabel:Set(string.format("Before: %d | Now: %d | %+d FPS", before, current, imp),
                imp > 0 and Theme.Good or Theme.SubText)
        end
    end
end))

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 12: OTHER EVENT HANDLERS
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

-- Infinite Jump
TrackConnection(UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
    if State.Flags.IJ and State.Flags.IJ:Get() and LP.Character then
        local hum = LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
    end
end))

-- Click Teleport
TrackConnection(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    if State.Flags.CTP and State.Flags.CTP:Get() and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then
        if Mse.Target and LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            LP.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(Mse.Hit.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
        end
    end
end))

-- Anti-AFK
pcall(function()
    local VU = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
    TrackConnection(LP.Idled:Connect(function()
        if State.Flags.AAFK and State.Flags.AAFK:Get() then
            VU:CaptureController()
            VU:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
        end
    end))
end)

-- Character Added (re-apply settings)
TrackConnection(LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.5)
    pcall(function()
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            hum.WalkSpeed = State.Flags.WS and State.Flags.WS:Get() or 16
            hum.JumpPower = State.Flags.JP and State.Flags.JP:Get() or 50
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        Cam.FieldOfView = State.Flags.FOV and State.Flags.FOV:Get() or 70
    end)

    -- Auto-respawn
    if State.Flags.ARespawn and State.Flags.ARespawn:Get() then
        -- This relies on game-specific respawn mechanics
        -- Basic implementation: wait and check
    end
end))

-- Silent Aim toggle watcher
task.spawn(function()
    local lastSilent = false
    while State.Alive and not State.Destroying do
        local currentSilent = State.Flags.ASilent and State.Flags.ASilent:Get()
        if currentSilent ~= lastSilent then
            lastSilent = currentSilent
            if currentSilent then
                SilentAim.enable()
            else
                SilentAim.disable()
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.5)
    end
end)

-- Trash Talk keybind trigger
TrackConnection(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    local trigKey = State.Flags.TTKey and State.Flags.TTKey:Get()
    local trigger = State.Flags.TTTrig and State.Flags.TTTrig:Get()
    if trigger == "Keybind" and trigKey and input.KeyCode == trigKey then
        TrashTalk.send("enemy")
    end
end))

-- Trash Talk timer
task.spawn(function()
    while State.Alive and not State.Destroying do
        task.wait(1)
        if State.Flags.TTOn and State.Flags.TTOn:Get() then
            local trigger = State.Flags.TTTrig and State.Flags.TTTrig:Get()
            if trigger == "Timer" then
                TrashTalk.send("everyone")
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 13: STARTUP
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

-- Load default config silently
if Ex.FileSystem then
    pcall(function()
        local allData = Config.loadAll()
        if allData and allData.profiles and allData.profiles.Default then
            Config.loadProfile("Default")
        end
    end)
end

-- Start auto-save
Config.startAutoSave()

-- Show loading screen then reveal UI
Lib.ShowLoadingScreen(function()
    Lib.MainFrame.Visible = true
    Lib.MainFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    task.spawn(function()
        Lib.SpringOpen(Lib.MainFrame, Lib.WindowSize, 0.5)
    end)

    task.delay(0.8, function()
        Notify({
            Title = "LvyuFlo Ready",
            Content = 'Press "' .. State.ToggleKey.Name .. '" to toggle | Insert = panic',
            Type = "Success",
            Duration = 5,
        })
    end)
end)

Console.log("success", "Part 2 loaded — All systems active")

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 14: RETURN
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

return {
    Destroy = Lib.Destroy,
    Notify = Notify,
    Console = Console,
    State = State,
    Library = Lib,
    Aimbot = Aimbot,
    SilentAim = SilentAim,
    TrashTalk = TrashTalk,
    FPSBooster = FPSBooster,
}

-- SECTION 4: CONSOLE (runs before UI)

local Console = {
    Logs = {},
    Max = 300,
    Visible = false,
    UIRefresh = nil,
}

Console.Types = {
    error   = {Icon = "X", Label = "ERROR", Color = Color3.fromRGB(240, 80, 80)},
    warning = {Icon = "!", Label = "WARN",  Color = Color3.fromRGB(250, 180, 50)},
    info    = {Icon = "i", Label = "INFO",  Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 180, 255)},
    success = {Icon = "✓", Label = "OK",    Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 120)},
    working = {Icon = "~", Label = "WORK",  Color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 255)},
}

function Console.log(ltype, msg, src)
    local entry = {
        Type = ltype or "info",
        Msg = tostring(msg or ""),
        Src = src or "System",
        Time = os.date("%H:%M:%S"),
        Tick = tick(),
    }
    table.insert(Console.Logs, 1, entry)
    while #Console.Logs > Console.Max do
        table.remove(Console.Logs)
    end
    if Console.UIRefresh then
        pcall(Console.UIRefresh)
    end
    return entry
end

function Console.clear()
    Console.Logs = {}
    if Console.UIRefresh then
        pcall(Console.UIRefresh)
    end
end

function Console.copyAll()
    if not Ex.Clip then return false end
    local lines = {"=== " .. SCRIPT_NAME .. " Console ===", ""}
    for _, l in ipairs(Console.Logs) do
        local t = Console.Types[l.Type] or Console.Types.info
        table.insert(lines, "[" .. l.Time .. "] [" .. t.Label .. "] " .. l.Msg)
    end
    pcall(setclipboard, table.concat(lines, "\n"))
    Console.log("success", "Copied to clipboard")
    return true
end

function Console.runChecks()
    Console.log("working", "Running system checks...")
    local checks = {
        {"Drawing", Ex.Draw},
        {"FileSystem", Ex.FS},
        {"Clipboard", Ex.Clip},
        {"mousemoverel", Ex.MMR},
        {"hookfunction", Ex.Hook},
        {"getrawmetatable", Ex.RawMT},
        {"setfpscap", Ex.FPSCap},
    }
    for _, c in ipairs(checks) do
        if c[2] then
            Console.log("success", c[1] .. " available")
        else
            Console.log("warning", c[1] .. " not available")
        end
    end
    Console.log("info", "Executor: " .. Ex.Name)
    Console.log("success", "Checks complete")
end

Console.runChecks()

-- SECTION 5: UTILITY FUNCTIONS

local function RndName()
    local c = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"
    local n = ""
    for i = 1, math.random(8, 14) do
        local idx = math.random(1, #c)
        n = n .. c:sub(idx, idx)
    end
    return n .. tostring(math.random(100, 999))
end

local function Mk(class, props, kids)
    local ok, obj = pcall(Instance.new, class)
    if not ok or not obj then
        Console.log("error", "Failed to create " .. tostring(class))
        return Instance.new("Frame") -- safe fallback
    end
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do
        if k ~= "Parent" then
            pcall(function() obj[k] = v end)
        end
    end
    for _, kid in ipairs(kids or {}) do
        pcall(function() kid.Parent = obj end)
    end
    if props and props.Parent then
        pcall(function() obj.Parent = props.Parent end)
    end
    return obj
end

local function Tw(obj, goal, dur, style, dir)
    if not obj then return nil end
    local ok = pcall(function() return obj.Parent end)
    if not ok then return nil end
    dur = dur or UI.ANIM
    style = style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint
    dir = dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out
    local s, tw = pcall(function()
        return TS:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(dur, style, dir), goal)
    end)
    if s and tw then
        tw:Play()
        return tw
    end
    return nil
end

local function RC(p, r)
    return Mk("UICorner", {CornerRadius = r or UDim.new(0, UI.CR), Parent = p})
end

local function ST(p, c, th, tr)
    return Mk("UIStroke", {
        Color = c or Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 60),
        Thickness = th or 1,
        Transparency = tr or 0.5,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
        Parent = p,
    })
end

local function PD(p, t, b, l, r)
    return Mk("UIPadding", {
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, t or 0),
        PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, b or 0),
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, l or 0),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, r or 0),
        Parent = p,
    })
end

local function LL(p, pd)
    return Mk("UIListLayout", {
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, pd or UI.PAD),
        Parent = p,
    })
end

local function FV(v, inc)
    if inc >= 1 then
        return tostring(math.floor(v + 0.5))
    end
    local dp = math.max(0, math.ceil(-math.log10(inc)))
    return string.format("%." .. dp .. "f", v)
end

local function VPCenter()
    local vp = Cam.ViewportSize
    local inset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    pcall(function() inset = GS:GetGuiInset() end)
    return Vector2.new(vp.X / 2, vp.Y / 2 + inset.Y / 2)
end

local function SetAccent(color)
    if not color or typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    T.Acc = color
    local h, s, v = color:ToHSV()
    T.AccH = Color3.fromHSV(h, math.max(s - 0.1, 0), math.min(v + 0.1, 1))
    T.AccD = Color3.fromHSV(h, math.min(s + 0.1, 1), math.max(v - 0.15, 0))
    T.AccL = Color3.fromHSV(h, math.max(s - 0.25, 0), math.min(v + 0.2, 1))
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
