local function SetAccent(color)
    if not color or typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    T.Acc = color
    local h, s, v = color:ToHSV()
    T.AccH = Color3.fromHSV(h, math.max(s - 0.10, 0), math.min(v + 0.10, 1))
    T.AccD = Color3.fromHSV(h, math.min(s + 0.10, 1), math.max(v - 0.15, 0))
    T.AccL = Color3.fromHSV(h, math.max(s - 0.25, 0), math.min(v + 0.20, 1))
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 8: ANIMATIONS
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local function Ripple(parent)
    if not parent then return end
    if not pcall(function() return parent.AbsoluteSize end) then return end
    local sz = math.max(parent.AbsoluteSize.X, parent.AbsoluteSize.Y) * 2.5
    local c = Mk("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3    = T.W,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.78,
        Size                = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        Position            = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        AnchorPoint         = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        ZIndex              = 100,
        Parent              = parent,
    })
    RC(c, UDim.new(1, 0))
    Tw(c, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sz, sz), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    task.delay(0.55, function() pcall(game.Destroy, c) end)
end

local function SprOpen(fr, tgt, dur)
    if not fr or not tgt then return end
    dur = dur or UI.SPRING
    fr.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    local tx, ty = tgt.X.Offset, tgt.Y.Offset

    local t1 = Tw(fr, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(tx * 1.05, ty * 1.05) }, dur * 0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
    if t1 then t1.Completed:Wait() end
    local t2 = Tw(fr, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(tx * 0.98, ty * 0.98) }, dur * 0.20, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    if t2 then t2.Completed:Wait() end
    Tw(fr, { Size = tgt }, dur * 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
end

local function SprClose(fr, dur)
    if not fr then return end
    dur = dur or UI.ANIMS
    local cs = fr.Size
    local t1 = Tw(fr, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cs.X.Offset * 1.03, cs.Y.Offset * 1.03) }, dur * 0.20, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
    if t1 then t1.Completed:Wait() end
    local t2 = Tw(fr, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }, dur * 0.80, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
    if t2 then t2.Completed:Wait() end
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 9: FPS BOOSTER
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local FPS = {
    Active  = false,
    Level   = "Moderate",
    Changes = { Sounds = {}, Particles = {}, Beams = {} },
    Stats   = { Start = 0, Current = 0, Improve = 0 },
    Levels  = {
        Conservative = { GC = 60, Snd = 600, Part = 400, PartR = 0.7, Beam = 300 },
        Moderate     = { GC = 30, Snd = 400, Part = 250, PartR = 0.5, Beam = 200 },
        Aggressive   = { GC = 15, Snd = 200, Part = 150, PartR = 0.3, Beam = 100 },
    },
}

function FPS.isDup()
    if _G[BOOSTER_KEY] or shared[BOOSTER_KEY] then
        Console.log("warning", "FPS Booster already running — skipping")
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function FPS.register()
    _G[BOOSTER_KEY]     = { active = true, time = tick() }
    shared[BOOSTER_KEY] = true
    FPS.Active          = true
end

function FPS.unregister()
    _G[BOOSTER_KEY]     = nil
    shared[BOOSTER_KEY] = nil
    FPS.Active          = false
end

function FPS.measureFPS(samples)
    samples = samples or 30
    local frames, st = 0, tick()
    for _ = 1, samples do RS.RenderStepped:Wait(); frames = frames + 1 end
    return math.floor(frames / (tick() - st))
end

function FPS.enable()
    if FPS.isDup() then return false end
    Console.log("working", "FPS Booster starting (" .. FPS.Level .. ")")
    FPS.Stats.Start = FPS.measureFPS(30)
    Console.log("info", "Baseline FPS: " .. FPS.Stats.Start)
    FPS.register()

    local lv = FPS.Levels[FPS.Level] or FPS.Levels.Moderate

    -- GC optimization
    pcall(function() collectgarbage("setpause", 100) end)
    pcall(function() collectgarbage("setstepmul", 200) end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while FPS.Active and S.Alive do
            task.wait(lv.GC)
            for _ = 1, 3 do pcall(function() collectgarbage("step", 80) end); task.wait() end
        end
    end)

    -- Physics sleep
    pcall(function() settings().Physics.AllowSleep = true end)

    -- Optimize distant sounds
    task.spawn(function()
        local count = 0
        pcall(function()
            for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("Sound") and obj.Playing then
                    local p = obj.Parent
                    if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local d = (p.Position - Cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                        if d > lv.Snd then
                            local nm = obj.Name:lower()
                            if not nm:match("music") and not nm:match("ambient") and not nm:match("bgm") then
                                table.insert(FPS.Changes.Sounds, { O = obj, V = obj.Volume })
                                obj.Volume = obj.Volume * 0.2
                                count = count + 1
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        if count > 0 then Console.log("success", count .. " distant sounds reduced") end
    end)

    -- Optimize distant particles
    task.spawn(function()
        local count = 0
        pcall(function()
            for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") and obj.Enabled then
                    local p = obj.Parent
                    if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local d = (p.Position - Cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                        if d > lv.Part then
                            table.insert(FPS.Changes.Particles, { O = obj, R = obj.Rate })
                            obj.Rate = math.max(1, obj.Rate * lv.PartR)
                            count = count + 1
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        if count > 0 then Console.log("success", count .. " particles optimized") end
    end)

    -- Optimize distant beams
    task.spawn(function()
        local count = 0
        pcall(function()
            for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("Beam") then
                    local a0 = obj.Attachment0
                    if a0 and a0.Parent and a0.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local d = (a0.Parent.Position - Cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
                        if d > lv.Beam then
                            table.insert(FPS.Changes.Beams, { O = obj, Seg = obj.Segments })
                            obj.Segments = math.max(3, math.floor(obj.Segments * 0.5))
                            count = count + 1
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        if count > 0 then Console.log("success", count .. " beams optimized") end
    end)

    -- FPS monitor
    task.spawn(function()
        local fc, st2 = 0, tick()
        while FPS.Active and S.Alive do
            RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
            fc = fc + 1
            if tick() - st2 >= 1 then
                FPS.Stats.Current = fc
                FPS.Stats.Improve = fc - FPS.Stats.Start
                fc, st2 = 0, tick()
            end
        end
    end)

    Console.log("success", "FPS Booster active!")
    return true
end

function FPS.cleanup()
    Console.log("working", "Restoring FPS changes...")
    for _, d in ipairs(FPS.Changes.Sounds) do
        pcall(function() if d.O and d.O.Parent then d.O.Volume = d.V end end)
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(FPS.Changes.Particles) do
        pcall(function() if d.O and d.O.Parent then d.O.Rate = d.R end end)
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(FPS.Changes.Beams) do
        pcall(function() if d.O and d.O.Parent then d.O.Segments = d.Seg end end)
    end
    FPS.Changes = { Sounds = {}, Particles = {}, Beams = {} }
    FPS.unregister()
    Console.log("success", "FPS Booster restored")
end

-- Auto-enable on load
task.spawn(function() FPS.enable() end)

local function UpdateNotifPos()
    local p = S.NotifPos or "BR"
    if p == "BR" then
        NHolder.Position   = UDim2.new(1, -(UI.NOTIF_W + 10), 1, -20)
        NHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
    elseif p == "BL" then
        NHolder.Position   = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -20)
        NHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
    elseif p == "TR" then
        NHolder.Position   = UDim2.new(1, -(UI.NOTIF_W + 10), 0, 20)
        NHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    elseif p == "TL" then
        NHolder.Position   = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 20)
        NHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    end
end
UpdateNotifPos()

local notifCount = 0

local function Notify(opts)
    if S.Destroying then return end
    opts = opts or {}
    if notifCount >= UI.MAX_NOTIF then return end

    local title   = opts.Title    or ""
    local content = opts.Content  or ""
    local dur     = opts.Duration or UI.NOTIF_DUR
    local ntype   = opts.Type     or "Info"
    local ac      = ({ Info = T.Acc, Success = T.Good, Warning = T.Warn, Error = T.Bad })[ntype] or T.Acc

    notifCount = notifCount + 1

    local nf = Mk("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = T.BG,
        Size             = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 72),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Parent           = NHolder,
    })
    RC(nf, UDim.new(0, 10))
    ST(nf, ac, 1, 0.4)

    Mk("Frame",     { BackgroundColor3 = ac, Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -8), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4), Parent = nf })
    Mk("TextLabel", { Text = title, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 10), Parent = nf })
    Mk("TextLabel", { Text = content, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextWrapped = true, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 28), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 32), Parent = nf })

    local pg = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = ac, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -3), Parent = nf })
    Tw(pg, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 3) }, dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)

    nf.Position = UDim2.new(1, 60, 0, 0)
    Tw(nf, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)

    task.delay(dur, function()
        notifCount = math.max(0, notifCount - 1)
        if not nf or not nf.Parent then return end
        local tw = Tw(nf, { Position = UDim2.new(1, 60, 0, 0) }, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        if tw then tw.Completed:Wait() end
        pcall(game.Destroy, nf)
    end)
end

local function Confirm(title, msg, onY, onN)
    if S.Destroying then return end

    local ov = Mk("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3    = T.Bk,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size                = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        ZIndex              = 9000,
        Parent              = SG,
    })
    Tw(ov, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.4 }, 0.25)

    local dlg = Mk("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint      = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundColor3 = T.Side,
        Position         = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size             = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        ZIndex           = 9001,
        Parent           = ov,
    })
    RC(dlg, UDim.new(0, 12))
    ST(dlg, T.Acc, 2, 0.2)

    -- Drop shadow
    local shadow = Mk("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint         = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundColor3    = T.Bk,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.6,
        Position            = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        Size                = UDim2.new(1, 20, 1, 20),
        ZIndex              = 9000,
        Parent              = dlg,
    })
    RC(shadow, UDim.new(0, 16))

    local ct = Mk("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size          = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        ZIndex        = 9002,
        Parent        = dlg,
    })
    PD(ct, 20, 20, 20, 20)
    LL(ct, 12)

    Mk("TextLabel", { Text = title, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = T.Txt, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), ZIndex = 9003, Parent = ct })
    Mk("TextLabel", { Text = msg,   Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,     TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextWrapped = true, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 9003, Parent = ct })

    local bf = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), ZIndex = 9003, Parent = ct })

    local canBtn = Mk("TextButton", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, Text = "Cancel",  TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextSize = 13, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 9004, Parent = bf })
    RC(canBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))

    local yesBtn = Mk("TextButton", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc, Position = UDim2.new(0.52, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 1, 0), Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, Text = "Confirm", TextColor3 = T.W, TextSize = 13, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 9004, Parent = bf })
    RC(yesBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))

    canBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(canBtn, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Div  }, 0.12) end)
    canBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(canBtn, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner }, 0.12) end)
    yesBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(yesBtn, { BackgroundColor3 = T.AccH }, 0.12) end)
    yesBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(yesBtn, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc  }, 0.12) end)

    task.spawn(function() SprOpen(dlg, UDim2.fromOffset(380, 0), 0.4) end)

    local closed = false
    local function cls()
        if closed then return end
        closed = true
        task.spawn(function()
            Tw(ov, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.25)
            SprClose(dlg, 0.3)
            task.wait(0.35)
            pcall(game.Destroy, ov)
        end)
    end

    canBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Ripple(canBtn); cls()
        if onN then task.spawn(onN) end
    end)
    yesBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Ripple(yesBtn); cls()
        if onY then task.spawn(onY) end
    end)

    ov.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            local m  = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
            local dp = dlg.AbsolutePosition
            local ds = dlg.AbsoluteSize
            if m.X < dp.X or m.X > dp.X + ds.X or m.Y < dp.Y or m.Y > dp.Y + ds.Y then
                cls()
                if onN then task.spawn(onN) end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 14: DESTROY / SELF-CLEANER
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local function DestroyAll(panic)
    if S.Destroying then return end
    S.Destroying = true
    S.Alive      = false

    Console.log("working", panic and "PANIC CLEANUP" or "Cleanup starting...")

    -- NOTE: Config save happens in Part 2 via _G callback
    if _G.LvyuFlo_SaveConfig and not panic then
        pcall(_G.LvyuFlo_SaveConfig)
    end

    task.wait(panic and 0 or 0.1)

    -- Disconnect connections
    local cnt = 0
    for _, c in pairs(S.Conns) do
        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
        cnt = cnt + 1
        if not panic and cnt % 30 == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    S.Conns = {}

    -- Remove drawings
    cnt = 0
    for _, d in ipairs(S.Draws) do
        pcall(function() d.Visible = false end)
        pcall(function() d:Remove() end)
        cnt = cnt + 1
        if not panic and cnt % 20 == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    S.Draws = {}

    -- Destroy ESP instances
    for _, esp in pairs(S.ESP) do
        pcall(function()
            if esp.Folder then esp.Folder:Destroy() end
        end)
    end
    S.ESP = {}

    -- Restore environment
    pcall(function() Ltg.Ambient      = S.Orig.Ambient end)
    pcall(function() Ltg.Brightness   = S.Orig.Bright end)
    pcall(function() Ltg.FogEnd       = S.Orig.FogEnd end)
    pcall(function() Ltg.FogStart     = S.Orig.FogStart or 0 end)
    pcall(function() Ltg.GlobalShadows = S.Orig.Shadows end)
    pcall(function() if S.Orig.Clock then Ltg.ClockTime = S.Orig.Clock end end)
    pcall(function() Cam.FieldOfView  = S.Orig.FOV end)
    pcall(function() workspace.Gravity = S.Orig.Grav or 196 end)

    -- Restore character
    pcall(function()
        if LP.Character then
            local hum = LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                hum.WalkSpeed  = 16
                hum.JumpPower  = 50
            end
            for _, p in pairs(LP.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                    p.CanCollide = true
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- FPS Booster cleanup
    pcall(function() FPS.cleanup() end)

    -- Clear globals
    _G[BOOSTER_KEY]         = nil
    shared[BOOSTER_KEY]     = nil
    _G.LvyuFlo_Lib          = nil
    _G.LvyuFlo_SaveConfig   = nil

    task.wait(panic and 0 or 0.1)
    pcall(game.Destroy, SG)
    collectgarbage("collect")
    collectgarbage("collect")
    Console.log("success", "Cleanup done")
end

-- Keybind handler
TC(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    if inp.KeyCode == S.TK then
        -- ToggleUI is defined below, called via library reference
        if _G.LvyuFlo_Lib and _G.LvyuFlo_Lib.ToggleUI then
            _G.LvyuFlo_Lib.ToggleUI()
        end
    end
    if inp.KeyCode == S.PK then
        DestroyAll(true)
    end
end))

    local b = Mk("TextButton", {
        Text                = txt,
        Font                = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
        TextSize            = 14,
        TextColor3          = T.Sub,

        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        AutoButtonColor     = false,
        Size                = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28),
        Position            = UDim2.new(1, xo, 0, 12),
        ZIndex              = 4,
        Parent              = TB,
    })
    RC(b, UDim.new(0, 6))
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(b, { BackgroundTransparency = 0,   TextColor3 = hc    }, 0.12) end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(b, { BackgroundTransparency = 1,   TextColor3 = T.Sub }, 0.12) end)
    return b
end

local hideBtn = MkTBBtn("□", -40, T.Acc)
local miniBtn = MkTBBtn("—", -72, T.Warn)

-- FPS counter
local fpsLbl = Mk("TextLabel", {
    Text = "--",
    Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Dim,
    TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
    BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, BackgroundTransparency = 0.7,
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 28), Position = UDim2.new(1, -148, 0, 12),
    ZIndex = 4, Parent = TB,
})
RC(fpsLbl, UDim.new(0, 6))

task.spawn(function()
    local lt, fc = tick(), 0
    while S.Alive and not S.Destroying do
        fc = fc + 1
        if tick() - lt >= 1 then
            local fps = math.floor(fc / (tick() - lt))
            if fpsLbl and fpsLbl.Parent then
                fpsLbl.Text       = tostring(fps)
                fpsLbl.TextColor3 = fps > 50 and T.Good or fps > 30 and T.Warn or T.Bad
            end
            fc, lt = 0, tick()
        end
        RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
end)

-- Drag
local dTgt = MF.Position
local dOn, dSt, dSp = false, nil, nil

TB.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
    if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        dOn, dSt, dSp = true, inp.Position, dTgt
        inp.Changed:Connect(function()
            if inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                dOn = false
            end
        end)
    end
end)

TC(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
    if dOn and dSt and dSp then
        local t = inp.UserInputType
        if t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or t == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local d = inp.Position - dSt
            dTgt = UDim2.new(dSp.X.Scale, dSp.X.Offset + d.X, dSp.Y.Scale, dSp.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end
    end
end))

TC(RS.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not S.Alive or S.Destroying then return end
    local cx = MF.Position.X.Offset
    local cy = MF.Position.Y.Offset
    MF.Position = UDim2.new(
        dTgt.X.Scale, cx + (dTgt.X.Offset - cx) * 0.12,
        dTgt.Y.Scale, cy + (dTgt.Y.Offset - cy) * 0.12
    )
end))

-- Minimize
miniBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    Ripple(miniBtn)
    S.Mini = not S.Mini
    if S.Mini then
        Tw(MF, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(WS.X.Offset, UI.TH) }, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        miniBtn.Text = "+"
    else
        MF.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
        task.spawn(function() SprOpen(MF, WS, 0.45) end)
        miniBtn.Text = "—"
    end
end)

-- Hide
hideBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    Ripple(hideBtn)
    S.Visible = false
    task.spawn(function() SprClose(MF, 0.35) end)
end)

-- Toggle UI
local function ToggleUI()
    S.Visible = not S.Visible
    task.spawn(function()
        if S.Visible then
            SG.Enabled = true
            MF.Visible = true
            MF.Size    = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
            SprOpen(MF, S.Mini and UDim2.fromOffset(WS.X.Offset, UI.TH) or WS, 0.45)
        else
            SprClose(MF, 0.35)
        end
    end)
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 17: SIDEBAR & CONTENT AREA
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local Side = Mk("Frame", {
    BackgroundColor3 = T.Side,
    Size             = UDim2.new(0, UI.SW, 1, -UI.TH),
    Position         = UDim2.fromOffset(0, UI.TH),
    BorderSizePixel  = 0,
    ClipsDescendants = true,
    ZIndex           = 2,
    Parent           = MF,
})
RC(Side, UDim.new(0, 12))

-- Square off inner corners
for _, pos in ipairs({
    UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
    UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 0),
    UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, -14),
}) do
    Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Side, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), Position = pos, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2, Parent = Side })
end

-- Search bar
local srW = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 28), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), ZIndex = 3, Parent = Side })
RC(srW, UDim.new(0, 6))
local srB = Mk("TextBox", {
    PlaceholderText  = "🔍 Search...",
    PlaceholderColor3 = T.Dim,
    Text             = "",
    Font             = Enum.Font.Gotham,
    TextSize         = 12,
    TextColor3       = T.Txt,
    TextXAlignment   = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
    ClearTextOnFocus = false,
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Size             = UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, 0),
    Position         = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0),
    ZIndex           = 4,
    Parent           = srW,
})

-- Tab scroll
local tScr = Mk("ScrollingFrame", {
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Size                   = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -82),
    Position               = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 42),
    ScrollBarThickness     = 2,
    ScrollBarImageColor3   = T.Acc,
    ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.4,
    CanvasSize             = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
    AutomaticCanvasSize    = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
    BorderSizePixel        = 0,
    ZIndex                 = 3,
    Parent                 = Side,
})
LL(tScr, 4)
PD(tScr, 4, 4, 8, 8)

-- Divider line
Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Div, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, -UI.TH), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(UI.SW, UI.TH), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2, Parent = MF })

-- Content area
local CA = Mk("Frame", {
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Size                   = UDim2.new(1, -UI.SW - 1, 1, -UI.TH),
    Position               = UDim2.fromOffset(UI.SW + 1, UI.TH),
    ClipsDescendants       = true,
    ZIndex                 = 2,
    Parent                 = MF,
})

-- Global search
srB:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
    local q = srB.Text:lower()
    for _, tab in ipairs(S.Tabs) do
        if tab._cf then
            for _, ch in ipairs(tab._cf:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("Frame") then
                    local tl = ch:FindFirstChild("Title")
                    ch.Visible = not tl or q == "" or (tl.Text:lower():find(q, 1, true) ~= nil)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 18: TAB SYSTEM & COMPONENT LIBRARY
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local Lib = {}

function Lib:CreateTab(name, icon)
    local Tab = { _ord = 0 }

    local tBtn = Mk("TextButton", {
        Text                   = "",
        BackgroundColor3       = T.Acc,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        AutoButtonColor        = false,
        Size                   = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34),
        LayoutOrder            = #S.Tabs + 1,
        ZIndex                 = 4,
        Parent                 = tScr,
    })
    RC(tBtn, UDim.new(0, 6))

    local ind = Mk("Frame", {
        Name            = "Ind",
        BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc,
        Size            = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 0),
        Position        = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
        AnchorPoint     = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex          = 5,
        Parent          = tBtn,
    })
    RC(ind, UDim.new(0, 2))

    local iL = Mk("TextLabel", { Text = icon or "📁", Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,         TextSize = 15, TextColor3 = T.Dim, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 34), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), ZIndex = 5, Parent = tBtn })
    local nL = Mk("TextLabel", { Text = name,        Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 0), ZIndex = 5, Parent = tBtn })

    local cf = Mk("ScrollingFrame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size                   = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        ScrollBarThickness     = 3,
        ScrollBarImageColor3   = T.Acc,
        ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.3,
        CanvasSize             = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        AutomaticCanvasSize    = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BorderSizePixel        = 0,
        Visible                = false,
        ZIndex                 = 3,
        Parent                 = CA,
    })
    LL(cf, 6)
    PD(cf, 10, 10, 10, 10)

    Tab._cf  = cf
    Tab._btn = tBtn

    local function Activate()
        for _, ot in ipairs(S.Tabs) do
            if ot ~= Tab then
                ot._cf.Visible = false
                Tw(ot._btn, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.2)
                local oi = ot._btn:FindFirstChild("Ind")
                if oi then Tw(oi, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 0) }, 0.2) end
                for _, ch in ipairs(ot._btn:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then
                        Tw(ch, { TextColor3 = ch.TextSize >= 15 and T.Dim or T.Sub }, 0.2)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        cf.Visible          = true
        cf.CanvasPosition   = Vector2.zero
        Tw(tBtn, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.88 }, 0.2)
        Tw(ind,  { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 18) }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
        Tw(iL,   { TextColor3 = T.Acc }, 0.2)
        Tw(nL,   { TextColor3 = T.Txt }, 0.2)
        S.AT = Tab
    end

    tBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Activate(); Ripple(tBtn) end)
    tBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if S.AT ~= Tab then Tw(tBtn, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.92 }, 0.12) end end)
    tBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if S.AT ~= Tab then Tw(tBtn, { BackgroundTransparency = 1   }, 0.12) end end)

    if #S.Tabs == 0 then task.defer(Activate) end
    table.insert(S.Tabs, Tab)

    -- Helper: resolve parent frame (section or content)
    local function GP(c)
        return (c and c.Section and c.Section._fr) or cf
    end

    -- Helper: next layout order
    local function NO()
        Tab._ord = Tab._ord + 1
        return Tab._ord
    end

    -- ──────────── SECTION ────────────
    function Tab:CreateSection(c)
        c = c or {}
        local sec = {}
        local sf = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = cf })
        Mk("TextLabel",  { Text = "◆ " .. (c.Name or "SECTION"):upper(), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.Acc, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0), Parent = sf })
        Mk("Frame",      { BackgroundColor3 = T.Div, BackgroundTransparency = 0.5, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 19), BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = sf })
        local sc = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Parent = sf })
        LL(sc, 6)
        sec._fr = sc
        return sec
    end

    -- ──────────── DIVIDER ────────────
    function Tab:CreateDivider()
        Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Div, BackgroundTransparency = 0.5, Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 1), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = cf })
    end

    -- ──────────── LABEL ────────────
    function Tab:CreateLabel(c)
        c = c or {}
        local lf = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP(c) })
        local lb = Mk("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Title", Text = c.Text or "", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = c.Color or T.Sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0), Parent = lf,
        })
        return {
            Frame = lf,
            Set   = function(_, t, cl) lb.Text = t; if cl then lb.TextColor3 = cl end end,
        }
    end

    -- ──────────── COLOR INFO ────────────
    function Tab:CreateColorInfo(text, color)
        local fr = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP({}) })
        RC(fr)
        ST(fr, T.Div, 1, 0.6)
        Mk("Frame",     { BackgroundColor3 = color or T.Acc, Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -8), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4), Parent = fr })
        local lbl = Mk("TextLabel", { Text = text, Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 0), Parent = fr })
        return { Frame = fr, Set = function(_, t) lbl.Text = t end }
    end

    -- ──────────── BUTTON ────────────
    function Tab:CreateButton(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cb  = c.Callback    or function() end
        local desc = c.Description
        local h    = desc and UI.EHD or 38

        local fr = Mk("Frame", { Name = c.Name or "Btn", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h), LayoutOrder = NO(), ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = GP(c) })
        RC(fr)
        ST(fr, T.Div, 1, 0.6)

        Mk("TextLabel", { Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "Button", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, desc and 6 or 10), Parent = fr })
        if desc then
            Mk("TextLabel", { Text = desc, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 26), Parent = fr })
        end
        Mk("TextLabel", { Text = "→", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 18, TextColor3 = T.Acc, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, h), Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0), Parent = fr })

        local btn = Mk("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 2, Parent = fr })
        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH }, 0.12) end)
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem  }, 0.12) end)
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Ripple(fr); task.spawn(cb) end)

        return {
            Frame = fr,
            Set   = function(_, n) local t = fr:FindFirstChild("Title"); if t then t.Text = n end end,
        }
    end

    -- ──────────── TOGGLE ────────────
    function Tab:CreateToggle(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cb   = c.Callback    or function() end
        local flag = c.Flag        or c.Name or "Tog"
        local on   = c.Default     or false
        local desc = c.Description
        local tip  = c.Tooltip
        local unst = c.Unstable
        local h    = desc and UI.EHD or UI.EH

        local fr = Mk("Frame", { Name = c.Name or "Tog", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP(c) })
        RC(fr)
        ST(fr, T.Div, 1, 0.6)

        local titleTxt = c.Name or "Toggle"
        if unst then titleTxt = "⚠️ " .. titleTxt end

        Mk("TextLabel", { Name = "Title", Text = titleTxt, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = unst and T.Unst or T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, desc and 6 or 11), Parent = fr })

        if desc then
            Mk("TextLabel", { Text = desc, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 26), Parent = fr })
        end

        -- Tooltip "?" button
        if tip then
            local tipBtn = Mk("TextButton", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), Position = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0.5, -9), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "?", TextColor3 = T.Dim, TextSize = 10, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 5, Parent = fr })
            RC(tipBtn, UDim.new(1, 0))
            local hv = false
            tipBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                hv = true
                Tw(tipBtn, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc, TextColor3 = T.W }, 0.15)
                task.spawn(function()
                    local st2 = tick()
                    while hv and S.Alive do
                        if tick() - st2 >= UI.TIP_DELAY then
                            ShowTip(tip, tipBtn.AbsolutePosition.X, tipBtn.AbsolutePosition.Y)
                            break
                        end
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                end)
            end)
            tipBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                hv = false; HideTip()
                Tw(tipBtn, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, TextColor3 = T.Dim }, 0.15)
            end)
        end

        -- Switch track
        local sw = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), ZIndex = 2, Parent = fr })
        RC(sw, UDim.new(1, 0))
        local kn = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Sub, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), ZIndex = 3, Parent = sw })
        RC(kn, UDim.new(1, 0))

        local function Update(v, skip)
            on = v
            if on then
                Tw(kn, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -19, 0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = T.W   }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                Tw(sw, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc  }, 0.15)
            else
                Tw(kn, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0),   BackgroundColor3 = T.Sub }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                Tw(sw, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner }, 0.15)
            end
            if not skip then
                task.spawn(function() pcall(cb, on) end)
            end
        end

        local clk = Mk("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 4, Parent = fr })
        clk.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Update(not on) end)
        clk.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function() Update(c.Default or false) end)  -- right-click reset
        fr.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH }, 0.12) end)
        fr.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem  }, 0.12) end)

        if c.Default then Update(true, true) end

        local obj = {
            Frame   = fr,
            Type    = "Toggle",
            Flag    = flag,
            Default = c.Default or false,
            Set     = function(_, v, skip) Update(v, skip) end,
            Get     = function() return on end,
        }
        S.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    -- ──────────── SLIDER ────────────
    function Tab:CreateSlider(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cb   = c.Callback  or function() end
        local flag = c.Flag      or c.Name or "Sld"
        local mn   = c.Min       or 0
        local mx   = c.Max       or 100
        local inc  = c.Increment or 1
        local suf  = c.Suffix    or ""
        local desc = c.Description
        local tip  = c.Tooltip
        local h    = desc and 64 or 54
        local val  = math.clamp(c.Default or mn, mn, mx)

        local fr = Mk("Frame", { Name = c.Name or "Sld", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP(c) })
        RC(fr)
        ST(fr, T.Div, 1, 0.6)

        Mk("TextLabel", { Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "Slider", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, desc and 6 or 8), Parent = fr })
        if desc then
            Mk("TextLabel", { Text = desc, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Dim, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 24), Parent = fr })
        end

        -- Tooltip
        if tip then
            local tipBtn2 = Mk("TextButton", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0.55, -22, 0, desc and 7 or 9), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = "?", TextColor3 = T.Dim, TextSize = 9, AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 5, Parent = fr })
            RC(tipBtn2, UDim.new(1, 0))
            local hv2 = false
            tipBtn2.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                hv2 = true
                Tw(tipBtn2, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc, TextColor3 = T.W }, 0.15)
                task.spawn(function()
                    local st3 = tick()
                    while hv2 and S.Alive do
                        if tick() - st3 >= UI.TIP_DELAY then
                            ShowTip(tip, tipBtn2.AbsolutePosition.X, tipBtn2.AbsolutePosition.Y)
                            break
                        end
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                end)
            end)
            tipBtn2.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                hv2 = false; HideTip()
                Tw(tipBtn2, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, TextColor3 = T.Dim }, 0.15)
            end)
        end

        local vL = Mk("TextLabel", {
            Text = FV(val, inc) .. suf, Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = T.Acc,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(0.4, -14, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, desc and 6 or 8),
            Parent = fr,
        })

        local ty  = desc and 44 or 34
        local trk = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 8), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, ty), ZIndex = 2, Parent = fr })
        RC(trk, UDim.new(1, 0))

        local pct  = (val - mn) / (mx - mn)
        local fill = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc, Size = UDim2.new(pct, 0, 1, 0), ZIndex = 3, Parent = trk })
        RC(fill, UDim.new(1, 0))
        Mk("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(T.AccD, T.Acc), Parent = fill })

        local knb = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.W, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), Position = UDim2.new(pct, -8, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), ZIndex = 4, Parent = trk })
        RC(knb, UDim.new(1, 0))

        local sData = {}
        sData.Update = function(inp)
            if not trk or not trk.Parent then return end
            local tPos = trk.AbsolutePosition
            local tSz  = trk.AbsoluteSize
            if tSz.X <= 0 then return end
            local rx = math.clamp((inp.Position.X - tPos.X) / tSz.X, 0, 1)
            val = math.floor((mn + (mx - mn) * rx) / inc + 0.5) * inc
            val = math.clamp(val, mn, mx)
            local p2 = (val - mn) / (mx - mn)
            fill.Size       = UDim2.new(p2, 0, 1, 0)
            knb.Position    = UDim2.new(p2, -8, 0.5, 0)
            vL.Text         = FV(val, inc) .. suf
            task.spawn(function() pcall(cb, val) end)
        end

        local ca = Mk("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 16, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-8, -8), ZIndex = 5, Parent = trk })
        ca.InputBegan:Connect(function(i2)
            if i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                S.ASlider = sData
                sData.Update(i2)
            end
        end)
        -- Right-click = reset to default
        ca.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
            val = math.clamp(c.Default or mn, mn, mx)
            local p2 = (val - mn) / (mx - mn)
            fill.Size    = UDim2.new(p2, 0, 1, 0)
            knb.Position = UDim2.new(p2, -8, 0.5, 0)
            vL.Text      = FV(val, inc) .. suf
            task.spawn(function() pcall(cb, val) end)
        end)

        fr.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH }, 0.12) end)
        fr.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem  }, 0.12) end)

        local obj = {
            Frame   = fr,
            Type    = "Slider",
            Flag    = flag,
            Default = c.Default or mn,
            Set     = function(_, v, skip)
                val = math.clamp(v, mn, mx)
                local p2 = (val - mn) / (mx - mn)
                fill.Size    = UDim2.new(p2, 0, 1, 0)
                knb.Position = UDim2.new(p2, -8, 0.5, 0)
                vL.Text      = FV(val, inc) .. suf
                if not skip then task.spawn(function() pcall(cb, val) end) end
            end,
            Get = function() return val end,
        }
        S.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    -- ──────────── DROPDOWN ────────────
    function Tab:CreateDropdown(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cb   = c.Callback or function() end
        local flag = c.Flag     or c.Name or "DD"
        local opts = c.Options  or {}
        local sel  = c.Default  or (opts[1] or "")
        local isOp = false

        local fr = Mk("Frame", { Name = c.Name or "DD", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI.EH), LayoutOrder = NO(), ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 3, Parent = GP(c) })
        RC(fr)
        ST(fr, T.Div, 1, 0.6)

        Mk("TextLabel", { Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "Dropdown", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 0, UI.EH), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0), ZIndex = 4, Parent = fr })

        local sL = Mk("TextLabel", { Text = tostring(sel), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0.45, -40, 0, UI.EH), Position = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 0, 0), ZIndex = 4, Parent = fr })
        local ar = Mk("TextLabel", { Text = "▼", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.Sub, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, UI.EH), Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 0), ZIndex = 4, Parent = fr })

        local ow = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 46), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex = 5, Parent = fr })
        local oL = LL(ow, 3)

        local function MkOption(n)
            local isSel = sel == n
            local ob = Mk("TextButton", { Name = n, Text = "", BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, AutoButtonColor = false, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), ZIndex = 6, Parent = ow })
            RC(ob, UDim.new(0, 6))

            local ck = Mk("Frame", { Name = "Ck", BackgroundColor3 = isSel and T.Acc or T.Inner, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), ZIndex = 7, Parent = ob })
            RC(ck, UDim.new(1, 0))
            ST(ck, T.Div, 1, 0.5)

            local cm = Mk("TextLabel", { Text = "✓", Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.W, TextTransparency = isSel and 0 or 1, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 8, Parent = ck })
            Mk("TextLabel", { Text = n, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 0), ZIndex = 7, Parent = ob })

            ob.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(ob, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Div   }, 0.1) end)
            ob.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(ob, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner }, 0.1) end)
            ob.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                sel   = n
                sL.Text = n
                for _, ch in ipairs(ow:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local k2 = ch:FindFirstChild("Ck")
                        if k2 then
                            local m2 = k2:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                            k2.BackgroundColor3 = ch.Name == n and T.Acc or T.Inner
                            if m2 then m2.TextTransparency = ch.Name == n and 0 or 1 end
                        end
                    end
                end
                task.spawn(function() pcall(cb, sel) end)
            end)
        end

        for _, o in ipairs(opts) do MkOption(o) end

        local tgl = Mk("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI.EH), ZIndex = 5, Parent = fr })
        tgl.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            isOp = not isOp
            if isOp then
                fr.ClipsDescendants = false
                Tw(fr, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48 + oL.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10) }, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
                Tw(ar, { Rotation = 180 }, 0.2)
            else
                Tw(fr, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI.EH) }, 0.22)
                Tw(ar, { Rotation = 0   }, 0.2)
                task.delay(0.25, function() if fr then fr.ClipsDescendants = true end end)
            end
        end)
        -- Right-click = reset
        tgl.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
            local dv = c.Default or (opts[1] or "")
            sel = dv; sL.Text = dv
            for _, ch in ipairs(ow:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("TextButton") then
                    local k2 = ch:FindFirstChild("Ck")
                    if k2 then
                        local m2 = k2:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                        k2.BackgroundColor3 = ch.Name == dv and T.Acc or T.Inner
                        if m2 then m2.TextTransparency = ch.Name == dv and 0 or 1 end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        tgl.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH }, 0.12) end)
        tgl.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem  }, 0.12) end)

        local obj = {
            Frame   = fr,
            Type    = "Dropdown",
            Flag    = flag,
            Default = c.Default or (opts[1] or ""),
            Set = function(_, v, skip)
                sel = v; sL.Text = v
                for _, ch in ipairs(ow:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("TextButton") then
                        local k2 = ch:FindFirstChild("Ck")
                        if k2 then
                            local m2 = k2:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                            k2.BackgroundColor3 = ch.Name == v and T.Acc or T.Inner
                            if m2 then m2.TextTransparency = ch.Name == v and 0 or 1 end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if not skip then task.spawn(function() pcall(cb, sel) end) end
            end,
            Get     = function() return sel end,
            Refresh = function(_, nO)
                for _, ch in ipairs(ow:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
                end
                opts = nO
                for _, o in ipairs(opts) do MkOption(o) end
            end,
        }
        S.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    -- ──────────── INPUT ────────────
    function Tab:CreateInput(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cb   = c.Callback or function() end
        local flag = c.Flag     or c.Name or "Inp"

        local fr = Mk("Frame", { Name = c.Name or "Inp", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI.EH), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP(c) })
        RC(fr)
        ST(fr, T.Div, 1, 0.6)
        Mk("TextLabel", { Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "Input", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0), Parent = fr })

        local bw  = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.new(0.52, -12, 0, 26), Position = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), ZIndex = 2, Parent = fr })
        RC(bw, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local bSt = ST(bw, T.Div, 1, 0.6)

        local box = Mk("TextBox", {
            PlaceholderText  = c.Placeholder or "Type...",
            PlaceholderColor3 = T.Dim,
            Text             = c.Default or "",
            Font             = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            TextSize         = 13,
            TextColor3       = T.Txt,
            TextXAlignment   = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size             = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0),
            Position         = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 0),
            ZIndex           = 3,
            Parent           = bw,
        })

        box.Focused:Connect(function()  Tw(bSt, { Color = T.Acc, Transparency = 0.2 }, 0.15) end)
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            Tw(bSt, { Color = T.Div, Transparency = 0.6 }, 0.15)
            task.spawn(function() pcall(cb, box.Text) end)
        end)
        fr.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH }, 0.12) end)
        fr.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem  }, 0.12) end)

        local obj = {
            Frame   = fr,
            Type    = "Input",
            Flag    = flag,
            Default = c.Default or "",
            Set     = function(_, v, skip) box.Text = tostring(v); if not skip then task.spawn(function() pcall(cb, v) end) end end,
            Get     = function() return box.Text end,
        }
        S.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    -- ──────────── KEYBIND ────────────
    function Tab:CreateKeybind(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cb   = c.Callback or function() end
        local flag = c.Flag     or c.Name or "Key"
        local key  = c.Default  or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
        local lst  = false
        local nc   = c.NoCallback  -- if true, don't fire cb on keypress (toggle-style)

        local fr = Mk("Frame", { Name = c.Name or "Key", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI.EH), LayoutOrder = NO(), Parent = GP(c) })
        RC(fr)
        ST(fr, T.Div, 1, 0.6)
        Mk("TextLabel", { Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "Keybind", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0), Parent = fr })

        local kB = Mk("TextButton", {
            Text             = key == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "None" or key.Name,
            Font             = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize         = 12,
            TextColor3       = T.Acc,
            BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner,
            AutoButtonColor  = false,
            Size             = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 24),
            Position         = UDim2.new(1, -82, 0.5, 0),
            AnchorPoint      = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            ZIndex           = 2,
            Parent           = fr,
        })
        RC(kB, UDim.new(0, 6))
        ST(kB, T.Div, 1, 0.5)

        kB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            lst = true
            kB.Text = "..."
            Tw(kB, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc, TextColor3 = T.W }, 0.12)
        end)
        kB.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
            key = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
            kB.Text = "None"
            Tw(kB, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, TextColor3 = T.Acc }, 0.12)
        end)

        TC(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, gpe)
            if lst then
                if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                    if inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                        key = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown; kB.Text = "None"
                    else
                        key = inp.KeyCode; kB.Text = key.Name
                    end
                    lst = false
                    Tw(kB, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, TextColor3 = T.Acc }, 0.12)
                    if c.OnChanged then task.spawn(function() pcall(c.OnChanged, key) end) end
                end
            elseif not nc and not gpe and inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                if key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and inp.KeyCode == key then
                    task.spawn(cb)
                end
            end
        end))

        fr.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH }, 0.12) end)
        fr.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem  }, 0.12) end)

        local obj = {
            Frame   = fr,
            Type    = "Keybind",
            Flag    = flag,
            Default = c.Default or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown,
            Set     = function(_, k) key = k; kB.Text = k == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and "None" or k.Name end,
            Get     = function() return key end,
        }
        S.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    -- ──────────── COLOR PICKER ────────────
    function Tab:CreateColorPicker(c)
        c = c or {}
        local cb    = c.Callback or function() end
        local flag  = c.Flag     or c.Name or "Col"
        local color = c.Default  or T.Acc
        if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then color = T.Acc end
        local isOp    = false
        local h3, s3, v3 = color:ToHSV()

        local fr = Mk("Frame", { Name = c.Name or "Col", BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI.EH), LayoutOrder = NO(), ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = GP(c) })
        RC(fr)
        ST(fr, T.Div, 1, 0.6)

        Mk("TextLabel", { Name = "Title", Text = c.Name or "Color", Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = T.Txt, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, UI.EH), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0), ZIndex = 4, Parent = fr })

        local prev = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = color, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 20), Position = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), ZIndex = 4, Parent = fr })
        RC(prev, UDim.new(0, 4))
        ST(prev, T.Div, 1, 0.3)

        local slFr = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 80), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 46), ZIndex = 5, Parent = fr })
        LL(slFr, 4)

        local function MkCS(label, initV, onChange)
            local slFrame = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), ZIndex = 6, Parent = slFr })
            Mk("TextLabel", { Text = label, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.Sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 22), ZIndex = 7, Parent = slFrame })
            local slT = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Inner, Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 8), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 7), ZIndex = 6, Parent = slFrame })
            RC(slT, UDim.new(1, 0))
            local slF = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.Acc,   Size = UDim2.new(initV, 0, 1, 0),       ZIndex = 7, Parent = slT })
            RC(slF, UDim.new(1, 0))
            local slK = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.W,     Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), Position = UDim2.new(initV, -6, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), ZIndex = 8, Parent = slT })
            RC(slK, UDim.new(1, 0))

            local slDat = {}
            slDat.Update = function(inp)
                local pos = slT.AbsolutePosition
                local sz  = slT.AbsoluteSize
                if sz.X <= 0 then return end
                local rx = math.clamp((inp.Position.X - pos.X) / sz.X, 0, 1)
                slF.Size       = UDim2.new(rx, 0, 1, 0)
                slK.Position   = UDim2.new(rx, -6, 0.5, 0)
                onChange(rx)
            end

            local slBtn = Mk("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 8, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-4, -5), ZIndex = 9, Parent = slT })
            slBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    S.ASlider = slDat
                    slDat.Update(inp)
                end
            end)

            return {
                Set = function(val2)
                    if not slF or not slF.Parent then return end
                    slF.Size     = UDim2.new(math.clamp(val2, 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
                    slK.Position = UDim2.new(math.clamp(val2, 0, 1), -6, 0.5, 0)
                end,
            }
        end

        local function UpdCol()
            color = Color3.fromHSV(h3 % 1, math.clamp(s3, 0, 1), math.clamp(v3, 0, 1))
            if prev and prev.Parent then prev.BackgroundColor3 = color end
            task.spawn(function() pcall(cb, color) end)
        end

        local hSl = MkCS("H", h3, function(v) h3 = v; UpdCol() end)
        local sSl = MkCS("S", s3, function(v) s3 = v; UpdCol() end)
        local vSl = MkCS("V", v3, function(v) v3 = v; UpdCol() end)

        local togBtn = Mk("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI.EH), ZIndex = 5, Parent = fr })
        togBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            isOp = not isOp
            if isOp then
                fr.ClipsDescendants = false
                Tw(fr, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI.EH + 90) }, 0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
            else
                Tw(fr, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, UI.EH) }, 0.22)
                task.delay(0.25, function() if fr then fr.ClipsDescendants = true end end)
            end
        end)
        fr.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.ElemH }, 0.12) end)
        fr.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tw(fr, { BackgroundColor3 = T.Elem  }, 0.12) end)

        local obj = {
            Frame   = fr,
            Type    = "ColorPicker",
            Flag    = flag,
            Default = c.Default or T.Acc,
            Set     = function(_, c3, skip)
                if not c3 or typeof(c3) ~= "Color3" then return end
                color = c3; h3, s3, v3 = c3:ToHSV()
                if prev and prev.Parent then prev.BackgroundColor3 = c3 end
                hSl.Set(h3); sSl.Set(s3); vSl.Set(v3)
                if not skip then task.spawn(function() pcall(cb, color) end) end
            end,
            Get = function() return color end,
        }
        S.Flags[flag] = obj
        return obj
    end

    return Tab
end -- ← end CreateTab

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 19: CONFIG SYSTEM
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local Cfg = {
    Profile  = "Default",
    AutoSave = true,
    Interval = 60,
}

local function XOR(data, key2)
    local r = {}
    for i = 1, #data do
        local ki = ((i - 1) % #key2) + 1
        r[i] = string.char(bit32.bxor(string.byte(data, i), string.byte(key2, ki)))
    end
    return table.concat(r)
end

function Cfg.ensureFolder()
    if not Ex.FS then return false end
    pcall(function() if not isfolder(CFG_FOLDER) then makefolder(CFG_FOLDER) end end)
    return true
end

function Cfg.path() return CFG_FOLDER .. "/" .. CFG_FILE end

function Cfg.loadAll()
    if not Ex.FS then return nil end
    Cfg.ensureFolder()
    if not isfile(Cfg.path()) then return nil end
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        local raw     = readfile(Cfg.path())
        local wrapper = Http:JSONDecode(raw)
        if wrapper and wrapper.d then
            return Http:JSONDecode(XOR(wrapper.d, ENC_KEY))
        end
        return nil
    end)
    if ok and result then return result end
    Console.log("error", "Config load failed")
    return nil
end

function Cfg.saveAll(data)
    if not Ex.FS then return false end
    Cfg.ensureFolder()
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local json    = Http:JSONEncode(data)
        local enc     = XOR(json, ENC_KEY)
        local wrapper = Http:JSONEncode({ v = 1, d = enc, t = tick() })
        writefile(Cfg.path(), wrapper)
    end)
    if not ok then Console.log("error", "Config save: " .. tostring(err)) end
    return ok
end

function Cfg.saveProfile(pname)
    pname = pname or Cfg.Profile
    local all = Cfg.loadAll() or { profiles = {}, meta = {} }
    local flags = {}
    for fn, el in pairs(S.Flags) do
        if el.Get then
            local v = el:Get()
            local tv = type(v)
            if tv == "boolean" or tv == "number" or tv == "string" then
                flags[fn] = v
            elseif typeof(v) == "Color3" then
                flags[fn] = { _t = "C3", R = v.R, G = v.G, B = v.B }
            elseif typeof(v) == "EnumItem" then
                flags[fn] = { _t = "EK", N = v.Name }
            end
        end
    end
    all.profiles         = all.profiles or {}
    all.profiles[pname]  = flags
    all.meta             = all.meta or {}
    all.meta.last        = pname
    all.meta.mod         = tick()
    return Cfg.saveAll(all)
end

function Cfg.loadProfile(pname)
    if S.Loading then return false end
    S.Loading = true
    local all = Cfg.loadAll()
    if not all or not all.profiles then S.Loading = false; return false end
    local prof = all.profiles[pname]
    if not prof then Console.log("warning", "Profile not found: " .. pname); S.Loading = false; return false end
    for fn, v in pairs(prof) do
        local el = S.Flags[fn]
        if el and el.Set then
            pcall(function()
                if type(v) == "table" then
                    if v._t == "C3" then
                        el:Set(Color3.new(v.R, v.G, v.B), true)
                    elseif v._t == "EK" then
                        el:Set(Enum.KeyCode[v.N], true)
                    end
                else
                    el:Set(v, true)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    Cfg.Profile = pname
    S.Loading   = false
    Console.log("success", "Loaded profile: " .. pname)
    return true
end

function Cfg.getProfiles()
    local all = Cfg.loadAll()
    if not all or not all.profiles then return { "Default" } end
    local p = {}
    for n in pairs(all.profiles) do table.insert(p, n) end
    if #p == 0 then p = { "Default" } end
    return p
end

function Cfg.deleteProfile(pname)
    local all = Cfg.loadAll()
    if not all or not all.profiles then return false end
    all.profiles[pname] = nil
    return Cfg.saveAll(all)
end

function Cfg.exportProfile(pname)
    if not Ex.Clip then return false end
    local all = Cfg.loadAll()
    if not all or not all.profiles then return false end
    local prof = all.profiles[pname or Cfg.Profile]
    if not prof then return false end
    pcall(setclipboard, Http:JSONEncode(prof))
    return true
end

function Cfg.startAutoSave()
    task.spawn(function()
        while S.Alive and not S.Destroying do
            task.wait(Cfg.Interval)
            if Cfg.AutoSave and not S.Destroying then
                pcall(function() Cfg.saveProfile() end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Register save callback for DestroyAll
_G.LvyuFlo_SaveConfig = function()
    if Cfg.AutoSave then
        pcall(function() Cfg.saveProfile() end)
    end
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- SECTION 20: LOADING SCREEN
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local function ShowLoading(onDone)
    local lg = Mk("ScreenGui", { Name = RndName(), ResetOnSpawn = false, DisplayOrder = 999999, IgnoreGuiInset = true })
    pcall(function() lg.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if not lg.Parent then pcall(function() lg.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end) end

    local bg = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 15), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Parent = lg })
    Mk("UIGradient", {
        Color    = ColorSequence.new({ ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 20)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 28)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 20)) }),
        Rotation = 45,
        Parent   = bg,
    })

    local ctr = Mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 120), Parent = bg })

    local logo = Mk("TextLabel", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 40), Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Text = SCRIPT_NAME, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220), TextSize = 32, Parent = ctr })

    local glow = logo:Clone()
    glow.TextTransparency = 0.7; glow.TextSize = 34; glow.ZIndex = -1; glow.Parent = ctr

    Mk("TextLabel", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.38), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 16), Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, Text = "v" .. VERSION .. " Premium", TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 130), TextSize = 12, Parent = ctr })

    local barBg = Mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 50), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 4), Parent = ctr })
    RC(barBg, UDim.new(1, 0))
    local barFill = Mk("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Parent = barBg })
    RC(barFill, UDim.new(1, 0))
    Mk("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new({ ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(100, 60, 180)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(180, 130, 250)) }), Parent = barFill })

    local dotC = Mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.8), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 10), Parent = ctr })
    local dots = {}
    for i = 1, 3 do
        local d = Mk("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 220), Position = UDim2.fromScale((i - 1) * 0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6), Parent = dotC })
        RC(d, UDim.new(1, 0))
        dots[i] = d
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local di = 1
        while lg and lg.Parent do
            for i, d in ipairs(dots) do
                if d and d.Parent then
                    Tw(d, {
                        BackgroundTransparency = i == di and 0 or 0.7,
                        Size = i == di and UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8) or UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6),
                    }, 0.15)
                end
            end
            di = (di % 3) + 1
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        local stages = { 0.2, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9, 1.0 }
        local durs   = { 0.3, 0.5,  0.4, 0.3, 0.2 }
        local total  = 0
        for i, tgt in ipairs(stages) do
            local d = durs[i]
            Tw(barFill, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(tgt, 1) }, d, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
            task.wait(d)
            total = total + d
        end
        if total < 1.5 then task.wait(1.5 - total) end

        -- Fade out
        for _, ch in pairs(ctr:GetDescendants()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then Tw(ch, { TextTransparency = 1 }, 0.3)
            elseif ch:IsA("Frame") then Tw(ch, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.3) end
        end
        Tw(bg, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.3)
        task.wait(0.35)
        pcall(game.Destroy, lg)
        if onDone then onDone() end
    end)
end
